local function resolveNexusUIFontEnum(weight)
    if weight == Enum.FontWeight.ExtraBold or weight == Enum.FontWeight.Heavy then
        return Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    end
    if weight == Enum.FontWeight.Bold then
        return Enum.Font.GothamBold
    end
    if weight == Enum.FontWeight.Medium then
        return Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    end
    return defaultNexusUIFontEnum
end

local NexusUIFontFaceSupported = false

local function createNexusNativeFontFromFamily(family, weight, style)
    if not nativeRobloxFontDatatype or type(nativeRobloxFontDatatype.new) ~= "function" then
        return nil
    end

    local okFont, nativeFont = pcall(nativeRobloxFontDatatype.new, family, weight, style)
    if okFont then
        return nativeFont
    end

    return nil
end

local function createNexusNativeFontFromEnum(enumFont)
    if not nativeRobloxFontDatatype or type(nativeRobloxFontDatatype.fromEnum) ~= "function" then
        return nil
    end

    local okFont, nativeFont = pcall(nativeRobloxFontDatatype.fromEnum, enumFont)
    if okFont then
        return nativeFont
    end

    return nil
end

local function makeNexusUIFontDescriptor(family, weight, style, enumFont, nativeFont)
    local resolvedWeight = weight or Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold
    local resolvedStyle = style or Enum.FontStyle.Normal
    local resolvedEnumFont = enumFont or resolveNexusUIFontEnum(resolvedWeight)

    return {
        __NexusUIFontDescriptor = true,
        Family = family or defaultNexusUIFontFamily,
        Weight = resolvedWeight,
        Style = resolvedStyle,
        EnumFont = resolvedEnumFont,
        NativeFont = nativeFont
    }
end
 
local function isNexusUIFontDescriptor(value)
    return type(value) == "table" and value.__NexusUIFontDescriptor == true
end

local function getFallbackNexusUIFont(value, fallbackEnum)
    if isNexusUIFontDescriptor(value) then
        return value.EnumFont or fallbackEnum or defaultNexusUIFontEnum
    end
    return fallbackEnum or defaultNexusUIFontEnum
end

local function applyNexusUIFont(instance, value, fallbackEnum)
    if not instance or not (instance:IsA("TextLabel") or instance:IsA("TextButton") or instance:IsA("TextBox")) then
        return false
    end

    local nativeFont = nil
    if isNexusUIFontDescriptor(value) then
        nativeFont = value.NativeFont
    elseif typeof(value) == "Font" then
        nativeFont = value
    end

    if nativeFont ~= nil then
        local okNative = pcall(function()
            instance.FontFace = nativeFont
        end)
        if okNative then
            NexusUIFontFaceSupported = true
            return true
        end
    end

    pcall(function()
        instance.Font = getFallbackNexusUIFont(value, fallbackEnum)
    end)

    return false
end

local Font = {
    new = function(family, weight, style)
        family = family or defaultNexusUIFontFamily
        weight = weight or Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold
        style = style or Enum.FontStyle.Normal
        local fallbackEnum = resolveNexusUIFontEnum(weight)
        local nativeFont = createNexusNativeFontFromFamily(family, weight, style) or createNexusNativeFontFromEnum(fallbackEnum)
        return makeNexusUIFontDescriptor(family, weight, style, fallbackEnum, nativeFont)
    end,
    fromEnum = function(enumFont)
        enumFont = enumFont or defaultNexusUIFontEnum
        local nativeFont = createNexusNativeFontFromEnum(enumFont)
        return makeNexusUIFontDescriptor(defaultNexusUIFontFamily, Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal, enumFont, nativeFont)
    end
}

local function makeThingExist(className, properties)
    local whatWeJustMade = Instance.new(className)
    for property, value in pairs(properties) do
        if property ~= "Parent" then
            if property == "FontFace" then
                applyNexusUIFont(whatWeJustMade, value)
            else
                pcall(function()
                    whatWeJustMade[property] = value
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    if properties.Parent then
        whatWeJustMade.Parent = properties.Parent
    end
    return whatWeJustMade
end

local function smoothMoveGoWoosh(instance, properties, duration, easingStyle, easingDirection)
    local tweenInfoYay = TweenInfo.new(duration or 0.22, easingStyle or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, easingDirection or Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    local tweenGoVroom = oMgThisTweenServiceIsSoCool:Create(instance, tweenInfoYay, properties)
    tweenGoVroom:Play()
    return tweenGoVroom
end
-- Creds to luke
local springSleepOffsetSqLimit = (1 / 3840) ^ 2
local springSleepVelocitySqLimit = 1e-4
local springEpsilon = 1e-5
local springPi = math.pi
local springStates = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

local function getSpringMagnitudeSq(vectorValues)
    local total = 0
    for _, value in ipairs(vectorValues) do
        total = total + value ^ 2
    end
    return total
end

local function getSpringDistanceSq(vector0, vector1)
    local total = 0
    for index, value in ipairs(vector0) do
        total = total + (vector1[index] - value) ^ 2
    end
    return total
end

local LinearSpring = {}
LinearSpring.__index = LinearSpring

function LinearSpring.new(dampingRatio, frequency, positionValue, typeMetadata, rawTarget)
    local intermediatePosition = typeMetadata.toIntermediate(positionValue)
    return setmetatable({
        d = dampingRatio,
        f = frequency,
        g = intermediatePosition,
        p = intermediatePosition,
        v = table.create(#intermediatePosition, 0),
        typedat = typeMetadata,
        rawTarget = rawTarget
    }, LinearSpring)
end

function LinearSpring:setGoal(goal)
    self.rawTarget = goal
    self.g = self.typedat.toIntermediate(goal)
end

function LinearSpring:canSleep()
    return getSpringMagnitudeSq(self.v) <= springSleepVelocitySqLimit
        and getSpringDistanceSq(self.p, self.g) <= springSleepOffsetSqLimit
end

function LinearSpring:step(dt)
    local dampingRatio = self.d
    local angularFrequency = self.f * 2 * springPi
    local goal = self.g
    local position = self.p
    local velocity = self.v

    if dampingRatio == 1 then
        local q = math.exp(-angularFrequency * dt)
        local w = dt * q
        local c0 = q + w * angularFrequency
        local c2 = q - w * angularFrequency
        local c3 = w * angularFrequency * angularFrequency
        for index = 1, #position do
            local offset = position[index] - goal[index]
            position[index] = offset * c0 + velocity[index] * w + goal[index]
            velocity[index] = velocity[index] * c2 - offset * c3
        end
    elseif dampingRatio < 1 then
        local q = math.exp(-dampingRatio * angularFrequency * dt)
        local c = math.sqrt(1 - dampingRatio * dampingRatio)
        local i = math.cos(dt * angularFrequency * c)
        local j = math.sin(dt * angularFrequency * c)
        local z = c > springEpsilon and (j / c) or (dt * angularFrequency)
        local y = angularFrequency * c > springEpsilon and (j / (angularFrequency * c)) or dt
        for index = 1, #position do
            local offset = position[index] - goal[index]
            position[index] = (offset * (1 + z * dampingRatio) + velocity[index] * y) * q + goal[index]
            velocity[index] = (velocity[index] * (1 - z * dampingRatio) - offset * (z * angularFrequency)) * q
        end
    else
        local c = math.sqrt(dampingRatio * dampingRatio - 1)
        local r1 = -angularFrequency * (dampingRatio - c)
        local r2 = -angularFrequency * (dampingRatio + c)
        local ec1 = math.exp(r1 * dt)
        local ec2 = math.exp(r2 * dt)
        for index = 1, #position do
            local offset = position[index] - goal[index]
            local coefficient2 = (velocity[index] - offset * r1) / (2 * angularFrequency * c)
            local coefficient1 = ec1 * (offset - coefficient2)
            position[index] = coefficient1 + coefficient2 * ec2 + goal[index]
            velocity[index] = coefficient1 * r1 + coefficient2 * ec2 * r2
        end
    end

    return self.typedat.fromIntermediate(position)
end

local springTypeMetadata = {
    number = {
        springType = LinearSpring.new,
        toIntermediate = function(value)
            return {value}
        end,
        fromIntermediate = function(value)
            return value[1]
        end
    },
    UDim2 = {
        springType = LinearSpring.new,
        toIntermediate = function(value)
            return {value.X.Scale, value.X.Offset, value.Y.Scale, value.Y.Offset}
        end,
        fromIntermediate = function(value)
            return UDim2.new(value[1], value[2], value[3], value[4])
        end
    },
    Vector2 = {
        springType = LinearSpring.new,
        toIntermediate = function(value)
            return {value.X, value.Y}
        end,
        fromIntermediate = function(value)
            return Vector2.new(value[1], value[2])
        end
    },
    Vector3 = {
        springType = LinearSpring.new,
        toIntermediate = function(value)
            return {value.X, value.Y, value.Z}
        end,
        fromIntermediate = function(value)
            return Vector3.new(value[1], value[2], value[3])
        end
    },
    Color3 = {
        springType = LinearSpring.new,
        toIntermediate = function(value)
            return {value.R, value.G, value.B}
        end,
        fromIntermediate = function(value)
            return Color3.new(value[1], value[2], value[3])
        end
    }
}

local spr = {}

runServiceMoreLikeWalkService.Stepped:Connect(function(_, dt)
    for instance, state in pairs(springStates) do
        local stateValid = true
        for property, spring in pairs(state) do
            local okStep = pcall(function()
                if spring:canSleep() then
                    state[property] = nil
                    instance[property] = spring.rawTarget
                else
                    instance[property] = spring:step(dt)
                end
            end)
            if not okStep then
                state[property] = nil
                stateValid = false
            end
        end
        if not next(state) or not stateValid then
            springStates[instance] = nil
        end
    end
end)

function spr.target(instance, dampingRatio, frequency, properties)
    if not instance or type(properties) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    local state = springStates[instance] or {}
    springStates[instance] = state

    for property, target in pairs(properties) do
        local typeMeta = springTypeMetadata[typeof(target)]
        if typeMeta then
            local currentValue = instance[property]
            local spring = state[property] or typeMeta.springType(dampingRatio, frequency, currentValue, typeMeta, target)
            state[property] = spring
            spring.d = dampingRatio
            spring.f = frequency
            spring:setGoal(target)
        end
    end
end

function spr.springend(instance, property)
    while springStates[instance] and springStates[instance][property] do
        task.wait()
    end
end

spr.Totallyspringedoff = spr.springend

local function springMoveGoBoing(instance, properties, dampingRatio, frequency)
    spr.target(instance, dampingRatio or 0.88, frequency or 5.2, properties)
end

local function disconnectNexusConnection(conn)
    if conn and typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
        pcall(function()
            if conn.Connected then
                conn:Disconnect()
            end
        end)
    elseif type(conn) == "function" then
        pcall(conn)
    end
end

local function isNexusUIGuiObjectActuallyVisible(guiObject)
    if not guiObject or not guiObject:IsA("GuiObject") then
        return false
    end
    if guiObject.AbsoluteSize.X <= 0 or guiObject.AbsoluteSize.Y <= 0 then
        return false
    end

    local current = guiObject
    while current do
        if current:IsA("GuiObject") and current.Visible == false then
            return false
        end
        if current:IsA("ScreenGui") then
            break
        end
        current = current.Parent
    end

    return true
end

local function makeDraggableCuzWeCan(frame, handle, libraryRef)
    local amIDragging = false
    local activeDragInput
    local whereDidIStart
    local whereWasIBefore
    local targetDragPosition = frame.Position
    local dragLerpSpeed = isMobileUserSadly and 18 or 22
    local renderDragConn
    
    handle = handle or frame

    local function stopDragLoop()
        disconnectNexusConnection(renderDragConn)
        renderDragConn = nil
    end

    local function ensureDragLoop()
        if renderDragConn and renderDragConn.Connected then
            return
        end
        renderDragConn = runServiceMoreLikeWalkService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            if not frame or not frame.Parent then
                stopDragLoop()
                return
            end
            local currentPos = frame.Position
            local goalPos = targetDragPosition
            local offsetDelta = math.abs(goalPos.X.Offset - currentPos.X.Offset) + math.abs(goalPos.Y.Offset - currentPos.Y.Offset)
            if offsetDelta <= 0.1 then
                if currentPos ~= goalPos then
                    frame.Position = goalPos
                end
                if not amIDragging then
                    stopDragLoop()
                end
                return
            end
            local alpha = math.clamp(1 - math.exp(-dragLerpSpeed * dt), 0, 0.45)
            frame.Position = currentPos:Lerp(goalPos, alpha)
        end)
        if libraryRef and type(libraryRef._TrackConnection) == "function" then
            libraryRef:_TrackConnection(renderDragConn)
        end
    end
    
    local function updateTargetPositionYay(input)
        local howMuchDidIMove = input.Position - whereDidIStart
        targetDragPosition = UDim2.new(
            whereWasIBefore.X.Scale,
            whereWasIBefore.X.Offset + howMuchDidIMove.X,
            whereWasIBefore.Y.Scale,
            whereWasIBefore.Y.Offset + howMuchDidIMove.Y
        )
    end
    
    local inputBeganConn = handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        local isMouse = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        local isTouch = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        if not isMouse and not isTouch then
            return
        end

        amIDragging = true
        activeDragInput = isTouch and input or nil
        whereDidIStart = input.Position
        whereWasIBefore = frame.Position
        targetDragPosition = frame.Position
        ensureDragLoop()
        
        local inputEndConn
        inputEndConn = input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                if frame and frame.Parent then
                    frame.Position = targetDragPosition
                end
                amIDragging = false
                activeDragInput = nil
                stopDragLoop()
                if inputEndConn then
                    inputEndConn:Disconnect()
                    inputEndConn = nil
                end
            end
        end)
        if libraryRef and type(libraryRef._TrackConnection) == "function" then
            libraryRef:_TrackConnection(inputEndConn)
        end
    end)
    
    local userInputChangedConn = thisInputServiceGoesHard.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not amIDragging then
            return
        end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            updateTargetPositionYay(input)
        elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and (activeDragInput == nil or input == activeDragInput) then
            updateTargetPositionYay(input)
        end
    end)

    if libraryRef and type(libraryRef._TrackConnection) == "function" then
        libraryRef:_TrackConnection(inputBeganConn)
        libraryRef:_TrackConnection(userInputChangedConn)
    end
end

local function startFloatingPositionTracker(libraryRef, anchorInstance, updateFn)
    if type(updateFn) ~= "function" or not anchorInstance then
        return nil
    end

    local active = true
    local connections = {}

    local function bindSignal(instance, propertyName)
        if not instance then
            return
        end
        table.insert(connections, libraryRef:_TrackConnection(instance:GetPropertyChangedSignal(propertyName):Connect(updateFn)))
    end

    bindSignal(anchorInstance, "AbsolutePosition")
    bindSignal(anchorInstance, "AbsoluteSize")
    table.insert(connections, libraryRef:_TrackConnection(anchorInstance.AncestryChanged:Connect(updateFn)))

    if libraryRef and libraryRef.screenGuiYay then
        bindSignal(libraryRef.screenGuiYay, "AbsoluteSize")
    end

    updateFn()

    return function()
        if not active then
            return
        end
        active = false
        for i = #connections, 1, -1 do
            disconnectNexusConnection(connections[i])
            connections[i] = nil
        end
    end
end

local function attachNexusUIScrollbar(libraryRef, scrollFrame, parentInstance, options)
    if not libraryRef or not scrollFrame or not parentInstance then
        return nil
    end

    options = options or {}

    local trackWidth = math.max(4, math.floor((options.TrackWidth or (6 * mobileScaleFactor)) + 0.5))
    local thumbWidth = math.max(2, math.min(trackWidth - 1, math.floor((options.ThumbWidth or (3 * mobileScaleFactor)) + 0.5)))
    local edgeInset = math.max(1, math.floor((options.EdgeInset or (2 * mobileScaleFactor)) + 0.5))
    local verticalInset = math.max(2, math.floor((options.VerticalInset or (4 * mobileScaleFactor)) + 0.5))
    local minThumbHeight = math.max(18, math.floor((options.MinThumbHeight or (26 * mobileScaleFactor)) + 0.5))
    local idleThumbHeight = math.max(minThumbHeight, math.floor((options.IdleThumbHeight or (42 * mobileScaleFactor)) + 0.5))
    local alwaysShowTrack = options.AlwaysShowTrack == true
    local zIndex = options.ZIndex or ((scrollFrame.ZIndex or 1) + 2)

    local trackFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        Name = "NexusUIScrollbarTrack",
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(11, 11, 14),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.12,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = zIndex,
        Parent = parentInstance
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = trackFrame})

    local thumbFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        Name = "NexusUIScrollbarThumb",
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = libraryRef.config.AccentColor,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, thumbWidth, 0, minThumbHeight),
        ZIndex = zIndex + 1,
        Parent = trackFrame
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = thumbFrame})

    local function resolveCanvasHeight()
        local canvasHeight = math.max(scrollFrame.CanvasSize.Y.Offset, 0)
        local okAbsoluteCanvas, absoluteCanvasSize = pcall(function()
            return scrollFrame.AbsoluteCanvasSize
        end)
        if okAbsoluteCanvas and typeof(absoluteCanvasSize) == "Vector2" then
            canvasHeight = math.max(canvasHeight, absoluteCanvasSize.Y)
        end
        return canvasHeight
    end

    local function updateScrollbar()
        if libraryRef._destroyed or not scrollFrame.Parent or not parentInstance.Parent or not trackFrame.Parent then
            return false
        end

        local frameSize = scrollFrame.AbsoluteSize
        local windowHeight = frameSize.Y
        local frameVisible = scrollFrame.Visible and frameSize.X > 0 and frameSize.Y > 0
        local canvasHeight = math.max(resolveCanvasHeight(), windowHeight)
        local canScroll = frameVisible and canvasHeight > (windowHeight + 1)

        if not frameVisible then
            trackFrame.Visible = false
            thumbFrame.Visible = false
            return true
        end

        local parentAbsolute = parentInstance.AbsolutePosition
        local frameAbsolute = scrollFrame.AbsolutePosition
        local trackHeight = math.max(0, frameSize.Y - (verticalInset * 2))
        if trackHeight <= 2 then
            trackFrame.Visible = false
            return true
        end
        local trackX = math.floor((frameAbsolute.X - parentAbsolute.X) + frameSize.X - trackWidth - edgeInset + 0.5)
        local trackY = math.floor((frameAbsolute.Y - parentAbsolute.Y) + verticalInset + 0.5)

        trackFrame.Visible = true
        trackFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(trackX, trackY)
        trackFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(trackWidth, math.floor(trackHeight + 0.5))
        trackFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(11, 11, 14)
        thumbFrame.BackgroundColor3 = libraryRef.config.AccentColor

        if not canScroll then
            trackFrame.Visible = alwaysShowTrack
            thumbFrame.Visible = alwaysShowTrack
            if alwaysShowTrack then
                local restingThumbHeight = math.min(idleThumbHeight, trackHeight)
                thumbFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(thumbWidth, math.floor(restingThumbHeight + 0.5))
                thumbFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(trackWidth * 0.5 + 0.5), math.floor(math.max(0, (trackHeight - restingThumbHeight) * 0.08) + 0.5))
            end
            return true
        end

        thumbFrame.Visible = true
        local minimumThumbHeight = math.min(minThumbHeight, trackHeight)
        local thumbHeight = math.clamp((windowHeight / canvasHeight) * trackHeight, minimumThumbHeight, trackHeight)
        local maxScroll = math.max(canvasHeight - windowHeight, 0)
        local scrollRatio = maxScroll > 0 and math.clamp(scrollFrame.CanvasPosition.Y / maxScroll, 0, 1) or 0
        local thumbTravel = math.max(trackHeight - thumbHeight, 0)

        thumbFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(thumbWidth, math.floor(thumbHeight + 0.5))
        thumbFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(trackWidth * 0.5 + 0.5), math.floor((thumbTravel * scrollRatio) + 0.5))

        return true
    end

    local function bindProperty(propertyName)
        local okSignal, signal = pcall(function()
            return scrollFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal(propertyName)
        end)
        if okSignal and signal then
            libraryRef:_TrackConnection(signal:Connect(updateScrollbar))
        end
    end

    bindProperty("CanvasPosition")
    bindProperty("CanvasSize")
    bindProperty("AbsoluteSize")
    bindProperty("AbsoluteCanvasSize")
    bindProperty("Visible")

    local stopFloatingTracker = startFloatingPositionTracker(libraryRef, scrollFrame, updateScrollbar)
    if stopFloatingTracker then
        libraryRef:_TrackConnection(stopFloatingTracker)
    end

    if type(libraryRef._scrollbarRefreshers) == "table" then
        table.insert(libraryRef._scrollbarRefreshers, updateScrollbar)
    end

    updateScrollbar()

    return {
        Track = trackFrame,
        Thumb = thumbFrame,
        Refresh = updateScrollbar
    }
end

local function getPlayerAvatarCuzTheyreBeautiful(userId)
    local didItWork, whatWeGot = pcall(function()
        return playersAreNoobs:GetUserThumbnailAsync(userId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size420x420)
    end)
    if didItWork then
        return whatWeGot
    end
    return "rbxassetid://135756197673563"
end

local function resolveAvatar3DUserIdFromRobloxEndpoint(defaultUserId)
    local fallbackUserId = tonumber(defaultUserId) or 1
    local endpoint = "https://thumbnails.roblox.com/v1/users/avatar-3d?userId=" .. tostring(fallbackUserId)

    local okBody, rawBody = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet(endpoint)
    end)
    if not okBody or type(rawBody) ~= "string" or rawBody == "" then
        return fallbackUserId, nil
    end

    local okDecode, payload = pcall(function()
        return httpServiceNoCap:JSONDecode(rawBody)
    end)
    if not okDecode or type(payload) ~= "table" then
        return fallbackUserId, nil
    end

    local resolvedUserId = tonumber(payload.targetId) or tonumber(payload.userId) or fallbackUserId
    local imageUrl = type(payload.imageUrl) == "string" and payload.imageUrl or nil
    return resolvedUserId, imageUrl
end

local function howWideIsThisText(text, textSize, font)
    local textBoundsYay = textServiceBeLikeText:GetTextSize(text, textSize, font or Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge))
    return textBoundsYay.X
end

local function truncateTextLikeABoss(text, maxWidth, textSize, font)
    local fullWidth = howWideIsThisText(text, textSize, font)
    if fullWidth <= maxWidth then
        return text
    end
    
    local truncated = text
    while howWideIsThisText(truncated .. "...", textSize, font) > maxWidth and #truncated > 0 do
        truncated = truncated:sub(1, -2)
    end
    return truncated .. "..."
end

local function normalizeSearchText(text)
    return string.lower(tostring(text or "")):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
end

local function compactSearchText(text)
    return normalizeSearchText(text):gsub("[^%w]+", "")
end

local function tokenizeSearchText(text)
    local tokens = {}
    local seen = {}
    for token in normalizeSearchText(text):gmatch("[%w]+") do
        if not seen[token] then
            seen[token] = true
            table.insert(tokens, token)
        end
    end
    return tokens
end

local function buildSearchQueryInfo(query)
    local normalized = normalizeSearchText(query)
    local tokens = tokenizeSearchText(normalized)
    return {
        Normalized = normalized,
        Compact = compactSearchText(normalized),
        Tokens = tokens,
        TokenCount = #tokens
    }
end

local function computeCompactSubsequenceScore(queryCompact, candidateCompact)
    if queryCompact == "" or candidateCompact == "" or #queryCompact > #candidateCompact then
        return 0, false
    end

    local queryIndex = 1
    local startIndex = nil

    for candidateIndex = 1, #candidateCompact do
        if candidateCompact:sub(candidateIndex, candidateIndex) == queryCompact:sub(queryIndex, queryIndex) then
            if startIndex == nil then
                startIndex = candidateIndex
            end
            queryIndex = queryIndex + 1
            if queryIndex > #queryCompact then
                local span = candidateIndex - startIndex + 1
                local extraGap = math.max(0, span - #queryCompact)
                local score = 170 - math.min(90, extraGap * 8) - math.min(36, (startIndex - 1) * 3)
                return math.max(score, 36), true
            end
        end
    end

    return 0, false
end

local function scoreSearchCandidate(queryInfo, candidateText)
    local candidateNormalized = normalizeSearchText(candidateText)
    if candidateNormalized == "" then
        return 0, 0, false
    end

    if queryInfo.Normalized == "" then
        return 1, 0, true
    end

    local candidateCompact = compactSearchText(candidateNormalized)
    local bestScore = 0
    local tokenMatches = 0
    local directIndex = string.find(candidateNormalized, queryInfo.Normalized, 1, true)

    if candidateNormalized == queryInfo.Normalized then
        bestScore = math.max(bestScore, 1240)
    end
    if candidateCompact ~= "" and candidateCompact == queryInfo.Compact and queryInfo.Compact ~= "" then
        bestScore = math.max(bestScore, 1180)
    end
    if directIndex then
        local directScore = directIndex == 1 and 1020 or math.max(660, 890 - ((directIndex - 1) * 22))
        local previousChar = directIndex > 1 and candidateNormalized:sub(directIndex - 1, directIndex - 1) or ""
        if directIndex == 1 or previousChar:match("[^%w]") then
            directScore = directScore + 72
        end
        bestScore = math.max(bestScore, directScore)
    end

    for _, queryToken in ipairs(queryInfo.Tokens) do
        local tokenScore = 0
        for candidateWord in candidateNormalized:gmatch("[%w]+") do
            if candidateWord == queryToken then
                tokenScore = math.max(tokenScore, 250)
            elseif candidateWord:sub(1, #queryToken) == queryToken then
                tokenScore = math.max(tokenScore, 212)
            else
                local tokenIndex = string.find(candidateWord, queryToken, 1, true)
                if tokenIndex then
                    tokenScore = math.max(tokenScore, tokenIndex == 1 and 186 or 146)
                end
            end
        end

        if tokenScore == 0 and candidateCompact ~= "" then
            local fuzzyScore, matched = computeCompactSubsequenceScore(queryToken, candidateCompact)
            if matched then
                tokenScore = math.max(tokenScore, math.max(fuzzyScore - 52, 52))
            end
        end

        if tokenScore > 0 then
            tokenMatches = tokenMatches + 1
            bestScore = math.max(bestScore, tokenScore)
        end
    end

    if tokenMatches > 0 then
        bestScore = bestScore + (tokenMatches * 96)
        if tokenMatches == queryInfo.TokenCount and queryInfo.TokenCount > 1 then
            bestScore = bestScore + 185
        elseif queryInfo.TokenCount > tokenMatches then
            bestScore = bestScore - ((queryInfo.TokenCount - tokenMatches) * 70)
        end
    end

    local fuzzyScore, fuzzyMatched = computeCompactSubsequenceScore(queryInfo.Compact, candidateCompact)
    if fuzzyMatched then
        bestScore = math.max(bestScore, fuzzyScore + (tokenMatches * 44))
    end

    return math.max(bestScore, 0), tokenMatches, bestScore > 0 or fuzzyMatched or directIndex ~= nil
end

local function scoreSearchTermList(queryInfo, termList)
    if queryInfo.Normalized == "" then
        return 1, 0, true
    end

    if type(termList) ~= "table" then
        return 0, 0, false
    end

    local bestScore = 0
    local maxTokenMatches = 0
    local matched = false
    local normalizedTerms = {}

    for _, term in ipairs(termList) do
        local normalized = normalizeSearchText(term)
        if normalized ~= "" then
            table.insert(normalizedTerms, normalized)
            local score, tokenMatches, didMatch = scoreSearchCandidate(queryInfo, normalized)
            if score > bestScore then
                bestScore = score
            end
            if tokenMatches > maxTokenMatches then
                maxTokenMatches = tokenMatches
            end
            matched = matched or didMatch
        end
    end

    if #normalizedTerms > 1 then
        local combinedScore, combinedTokenMatches, combinedMatched = scoreSearchCandidate(queryInfo, table.concat(normalizedTerms, " "))
        combinedScore = combinedScore + math.min(120, #normalizedTerms * 12)
        if combinedScore > bestScore then
            bestScore = combinedScore
        end
        if combinedTokenMatches > maxTokenMatches then
            maxTokenMatches = combinedTokenMatches
        end
        matched = matched or combinedMatched
    end

    return bestScore, maxTokenMatches, matched
end

local function normalizeDropdownOptions(options)
    local normalized = {}
    if type(options) == "table" then
        for _, option in ipairs(options) do
            if option ~= nil then
                table.insert(normalized, option)
            end
        end
    end
    if #normalized == 0 then
        normalized[1] = "None"
    end
    return normalized
end

local function getDropdownOptionsSignature(options)
    if type(options) ~= "table" then
        return "0"
    end
    local count = #options
    if count <= 0 then
        return "0"
    end
    local signaturePieces = table.create and table.create(count + 1, "") or {}
    signaturePieces[1] = tostring(count)
    for index, option in ipairs(options) do
        signaturePieces[index + 1] = tostring(option)
    end
    return table.concat(signaturePieces, "\31")
end

local function getNumberDecimalPlaces(value)
    if type(value) ~= "number" then
        return 0
    end
    local valueString = tostring(value)
    local decimalPart = valueString:match("%.(%d+)")
    if decimalPart then
        return #decimalPart
    end
    local exponentPart = valueString:match("[eE]([%+%-]?%d+)")
    if exponentPart then
        local exponent = tonumber(exponentPart) or 0
        if exponent < 0 then
            return -exponent
        end
    end
    return 0
end

local function roundToDecimalPlaces(value, decimals)
    if decimals <= 0 then
        if value >= 0 then
            return math.floor(value + 0.5)
        end
        return math.ceil(value - 0.5)
    end
    local factor = 10 ^ decimals
    if value >= 0 then
        return math.floor(value * factor + 0.5) / factor
    end
    return math.ceil(value * factor - 0.5) / factor
end

local function resolveSliderPrecision(minValue, maxValue, increment, defaultValue)
    local precision = 0
    precision = math.max(precision, getNumberDecimalPlaces(minValue))
    precision = math.max(precision, getNumberDecimalPlaces(maxValue))
    precision = math.max(precision, getNumberDecimalPlaces(increment))
    precision = math.max(precision, getNumberDecimalPlaces(defaultValue))
    return math.clamp(precision, 0, 6)
end

local function normalizeSliderValue(value, minValue, maxValue, increment, precision)
    local numericValue = tonumber(value) or minValue
    numericValue = math.clamp(numericValue, minValue, maxValue)
    local normalizedIncrement = math.max(math.abs(tonumber(increment) or 1), 1e-6)
    local steps = math.floor(((numericValue - minValue) / normalizedIncrement) + 0.5)
    local snappedValue = minValue + (steps * normalizedIncrement)
    snappedValue = roundToDecimalPlaces(snappedValue, precision)
    return math.clamp(snappedValue, minValue, maxValue)
end

local function formatSliderValue(value, precision)
    if precision <= 0 then
        return tostring(roundToDecimalPlaces(value, 0))
    end
    local formatted = string.format("%." .. tostring(precision) .. "f", value)
    formatted = formatted:gsub("(%..-)0+$", "%1"):gsub("%.$", "")
    return formatted
end

local function serializeConfigValue(value)
    local valueType = typeof(value)
    if valueType == "Color3" then
        return {
            __type = "Color3",
            r = value.R,
            g = value.G,
            b = value.B
        }
    end
    if valueType == "EnumItem" and value.EnumType == Enum.KeyCode then
        return {
            __type = "KeyCode",
            value = value.Name
        }
    end
    return value
end

local function deserializeConfigValue(value)
    if type(value) ~= "table" or not value.__type then
        return value
    end
    if value.__type == "Color3" and value.r and value.g and value.b then
        return Color3.new(value.r, value.g, value.b)
    end
    if value.__type == "KeyCode" and value.value then
        return Enum.KeyCode[value.value] or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
    end
    return value
end

local function sanitizeConfigName(name)
    local cleanName = tostring(name or "default")
    cleanName = cleanName:gsub("[\\/:*?\"<>|]", "_")
    cleanName = cleanName:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if cleanName == "" then
        cleanName = "default"
    end
    return cleanName
end

local function getNexusUIConfigFolderPath()
    return "Nexushub/configs"
end

local function getLegacyNexusUIConfigFolderPath()
    return "NexusUIConfigs"
end

local function ensureNexusUIConfigFolder()
    local folder = getNexusUIConfigFolderPath()
    local rootFolder = "Nexushub"

    if makefolder and (not isfolder or not isfolder(rootFolder)) then
        pcall(function()
            makefolder(rootFolder)
        end)
    end

    if isfolder and isfolder(folder) then
        return true, folder
    end

    if makefolder then
        pcall(function()
            makefolder(folder)
        end)
    end

    if isfolder and isfolder(folder) then
        return true, folder
    end

    return false, folder
end

local function getNexusUIConfigFileName(configName)
    return sanitizeConfigName(configName) .. ".pak"
end

local function getWritableNexusUIConfigPath(configName)
    local fileName = getNexusUIConfigFileName(configName)
    local okFolder, folder = ensureNexusUIConfigFolder()
    if okFolder then
        return folder .. "/" .. fileName, true
    end
    return fileName, false
end

local function getReadableNexusUIConfigPaths(configName)
    local fileName = getNexusUIConfigFileName(configName)
    local legacyFileName = sanitizeConfigName(configName) .. ".json"
    local folder = getNexusUIConfigFolderPath()
    local legacyFolder = getLegacyNexusUIConfigFolderPath()
    return {
        folder .. "/" .. fileName,
        folder .. "\\" .. fileName,
        folder .. "/" .. legacyFileName,
        folder .. "\\" .. legacyFileName,
        legacyFolder .. "/" .. fileName,
        legacyFolder .. "\\" .. fileName,
        legacyFolder .. "/" .. legacyFileName,
        legacyFolder .. "\\" .. legacyFileName,
        legacyFileName,
        fileName
    }
end

local function getNexusUIAssetFolderPath()
    return getNexusUIConfigFolderPath() .. "/assetys"
end

local function ensureNexusUIAssetFolder()
    local folder = getNexusUIAssetFolderPath()

    if isfolder and isfolder(folder) then
        return true, folder
    end

    ensureNexusUIConfigFolder()

    if makefolder then
        pcall(function()
            makefolder(folder)
        end)
    end

    if isfolder and isfolder(folder) then
        return true, folder
    end

    return false, folder
end

local function getNexusUIAssetLoader()
    if type(getcustomasset) == "function" then
        return getcustomasset
    end
    if type(getsynasset) == "function" then
        return getsynasset
    end
    if type(syn) == "table" and type(syn.asset) == "function" then
        return syn.asset
    end
    return nil
end

local function getNexusUIRequestFunction()
    if type(request) == "function" then
        return request
    end
    if type(http_request) == "function" then
        return http_request
    end
    if type(syn) == "table" and type(syn.request) == "function" then
        return syn.request
    end
    if type(http) == "table" and type(http.request) == "function" then
        return http.request
    end
    if type(fluxus) == "table" and type(fluxus.request) == "function" then
        return fluxus.request
    end
    return nil
end

local function sanitizeNexusUIAssetName(name)
    local sanitized = tostring(name or "background")
    sanitized = sanitized:gsub("[\\/:*?\"<>|]", "_")
    sanitized = sanitized:gsub("[%c%s]+", "_")
    sanitized = sanitized:gsub("[^%w%._%-]", "_")
    sanitized = sanitized:gsub("_+", "_")
    sanitized = sanitized:gsub("^_+", ""):gsub("_+$", "")
    if sanitized == "" then
        sanitized = "background"
    end
    if #sanitized > 48 then
        sanitized = sanitized:sub(1, 48)
    end
    return sanitized
end

local function hashNexusUIText(text)
    local hash = 0
    local input = tostring(text or "")
    for index = 1, #input do
        hash = (hash * 131 + string.byte(input, index)) % 2147483647
    end
    return string.format("%08x", hash)
end

local function normalizeNexusUIKeyScope(scope)
    local normalized = tostring(scope or "ui_v2"):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", ""):lower()
    normalized = normalized:gsub("[^%w_%-%.]", "_")
    if normalized == "" then
        normalized = "ui_v2"
    end
    return normalized
end

local function encodeNexusUIStoredSecret(value, scope)
    local secret = tostring(getNexusUISecurityHWID() or "unknown") .. ":" .. normalizeNexusUIKeyScope(scope) .. ":nexus-ui-key-store"
    local output = {}
    local secretLength = #secret

    for index = 1, #value do
        local valueByte = string.byte(value, index)
        local secretByte = string.byte(secret, ((index - 1) % secretLength) + 1)
        output[index] = string.format("%02X", (valueByte + secretByte + index) % 256)
    end

    return table.concat(output)
end

local function decodeNexusUIStoredSecret(encoded, scope)
    local secret = tostring(getNexusUISecurityHWID() or "unknown") .. ":" .. normalizeNexusUIKeyScope(scope) .. ":nexus-ui-key-store"
    local output = {}
    local secretLength = #secret
    local cursor = 1
    local outputIndex = 1

    while cursor < #encoded do
        local chunk = string.sub(encoded, cursor, cursor + 1)
        local encodedByte = tonumber(chunk, 16)
        if not encodedByte then
            return nil
        end
        local secretByte = string.byte(secret, ((outputIndex - 1) % secretLength) + 1)
        output[outputIndex] = string.char((encodedByte - secretByte - outputIndex) % 256)
        cursor = cursor + 2
        outputIndex = outputIndex + 1
    end

    return table.concat(output)
end

local function getNexusUIHeaderValue(headers, headerName)
    if type(headers) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    local targetName = string.lower(tostring(headerName or ""))
    for key, value in pairs(headers) do
        if string.lower(tostring(key)) == targetName then
            return value
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function getNexusUIImageExtension(source, contentType)
    local normalizedSource = tostring(source or "")
    local pathWithoutQuery = normalizedSource:match("^[^%?#]+") or normalizedSource
    local directExtension = pathWithoutQuery:match("%.([%w]+)$")
    if directExtension then
        directExtension = string.lower(directExtension)
        if directExtension == "png" or directExtension == "jpg" or directExtension == "jpeg" or directExtension == "webp" or directExtension == "bmp" or directExtension == "gif" then
            return "." .. directExtension
        end
    end

    local normalizedType = string.lower(tostring(contentType or ""))
    if normalizedType:find("png", 1, true) then
        return ".png"
    end
    if normalizedType:find("jpeg", 1, true) or normalizedType:find("jpg", 1, true) then
        return ".jpg"
    end
    if normalizedType:find("webp", 1, true) then
        return ".webp"
    end
    if normalizedType:find("bmp", 1, true) then
        return ".bmp"
    end
    if normalizedType:find("gif", 1, true) then
        return ".gif"
    end

    return ".png"
end

local function fetchNexusUIRemoteImage(url, headers)
    local requestFn = getNexusUIRequestFunction()
    if requestFn then
        local okResponse, response = pcall(function()
            return requestFn({
                Url = url,
                Method = "GET",
                Headers = type(headers) == "table" and headers or nil
            })
        end)

        if okResponse and type(response) == "table" then
            local statusCode = tonumber(response.StatusCode or response.Status or response.Code or 0) or 0
            local body = response.Body or response.body or response.ResponseBody or response.responseBody
            if statusCode >= 200 and statusCode < 300 and type(body) == "string" and body ~= "" then
                return true, body, response.Headers or response.headers
            end
        end
    end

    local okRequestAsync, requestAsyncResponse = pcall(function()
        return httpServiceNoCap:RequestAsync({
            Url = url,
            Method = "GET",
            Headers = type(headers) == "table" and headers or nil
        })
    end)
    if okRequestAsync and type(requestAsyncResponse) == "table" then
        local statusCode = tonumber(requestAsyncResponse.StatusCode or requestAsyncResponse.Status or requestAsyncResponse.Code or 0) or 0
        local body = requestAsyncResponse.Body or requestAsyncResponse.body or requestAsyncResponse.ResponseBody or requestAsyncResponse.responseBody
        local success = requestAsyncResponse.Success
        if ((success == true) or (success == nil and statusCode >= 200 and statusCode < 300))
            and type(body) == "string" and body ~= "" then
            return true, body, requestAsyncResponse.Headers or requestAsyncResponse.headers
        end
    end

    local okHttpServiceGet, httpServiceBody = pcall(function()
        return httpServiceNoCap:GetAsync(url, false, type(headers) == "table" and headers or nil)
    end)
    if okHttpServiceGet and type(httpServiceBody) == "string" and httpServiceBody ~= "" then
        return true, httpServiceBody, nil
    end

    local okHttpGet, body = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet(url)
    end)
    if okHttpGet and type(body) == "string" and body ~= "" then
        return true, body, nil
    end

    return false, nil, "Failed to download image body."
end

local function normalizeNexusUIBackgroundImageSource(source)
    if source == nil then
        return ""
    end
    if type(source) == "number" then
        local rounded = math.floor(source + 0.5)
        if rounded > 0 then
            return "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(rounded)
        end
        return ""
    end

    local normalized = tostring(source)
    normalized = normalized:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if normalized == "" then
        return ""
    end
    if normalized:match("^%d+$") then
        return "rbxassetid://" .. normalized
    end
    return normalized
end

local function resolveNexusUIBackgroundImageSource(source, headers)
    local normalizedSource = normalizeNexusUIBackgroundImageSource(source)
    local result = {
        Source = normalizedSource,
        ResolvedImage = "",
        LocalPath = nil,
        IsCustomAsset = false,
        Error = nil
    }

    if normalizedSource == "" then
        return result
    end

    if normalizedSource:match("^rbxassetid://") or normalizedSource:match("^rbxasset://") then
        result.ResolvedImage = normalizedSource
        return result
    end

    local assetLoader = getNexusUIAssetLoader()

    if isfile then
        local okExists, exists = pcall(function()
            return isfile(normalizedSource)
        end)
        if okExists and exists then
            if assetLoader then
                local okAsset, assetId = pcall(assetLoader, normalizedSource)
                if okAsset and type(assetId) == "string" then
                    result.ResolvedImage = assetId
                    result.LocalPath = normalizedSource
                    result.IsCustomAsset = true
                    return result
                end
                result.Error = "Failed to load custom asset: " .. tostring(assetId)
                return result
            end
            result.Error = "Custom asset loader is unavailable in this executor."
            return result
        end
    end

    if normalizedSource:match("^https?://") then
        if not writefile then
            result.Error = "writefile API is unavailable in this executor."
            return result
        end
        if not assetLoader then
            result.Error = "Custom asset loader is unavailable in this executor."
            return result
        end

        local okAssetFolder, assetFolder = ensureNexusUIAssetFolder()
        if not okAssetFolder then
            result.Error = "Failed to create asset cache folder."
            return result
        end

        local sourceStem = normalizedSource:match("/([^/%?#]+)") or "background"
        sourceStem = sourceStem:gsub("%.[%w]+$", "")
        sourceStem = sanitizeNexusUIAssetName(sourceStem)

        local cachedExtension = getNexusUIImageExtension(normalizedSource, nil)
        local cachedPath = assetFolder .. "/" .. sourceStem .. "_" .. hashNexusUIText(normalizedSource) .. cachedExtension

        local hasCachedFile = false
        if isfile then
            local okCached, cachedExists = pcall(function()
                return isfile(cachedPath)
            end)
            hasCachedFile = okCached and cachedExists == true
        end

        if not hasCachedFile then
            local okFetch, body, headersOrError = fetchNexusUIRemoteImage(normalizedSource, headers)
            if not okFetch or type(body) ~= "string" or body == "" then
                result.Error = tostring(headersOrError or "Unknown download failure.")
                return result
            end

            local resolvedExtension = getNexusUIImageExtension(normalizedSource, getNexusUIHeaderValue(headersOrError, "Content-Type"))
            if resolvedExtension ~= cachedExtension then
                cachedExtension = resolvedExtension
                cachedPath = assetFolder .. "/" .. sourceStem .. "_" .. hashNexusUIText(normalizedSource) .. cachedExtension
            end

            local okWrite, errWrite = pcall(function()
                writefile(cachedPath, body)
            end)
            if not okWrite then
                result.Error = "Failed to cache downloaded image: " .. tostring(errWrite)
                return result
            end
        end

        local okAsset, assetId = pcall(assetLoader, cachedPath)
        if okAsset and type(assetId) == "string" then
            result.ResolvedImage = assetId
            result.LocalPath = cachedPath
            result.IsCustomAsset = true
            return result
        end

        result.Error = "Failed to register cached image as a Roblox asset."
        return result
    end

    result.ResolvedImage = normalizedSource
    return result
end

local function resolveNexusUIRelativeUrl(baseUrl, maybeRelativeUrl)
    local candidate = tostring(maybeRelativeUrl or "")
    if candidate == "" then
        return ""
    end
    if candidate:match("^https?://") then
        return candidate
    end

    local origin = tostring(baseUrl or ""):match("^(https?://[^/]+)")
    if candidate:sub(1, 1) == "/" and origin then
        return origin .. candidate
    end

    local baseDirectory = tostring(baseUrl or ""):match("^(.*[/\\])") or tostring(baseUrl or "")
    return baseDirectory .. candidate
end

local function appendNexusUIQueryValue(url, key, value)
    local normalizedUrl = tostring(url or "")
    if normalizedUrl == "" or not normalizedUrl:match("^https?://") then
        return normalizedUrl
    end
    if value == nil or value == "" then
        return normalizedUrl
    end
    local queryKey = tostring(key or "")
    if queryKey == "" then
        return normalizedUrl
    end
    if normalizedUrl:find("[?&]" .. queryKey .. "=", 1, false) then
        return normalizedUrl
    end
    local separator = normalizedUrl:find("?", 1, true) and "&" or "?"
    return normalizedUrl .. separator .. queryKey .. "=" .. httpServiceNoCap:UrlEncode(tostring(value or ""))
end

local function loadNexusUIJsonDocument(source, headers)
    if type(source) == "table" then
        return true, source
    end

    local normalizedSource = tostring(source or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if normalizedSource == "" then
        return false, "JSON source is empty."
    end

    local body = nil
    if normalizedSource:match("^https?://") then
        local okFetch, fetchedBody, fetchError = fetchNexusUIRemoteImage(normalizedSource, headers)
        if not okFetch or type(fetchedBody) ~= "string" or fetchedBody == "" then
            return false, "Failed to fetch JSON: " .. tostring(fetchError)
        end
        body = fetchedBody
    else
        if type(readfile) ~= "function" then
            return false, "readfile API is unavailable."
        end
        local okRead, fileBody = pcall(function()
            return readfile(normalizedSource)
        end)
        if not okRead or type(fileBody) ~= "string" or fileBody == "" then
            return false, "Failed to read JSON source: " .. normalizedSource
        end
        body = fileBody
    end

    local okDecode, decoded = pcall(function()
        return httpServiceNoCap:JSONDecode(body)
    end)
    if not okDecode or type(decoded) ~= "table" then
        return false, "JSON source is invalid."
    end

    return true, decoded
end

local NexusUIRuntimeInstanceKey = "__NEXUS_UI_ACTIVE_LIBRARY"
local LegacyNexusUIRuntimeInstanceKey = "__Nexus_UI_ACTIVE_LIBRARY"
local NexusUISecurityStateKey = "__NEXUS_UI_SECURITY"
local LegacyNexusUISecurityStateKey = "__Nexus_UI_SECURITY"
local NexusUISecurityErrorKey = "__NEXUS_UI_SECURITY_ERROR"
local LegacyNexusUISecurityErrorKey = "__Nexus_UI_SECURITY_ERROR"
local NexusUIScreenGuiName = "NexusUIRoot"
local LegacyNexusUIScreenGuiName = "v0rtexdBestUIEver"

local function getNexusUISharedEnvironment()
    if type(getgenv) == "function" then
        local okEnv, sharedEnv = pcall(getgenv)
        if okEnv and type(sharedEnv) == "table" then
            return sharedEnv
        end
    end
    return _G
end

local function destroyExistingNexusUIScreenGuis()
    local roots = {}
    local seenRoots = {}

    local function addRoot(root)
        if not root or seenRoots[root] then
            return
        end
        seenRoots[root] = true
        table.insert(roots, root)
    end

    addRoot(coreGuiMoreLikeBoreGui)

    if type(gethui) == "function" then
        local okHui, huiRoot = pcall(gethui)
        if okHui then
            addRoot(huiRoot)
        end
    end

    if theOneAndOnlyPlayer then
        local okPlayerGui, playerGui = pcall(function()
            return theOneAndOnlyPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        end)
        if okPlayerGui then
            addRoot(playerGui)
        end
    end

    for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
        local okChildren, children = pcall(function()
            return root:GetChildren()
        end)
        if okChildren and type(children) == "table" then
            for _, child in ipairs(children) do
                if child and child:IsA("ScreenGui") and (child.Name == NexusUIScreenGuiName or child.Name == LegacyNexusUIScreenGuiName) then
                    pcall(function()
                        child:Destroy()
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local function cleanupPreviousNexusUIInstance()
    local sharedEnv = getNexusUISharedEnvironment()
    local previousInstance = rawget(sharedEnv, NexusUIRuntimeInstanceKey) or rawget(sharedEnv, LegacyNexusUIRuntimeInstanceKey)
    if previousInstance and type(previousInstance) == "table" and type(previousInstance.Destroy) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            previousInstance:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    rawset(sharedEnv, NexusUIRuntimeInstanceKey, nil)
    rawset(sharedEnv, LegacyNexusUIRuntimeInstanceKey, nil)
    destroyExistingNexusUIScreenGuis()
end

local function cloneNexusUITable(source)
    local cloned = {}
    if type(source) ~= "table" then
        return cloned
    end
    for key, value in pairs(source) do
        cloned[key] = value
    end
    return cloned
end

local function mergeIntoNexusUITable(target, source)
    if type(target) ~= "table" or type(source) ~= "table" then
        return target
    end
    for key, value in pairs(source) do
        target[key] = value
    end
    return target
end

local function generateNexusUISecurityNonce(length)
    length = math.max(tonumber(length) or 24, 8)
    local nonce = ""
    for index = 1, length do
        nonce = nonce .. string.format("%x", math.random(0, 15))
    end
    return nonce
end

local function computeNexusUISecuritySignature(apiKey, timestamp, nonce)
    local data = tostring(apiKey or "") .. tostring(timestamp or "") .. tostring(nonce or "")
    local hashA = 5381
    local hashB = 5381
    for index = 1, #data do
        local byteValue = string.byte(data, index)
        hashA = (hashA * 33 + byteValue) % 4294967296
        hashB = (hashB * 65599 + byteValue) % 4294967296
    end
    return string.sub(string.format("%08X%08X", hashA, hashB), 1, 16)
end

function getNexusUISecurityHWID()
    local hwid = "unknown"
    pcall(function()
        hwid = game:GetService("RbxAnalyticsService"):GetClientId()
    end)
    return tostring(hwid or "unknown")
end

local function verifyNexusUISecurityEnvironment(requireRequest)
    if pcall ~= capturedNexusUIPcall or tostring ~= capturedNexusUITostring or rawget ~= capturedNexusUIRawget or rawset ~= capturedNexusUIRawset then
        return false, "Detected runtime hook on protected core references."
    end

    if type(capturedNexusUIPcall) ~= "function" or type(capturedNexusUITostring) ~= "function" or type(capturedNexusUIRawget) ~= "function" or type(capturedNexusUIRawset) ~= "function" then
        return false, "Core runtime references are unavailable."
    end

    if task ~= capturedNexusUITask or type(capturedNexusUITask) ~= "table" or capturedNexusUITask.spawn ~= capturedNexusUITaskSpawn or capturedNexusUITask.wait ~= capturedNexusUITaskWait then
        return false, "Task scheduler APIs are unavailable."
    end

    if capturedNexusUIStringByte("A") ~= 65 or capturedNexusUIMathFloor(3.75) ~= 3 then
        return false, "Detected invalid core library behavior."
    end

    if requireRequest == true and type(getNexusUIRequestFunction()) ~= "function" then
        return false, "Executor request API is unavailable."
    end

    return true, nil
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary.SetApiKey(apiKey)
    local sharedEnv = getNexusUISharedEnvironment()
    local normalized = tostring(apiKey or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    rawset(sharedEnv, "NexusUIV2Key", normalized)
    rawset(sharedEnv, "NexusUIApiKey", normalized)
    rawset(sharedEnv, "NexusUIKey", normalized)
    return normalized ~= ""
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary.GetStoredKeyPath(scope)
    local safeScope = normalizeNexusUIKeyScope(scope)
    local fileName = "secure_key_" .. safeScope .. ".json"
    local okFolder, folder = ensureNexusUIConfigFolder()
    if okFolder then
        return folder .. "/" .. fileName
    end
    return fileName
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary.SaveSecureKey(scope, apiKey)
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
        return false, "writefile API is unavailable in this executor."
    end

    local safeScope = normalizeNexusUIKeyScope(scope)
    local normalizedKey = tostring(apiKey or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if normalizedKey == "" then
        return false, "Cannot store an empty key."
    end

    local payload = {
        version = 1,
        scope = safeScope,
        fingerprint = hashNexusUIText(normalizedKey),
        encoded = encodeNexusUIStoredSecret(normalizedKey, safeScope),
    }
    local encodedPayload = httpServiceNoCap:JSONEncode(payload)
    local path = superMegaAwesomeLibrary.GetStoredKeyPath(safeScope)
    local okWrite, writeError = pcall(function()
        writefile(path, encodedPayload)
    end)

    if not okWrite then
        return false, "Failed to store key: " .. tostring(writeError)
    end

    return true, path
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary.LoadSecureKey(scope)
    if type(readfile) ~= "function" then
        return nil, "readfile API is unavailable in this executor."
    end

    local safeScope = normalizeNexusUIKeyScope(scope)
    local path = superMegaAwesomeLibrary.GetStoredKeyPath(safeScope)
    if type(isfile) == "function" then
        local okExists, exists = pcall(function()
            return isfile(path)
        end)
        if okExists and exists ~= true then
            return nil, "No stored key."
        end
    end

    local okRead, rawData = pcall(function()
        return readfile(path)
    end)
    if not okRead or type(rawData) ~= "string" or rawData == "" then
        return nil, "No stored key."
    end

    local okDecode, payload = pcall(function()
        return httpServiceNoCap:JSONDecode(rawData)
    end)
    if not okDecode or type(payload) ~= "table" or type(payload.encoded) ~= "string" then
        return nil, "Stored key file is invalid."
    end

    local decoded = decodeNexusUIStoredSecret(payload.encoded, payload.scope or safeScope)
    if type(decoded) ~= "string" or decoded == "" then
        return nil, "Stored key could not be decoded on this device."
    end

    if payload.fingerprint and payload.fingerprint ~= hashNexusUIText(decoded) then
        return nil, "Stored key integrity check failed."
    end

    return decoded, path
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary.ClearSecureKey(scope)
    local path = superMegaAwesomeLibrary.GetStoredKeyPath(scope)
    if type(delfile) ~= "function" then
        return false, "delfile API is unavailable in this executor."
    end
    if type(isfile) == "function" then
        local okExists, exists = pcall(function()
            return isfile(path)
        end)
        if okExists and exists ~= true then
            return true, path
        end
    end
    local okDelete, deleteError = pcall(function()
        delfile(path)
    end)
    if not okDelete then
        return false, "Failed to delete stored key: " .. tostring(deleteError)
    end
    return true, path
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary.SetValidationUrl(validationUrl, heartbeatUrl)
    local sharedEnv = getNexusUISharedEnvironment()
    rawset(sharedEnv, "NexusUIValidationUrl", tostring(validationUrl or defaultNexusUISecurityValidateUrl))
    rawset(sharedEnv, "NexusUIAuthUrl", tostring(validationUrl or defaultNexusUISecurityValidateUrl))
    rawset(sharedEnv, "NexusUIHeartbeatUrl", tostring(heartbeatUrl or defaultNexusUISecurityHeartbeatUrl))
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary.SetClientVersion(clientVersion)
    local sharedEnv = getNexusUISharedEnvironment()
    local normalized = tostring(clientVersion or defaultNexusUIClientVersion):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if normalized == "" then
        normalized = defaultNexusUIClientVersion
    end
    rawset(sharedEnv, "NexusUIClientVersion", normalized)
    rawset(sharedEnv, "NexusUIVersion", normalized)
    return normalized
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary.SetClientProduct(clientProduct, scriptId)
    local sharedEnv = getNexusUISharedEnvironment()
    local normalizedProduct = tostring(clientProduct or "ui_v2"):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    local normalizedScriptId = tostring(scriptId or normalizedProduct):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if normalizedProduct == "" then
        normalizedProduct = "ui_v2"
    end
    if normalizedScriptId == "" then
        normalizedScriptId = normalizedProduct
    end
    rawset(sharedEnv, "NexusUIClientProduct", normalizedProduct)
    rawset(sharedEnv, "NexusUIProduct", normalizedProduct)
    rawset(sharedEnv, "NexusUIScriptId", normalizedScriptId)
    return normalizedProduct, normalizedScriptId
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary.ConfigureSecurity(securityConfig)
    local sharedEnv = getNexusUISharedEnvironment()
    securityConfig = type(securityConfig) == "table" and securityConfig or {}

    if securityConfig.ApiKey ~= nil or securityConfig.Key ~= nil then
        superMegaAwesomeLibrary.SetApiKey(resolveFirstDefined(securityConfig.ApiKey, securityConfig.Key))
    end
    if securityConfig.ApiBaseUrl ~= nil or securityConfig.BaseUrl ~= nil then
        rawset(sharedEnv, "NexusUIApiBaseUrl", tostring(resolveFirstDefined(securityConfig.ApiBaseUrl, securityConfig.BaseUrl)))
    end
    if securityConfig.ValidationUrl ~= nil or securityConfig.Url ~= nil or securityConfig.AuthUrl ~= nil or securityConfig.HeartbeatUrl ~= nil then
        superMegaAwesomeLibrary.SetValidationUrl(
            resolveFirstDefined(securityConfig.ValidationUrl, securityConfig.Url, securityConfig.AuthUrl),
            securityConfig.HeartbeatUrl
        )
    end
    if securityConfig.ClientVersion ~= nil or securityConfig.Version ~= nil then
        superMegaAwesomeLibrary.SetClientVersion(resolveFirstDefined(securityConfig.ClientVersion, securityConfig.Version))
    end
    if securityConfig.Product ~= nil or securityConfig.ScriptId ~= nil then
        superMegaAwesomeLibrary.SetClientProduct(securityConfig.Product, securityConfig.ScriptId)
    end

    return true
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary.new(config)
    cleanupPreviousNexusUIInstance()

    local self = setmetatable({}, superMegaAwesomeLibrary)
    
    self.config = config or {}
    local visualConfig = type(self.config.Visuals) == "table" and self.config.Visuals or {}
    self.config.Name = self.config.Name or "Nexus"
    self.config.AccentColor = self.config.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(168, 85, 247)
    self.config.BackgroundColor = self.config.BackgroundColor or Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 16)
    self.config.SecondaryColor = self.config.SecondaryColor or Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18)
    self.config.TextColor = self.config.TextColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    self.config.SubTextColor = self.config.SubTextColor or Color3.fromRGB(124, 124, 124)
    self.config.DynamicIslandLogoSource = resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.DynamicIslandLogoSource, self.config.DynamicIslandLogoSource, self.config.LogoSource, defaultNexusDynamicIslandLogo)
    self.config.DynamicIslandTitle = tostring(resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.DynamicIslandTitle, self.config.DynamicIslandTitle, "NEXUS") or "NEXUS")
    self.config.NotificationIconSource = resolveFirstDefined(
        visualConfig.NotificationIconSource,
        self.config.NotificationIconSource,
        self.config.NotificationIcon,
        self.config.DynamicIslandLogoSource,
        defaultNexusNotificationLogo
    )
    self.config.DynamicIslandClickable = resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.DynamicIslandClickable, self.config.DynamicIslandClickable)
    if self.config.DynamicIslandClickable == nil then
        self.config.DynamicIslandClickable = true
    end
    local backgroundImageEnabled = resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.BackgroundImageEnabled, self.config.BackgroundImageEnabled)
    local backgroundImageSource = resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.BackgroundImageSource, self.config.BackgroundImageSource, self.config.BackgroundImage)
    if backgroundImageEnabled == false then
        backgroundImageSource = ""
    elseif backgroundImageSource == nil then
        backgroundImageSource = defaultNexusBackgroundImage
    end
    self.config.BackgroundImageSource = backgroundImageSource
    self.config.BackgroundImageTransparency = math.clamp(tonumber(resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.BackgroundImageTransparency, self.config.BackgroundImageTransparency)) or 0.9, 0, 1)
    self.config.BackgroundOverlayOpacity = math.clamp(tonumber(resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.BackgroundOverlayOpacity, self.config.BackgroundOverlayOpacity)) or 0.1, 0, 1)
    self._backgroundImageState = resolveNexusUIBackgroundImageSource(self.config.BackgroundImageSource)
    self.config.BackgroundImage = self._backgroundImageState.ResolvedImage or ""
    self._backgroundOverlayOpacity = self.config.BackgroundOverlayOpacity
    self._configuredVideoLayers = resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.VideoLayers, self.config.VideoLayers)
    self._configuredBackgroundVideo = resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.BackgroundVideo, self.config.BackgroundVideo)
    self._configuredIntroVideo = resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.IntroVideo, self.config.IntroVideo)
    self._configuredLoadingVideo = resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.LoadingVideo, self.config.LoadingVideo)
    self._configuredVideoApiKey = resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.VideoApiKey, visualConfig.ApiKey, self.config.VideoApiKey, self.config.ApiKey)
    self._allowUserCustomization = resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.AllowUserCustomization, self.config.AllowUserCustomization, self.config.EnableUserCustomization)
    if self._allowUserCustomization == nil then
        self._allowUserCustomization = self.config.DisableUserCustomization ~= true and self.config.EnableUserCustomization == true
    end
    if self._allowUserCustomization == nil then
        self._allowUserCustomization = false
    end
    
    self.sectionsAreAwesome = {}
    self.tabsGoWoosh = {}
    self.currentTabYay = nil
    self.notificationsAreCool = {}
    self.isToggledOn = true
    self.dropdownHolderRef = nil
    self.toggleKeyCode = Enum.KeyCode.RightControl
    self.toggleButtonVisible = resolveFirstDefined(
        visualConfig.LegacyMobileToggle,
        self.config.LegacyMobileToggle,
        visualConfig.ShowMobileToggle,
        self.config.ShowMobileToggle,
        self.config.ToggleButtonVisible
    ) == true and self.config.DynamicIslandClickable ~= true
    self._destroyed = false
    self._connections = {}
    self._videoLayers = {}
    self._preloadedVideoAssetStore = {}
    self._videoLayerCounter = 0
    self._configuredVideoLayersCreated = false
    self._configuredVideoAssetsPrepared = false
    self._trackedControls = {}

        autoConfigSetting = self.config.AutoSaveConfig

        autoConfigSetting = false

    self._autoConfigEnabled = autoConfigSetting == true

        {Name = "Builder Mono", EnumFont = Enum.Font.RobotoMono, Family = "rbxasset://fonts/families/BuilderMono.json", Weight = Enum.FontWeight.Regular},
        {Name = "Builder Extended", EnumFont = Enum.Font.BuilderSansExtraBold, Family = "rbxasset://fonts/families/BuilderExtended.json", Weight = Enum.FontWeight.Heavy},
        {Name = "Comic Neue Angular", EnumFont = Enum.Font.Cartoon, Family = "rbxasset://fonts/families/ComicNeueAngular.json", Weight = Enum.FontWeight.Bold},
        {Name = "Roman Antique", EnumFont = Enum.Font.Antique, Family = "rbxasset://fonts/families/RomanAntique.json", Weight = Enum.FontWeight.Regular},
        {Name = "Press Start 2P", EnumFont = Enum.Font.Arcade, Family = "rbxasset://fonts/families/PressStart2P.json", Weight = Enum.FontWeight.Regular},
        {Name = "Balthazar", EnumFont = Enum.Font.Garamond, Family = "rbxasset://fonts/families/Balthazar.json", Weight = Enum.FontWeight.Regular},
        {Name = "Accanthis ADF Std", EnumFont = Enum.Font.Garamond, Family = "rbxasset://fonts/families/AccanthisADFStd.json", Weight = Enum.FontWeight.Regular}
    }
    self._fontPresetIndex = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(resolveFirstDefined(visualConfig.FontPresetIndex, self.config.DefaultFontPresetIndex, self.config.FontPresetIndex)) or 1), 1, #self._fontPresets)
    self._gradientLabels = {}
    self._gradientObjects = {}
    self._espPreviewProvider = nil
    self._espPreviewData = nil
    self._espPreviewState = nil
    self._espPreviewResolveAccumulator = 0
    self._espPreviewUpdateAccumulator = 0
    self._espPreviewWasShowing = false
    self._espPreviewPanel = nil
    self._espPreviewViewport = nil
    self._espPreviewWorldModel = nil
    self._espPreviewCamera = nil
    self._espPreviewCharacter = nil
    self._espPreviewLastCharacter = nil
    self._espPreviewPartDefaults = {}
    self._espPreviewHeadPart = nil
    self._espPreviewRootPart = nil
    self._espPreviewVisualState = nil
    self._espPreviewVisualDirty = false
    self._espPreviewProjectionCache = nil
    self._espPreviewProjectionDirty = true
    self._espPreviewHighlight = nil
    self._espPreviewHeaderTag = nil
    self._espPreviewBox = nil
    self._espPreviewBoxStroke = nil
    self._espPreviewHealthTrack = nil
    self._espPreviewHealthFill = nil
    self._espPreviewDot = nil
    self._espPreviewTracer = nil
    self._espPreviewName = nil
    self._espPreviewItem = nil
    self._espPreviewDistance = nil
    self._espPreviewWalkTrack = nil
    self._espPreviewAnimationId = nil
    self._espPreviewAvatar3DUserId = tonumber(theOneAndOnlyPlayer.UserId) or 0
    self._espPreviewAvatar3DImageUrl = nil
    self._espPreviewRotationYaw = math.rad(180)
    self._espPreviewRotationTargetYaw = math.rad(180)
    self._espPreviewStaticMode = true
    self._espPreviewAllowManualRotation = true
    self._espPreviewPivotYOffset = -2
    self._espPreviewRotateCapture = nil
    self._espPreviewIsRotating = false
    self._espPreviewRotateInput = nil
    self._espPreviewRotateLastX = 0
    self._securityConfig = self:_BuildSecurityConfig()
    local securityOk, securityErr = self:_ValidateSecurityAccess(false)
    if not securityOk then
        error("[NexusUI] " .. tostring(securityErr or "API-key validation failed."))
    end
    
    self:_PrepareConfiguredVideoAssets()
    self:CreateUIBecauseWeNeedIt()
    self:_StartSecurityMonitor()
    rawset(getNexusUISharedEnvironment(), NexusUIRuntimeInstanceKey, self)
    rawset(getNexusUISharedEnvironment(), LegacyNexusUIRuntimeInstanceKey, self)
    
    return self
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_BuildSecurityConfig()
    local sharedEnv = getNexusUISharedEnvironment()
    local securityConfig = type(self.config.Security) == "table" and cloneNexusUITable(self.config.Security) or {}

    securityConfig.Required = resolveFirstDefined(
        securityConfig.Required,
        self.config.RequireApiKey,
        self.config.RequireSecurity
    )
    if securityConfig.Required == nil then
        securityConfig.Required = true
    end

    securityConfig.ApiKey = resolveFirstDefined(
        securityConfig.ApiKey,
        securityConfig.Key,
        self.config.ApiKey,
        self.config.Key,
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIV2Key"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIApiKey"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIKey"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusApiKey"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "ApiKey"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "Key")
    )

    securityConfig.ValidationUrl = resolveFirstDefined(
        securityConfig.ValidationUrl,
        securityConfig.Url,
        securityConfig.AuthUrl,
        self.config.ValidationUrl,
        self.config.AuthUrl,
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIValidationUrl"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIAuthUrl"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIApiBaseUrl") and (tostring(rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIApiBaseUrl")):gsub("/+$", "") .. "/validate"),
        defaultNexusUISecurityValidateUrl
    )

    securityConfig.HeartbeatUrl = resolveFirstDefined(
        securityConfig.HeartbeatUrl,
        securityConfig.RevalidateUrl,
        self.config.HeartbeatUrl,
        self.config.RevalidateUrl,
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIHeartbeatUrl"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIApiBaseUrl") and (tostring(rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIApiBaseUrl")):gsub("/+$", "") .. "/heartbeat"),
        defaultNexusUISecurityHeartbeatUrl
    )

    securityConfig.Method = string.upper(tostring(resolveFirstDefined(
        securityConfig.Method,
        securityConfig.ValidationMethod,
        self.config.ValidationMethod,
        "POST"
    )))

    securityConfig.RevalidateInterval = math.max(tonumber(resolveFirstDefined(
        securityConfig.RevalidateInterval,
        self.config.RevalidateInterval,
        180
    )) or 180, 30)

    securityConfig.AllowOffline = securityConfig.AllowOffline == true
    securityConfig.Product = tostring(resolveFirstDefined(
        securityConfig.Product,
        self.config.Product,
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIClientProduct"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIProduct"),
        "ui_v2"
    ) or "ui_v2")
    securityConfig.ScriptId = tostring(resolveFirstDefined(
        securityConfig.ScriptId,
        self.config.ScriptId,
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIScriptId"),
        securityConfig.Product,
        "ui_v2"
    ) or "ui_v2")
    securityConfig.ClientVersion = tostring(resolveFirstDefined(
        securityConfig.ClientVersion,
        self.config.ClientVersion,
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIClientVersion"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIVersion"),
        defaultNexusUIClientVersion
    ) or defaultNexusUIClientVersion)
    securityConfig.ApiKeyHeader = tostring(resolveFirstDefined(
        securityConfig.ApiKeyHeader,
        self.config.ApiKeyHeader,
        "X-Nexus-UI-Key"
    ) or "X-Nexus-UI-Key")
    securityConfig.QueryKeyName = tostring(resolveFirstDefined(
        securityConfig.QueryKeyName,
        self.config.QueryKeyName,
        "api_key"
    ) or "api_key")
    securityConfig.Headers = type(securityConfig.Headers) == "table" and cloneNexusUITable(securityConfig.Headers) or {}
    securityConfig.Payload = type(securityConfig.Payload) == "table" and cloneNexusUITable(securityConfig.Payload) or {}
    securityConfig.Validate = type(securityConfig.Validate) == "function" and securityConfig.Validate or self.config.ValidateApiKey
    securityConfig.IsValidResponse = type(securityConfig.IsValidResponse) == "function" and securityConfig.IsValidResponse or nil
    securityConfig.BuildPayload = type(securityConfig.BuildPayload) == "function" and securityConfig.BuildPayload or nil

    if type(securityConfig.ValidationUrl) ~= "string" or securityConfig.ValidationUrl == "" then
        securityConfig.ValidationUrl = nil
    end
    if type(securityConfig.HeartbeatUrl) ~= "string" or securityConfig.HeartbeatUrl == "" then
        securityConfig.HeartbeatUrl = securityConfig.ValidationUrl
    end

    return securityConfig
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_BuildSecurityContext(isRevalidation)
    local sharedEnv = getNexusUISharedEnvironment()
    local securityConfig = self._securityConfig or {}
    local apiKey = resolveFirstDefined(
        securityConfig.ApiKey,
        self.config.ApiKey,
        self.config.Key,
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIV2Key"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIApiKey"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusUIKey"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "NexusApiKey"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "ApiKey"),
        rawget(sharedEnv, "Key")
    )
    apiKey = tostring(apiKey or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    securityConfig.ApiKey = apiKey

    local timestamp = tostring(math.floor(os.time()))
    local nonce = generateNexusUISecurityNonce(24)

    return {
        ApiKey = apiKey,
        Hwid = getNexusUISecurityHWID(),
        Timestamp = timestamp,
        Nonce = nonce,
        Signature = computeNexusUISecuritySignature(apiKey, timestamp, nonce),
        Product = tostring(securityConfig.Product or "ui_v2"),
        ScriptId = tostring(securityConfig.ScriptId or securityConfig.Product or "ui_v2"),
