        ClientVersion = tostring(securityConfig.ClientVersion or defaultNexusUIClientVersion),
        Revalidation = isRevalidation == true,
    }
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_RunSecurityCallback(context, isRevalidation)
    local securityConfig = self._securityConfig or {}
    local validateFn = securityConfig.Validate
    if type(validateFn) ~= "function" then
        return true, nil
    end

    local okCall, resultA, resultB = pcall(validateFn, context, self, isRevalidation == true)
    if not okCall then
        return false, "Security.Validate raised an error: " .. tostring(resultA)
    end

    if type(resultA) == "table" then
        local status = string.lower(tostring(resultA.status or ""))
        if resultA.valid == true or resultA.ok == true or resultA.success == true or resultA.allowed == true
            or status == "valid" or status == "ok" or status == "success" or status == "allowed" or status == "authorized" then
            return true, resultA
        end
        return false, tostring(resultA.message or resultA.error or resultA.reason or "Security callback rejected the API key.")
    end

    if resultA == true then
        return true, resultB
    end

    return false, tostring(resultB or resultA or "Security callback rejected the API key.")
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_InterpretSecurityResponse(responsePayload, statusCode, context, isRevalidation)
    local securityConfig = self._securityConfig or {}

    if type(securityConfig.IsValidResponse) == "function" then
        local okCall, customValid, customReason = pcall(securityConfig.IsValidResponse, responsePayload, statusCode, context, self, isRevalidation == true)
        if not okCall then
            return false, "Security response parser raised an error: " .. tostring(customValid)
        end
        if customValid == true then
            return true, responsePayload
        end
        return false, tostring(customReason or "Validation endpoint rejected the API key.")
    end

    if responsePayload == nil and statusCode >= 200 and statusCode < 300 then
        return true, nil
    end

    if type(responsePayload) == "table" then
        local status = string.lower(tostring(responsePayload.status or responsePayload.result or ""))
        if status == "version_mismatch" then
            local minVersion = tostring(responsePayload.min_client_version or responsePayload.min_version or "")
            local productName = tostring(responsePayload.client_product or responsePayload.product or context.Product or "ui_v2")
            if minVersion ~= "" then
                return false, "Client update required for " .. productName .. " (minimum " .. minVersion .. ")."
            end
            return false, "Client update required for " .. productName .. "."
        end
        if responsePayload.valid == true or responsePayload.ok == true or responsePayload.success == true or responsePayload.allowed == true
            or status == "valid" or status == "ok" or status == "success" or status == "allowed" or status == "authorized" then
            return true, responsePayload
        end
        return false, tostring(responsePayload.message or responsePayload.error or responsePayload.reason or ("API key rejected (HTTP " .. tostring(statusCode or 0) .. ")."))
    end

    if responsePayload == true then
        return true, responsePayload
    end

    if type(responsePayload) == "string" then
        local normalized = string.lower(responsePayload)
        if normalized == "ok" or normalized == "valid" or normalized == "authorized" then
            return true, responsePayload
        end
        return false, responsePayload
    end

    if statusCode >= 200 and statusCode < 300 then
        return true, responsePayload
    end

    return false, "Validation endpoint rejected the API key."
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ValidateSecurityAccess(isRevalidation)
    local securityConfig = self._securityConfig or {}
    if securityConfig.Required ~= true then
        return true, nil
    end

    local targetUrl = isRevalidation and securityConfig.HeartbeatUrl or securityConfig.ValidationUrl
    if isRevalidation == true and targetUrl == securityConfig.HeartbeatUrl and not (self._securityState and self._securityState.KeyHash) then
        targetUrl = securityConfig.ValidationUrl
    end
    local requestRequired = type(targetUrl) == "string" and targetUrl ~= ""
    local environmentOk, environmentErr = verifyNexusUISecurityEnvironment(requestRequired)
    if not environmentOk then
        return false, environmentErr
    end

    local context = self:_BuildSecurityContext(isRevalidation)
    if context.ApiKey == "" then
        return false, "Missing required API key. Set config.Security.ApiKey or config.ApiKey before creating the UI."
    end

    local callbackOk, callbackResult = self:_RunSecurityCallback(context, isRevalidation)
    if not callbackOk then
        return false, callbackResult
    end

    if type(targetUrl) ~= "string" or targetUrl == "" then
        if type((securityConfig or {}).Validate) == "function" then
            self._securityState = self._securityState or {}
            self._securityState.Validated = true
            self._securityState.LastValidatedAt = tick()
            self._securityState.LastResponse = callbackResult
            self._securityState.ApiKeyFingerprint = hashNexusUIText(context.ApiKey)
            self._securityState.Hwid = context.Hwid
            self._securityState.KeyHash = type(callbackResult) == "table" and callbackResult.key_hash or self._securityState.KeyHash
            return true, callbackResult
        end
        return false, "Security.Validate or Security.ValidationUrl is required when API-key security is enabled."
    end

    local requestFn = getNexusUIRequestFunction()
    if type(requestFn) ~= "function" then
        return false, "Executor request API is unavailable."
    end

    local requestUrl = targetUrl
    if securityConfig.SendApiKeyAsQuery == true then
        requestUrl = appendNexusUIQueryValue(requestUrl, securityConfig.QueryKeyName, context.ApiKey)
    end

    local payload = cloneNexusUITable(securityConfig.Payload)
    local cachedKeyHash = self._securityState and self._securityState.KeyHash or nil
    payload.api_key = resolveFirstDefined(payload.api_key, context.ApiKey)
    payload.key = resolveFirstDefined(payload.key, (isRevalidation == true and cachedKeyHash and targetUrl == securityConfig.HeartbeatUrl) and nil or context.ApiKey)
    payload.key_hash = resolveFirstDefined(payload.key_hash, (isRevalidation == true and cachedKeyHash) or nil)
    payload.hwid = resolveFirstDefined(payload.hwid, context.Hwid)
    payload.client_product = resolveFirstDefined(payload.client_product, context.Product)
    payload.script_id = resolveFirstDefined(payload.script_id, context.ScriptId)
    payload.client_version = resolveFirstDefined(payload.client_version, context.ClientVersion)
    payload.timestamp = resolveFirstDefined(payload.timestamp, context.Timestamp)
    payload.nonce = resolveFirstDefined(payload.nonce, context.Nonce)
    payload.signature = resolveFirstDefined(payload.signature, context.Signature)

    if type(securityConfig.BuildPayload) == "function" then
        local okBuild, builtPayload = pcall(securityConfig.BuildPayload, payload, context, self, isRevalidation == true)
        if not okBuild then
            return false, "Security.BuildPayload raised an error: " .. tostring(builtPayload)
        end
        if type(builtPayload) == "table" then
            payload = builtPayload
        end
    end

    local headers = {
        ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
    }
    mergeIntoNexusUITable(headers, securityConfig.Headers)
    if securityConfig.SendApiKeyAsQuery ~= true and headers[securityConfig.ApiKeyHeader] == nil then
        headers[securityConfig.ApiKeyHeader] = context.ApiKey
    end

    local requestOptions = {
        Url = requestUrl,
        Method = securityConfig.Method or "POST",
        Headers = headers,
    }

    if requestOptions.Method ~= "GET" then
        requestOptions.Body = httpServiceNoCap:JSONEncode(payload)
    end

    local okRequest, response = pcall(function()
        return requestFn(requestOptions)
    end)

    if not okRequest or type(response) ~= "table" then
        if isRevalidation == true and securityConfig.AllowOffline == true then
            return true, callbackResult
        end
        return false, "Failed to reach API-key validation endpoint."
    end

    local statusCode = tonumber(response.StatusCode or response.Status or response.Code or 0) or 0
    local responsePayload = response.Body or response.body or response.ResponseBody or response.responseBody

    if type(responsePayload) == "string" and responsePayload ~= "" then
        local okDecode, decoded = pcall(function()
            return httpServiceNoCap:JSONDecode(responsePayload)
        end)
        responsePayload = okDecode and decoded or responsePayload
    elseif responsePayload == nil then
        responsePayload = response
    end

    local responseOk, responseReason = self:_InterpretSecurityResponse(responsePayload, statusCode, context, isRevalidation)
    if not responseOk then
        return false, responseReason
    end

    self._securityState = self._securityState or {}
    self._securityState.Validated = true
    self._securityState.LastValidatedAt = tick()
    self._securityState.LastResponse = responsePayload
    self._securityState.ApiKeyFingerprint = hashNexusUIText(context.ApiKey)
    self._securityState.Hwid = context.Hwid
    self._securityState.Product = context.Product
    self._securityState.KeyHash = type(responsePayload) == "table" and responsePayload.key_hash or self._securityState.KeyHash

    local sharedEnv = getNexusUISharedEnvironment()
    local sharedState = {
        validated = true,
        fingerprint = self._securityState.ApiKeyFingerprint,
        product = context.Product,
        validatedAt = self._securityState.LastValidatedAt,
        keyHash = self._securityState.KeyHash,
    }
    rawset(sharedEnv, NexusUISecurityStateKey, sharedState)
    rawset(sharedEnv, LegacyNexusUISecurityStateKey, sharedState)

    return true, responsePayload
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_HandleSecurityFailure(reason)
    if self._securityFailureHandled or self._destroyed then
        return
    end

    self._securityFailureHandled = true
    self._securityMonitorAlive = false

    local message = tostring(reason or "Security validation failed.")
    warn("[NexusUI] " .. message)
    local sharedEnv = getNexusUISharedEnvironment()
    rawset(sharedEnv, NexusUISecurityErrorKey, message)
    rawset(sharedEnv, LegacyNexusUISecurityErrorKey, message)

    pcall(function()
        self:Destroy()
    end)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_StartSecurityMonitor()
    local securityConfig = self._securityConfig or {}
    if securityConfig.Required ~= true or self._securityMonitorAlive then
        return
    end

    self._securityMonitorAlive = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while self._securityMonitorAlive and not self._destroyed do
            task.wait(5)
            if not self._securityMonitorAlive or self._destroyed then
                break
            end

            local verifyOk, verifyErr = verifyNexusUISecurityEnvironment(type(securityConfig.HeartbeatUrl) == "string" and securityConfig.HeartbeatUrl ~= "")
            if not verifyOk then
                self:_HandleSecurityFailure(verifyErr or "Environment integrity check failed.")
                break
            end

            local lastValidatedAt = self._securityState and tonumber(self._securityState.LastValidatedAt) or 0
            if lastValidatedAt > 0 and (tick() - lastValidatedAt) >= (securityConfig.RevalidateInterval or 180) then
                local revalidateOk, revalidateErr = self:_ValidateSecurityAccess(true)
                if not revalidateOk then
                    self:_HandleSecurityFailure(revalidateErr or "API-key revalidation failed.")
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SetWatermarkStatus(text, color)
    self._watermarkStatusText = tostring(text or "READY")
    self._watermarkStatusColor = color or self.config.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(2, 133, 255)

    if self.watermarkStatusLabel and self.watermarkStatusLabel.Parent then
        self.watermarkStatusLabel.Text = self._watermarkStatusText
        self.watermarkStatusLabel.TextColor3 = self._watermarkStatusColor
    end

    if self.watermarkStatusFrame and self.watermarkStatusFrame.Parent then
        self.watermarkStatusFrame.BackgroundColor3 = self._watermarkStatusColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(9, 9, 10), 0.72)
    end

    if self.watermarkPulseDot and self.watermarkPulseDot.Parent then
        self.watermarkPulseDot.BackgroundColor3 = self._watermarkStatusColor
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SetDynamicIslandNotificationCount(count)
    local normalizedCount = math.max(0, math.floor((tonumber(count) or 0) + 0.5))
    self._dynamicIslandNotificationCount = normalizedCount

    if self.watermarkNotificationBadge and self.watermarkNotificationBadge.Parent then
        self.watermarkNotificationBadge.Visible = normalizedCount > 0
        self.watermarkNotificationBadge.BackgroundColor3 = self.config.AccentColor
    end

    if self.watermarkNotificationBadgeLabel and self.watermarkNotificationBadgeLabel.Parent then
        if normalizedCount > 99 then
            self.watermarkNotificationBadgeLabel.Text = "99+"
        else
            self.watermarkNotificationBadgeLabel.Text = tostring(normalizedCount)
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_RefreshNotificationHolderPosition()
    if not (self.notificationHolderFrame and self.notificationHolderFrame.Parent) then
        return
    end

    local xOffset = 0
    local yOffset = math.floor(68 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5)
    if self.watermarkFrame and self.watermarkFrame.Parent then
        local absolutePosition = self.watermarkFrame.AbsolutePosition
        local absoluteSize = self.watermarkFrame.AbsoluteSize
        xOffset = math.floor(absolutePosition.X + (absoluteSize.X * 0.5) + 0.5)
        yOffset = math.floor(absolutePosition.Y + absoluteSize.Y + (12 * mobileScaleFactor) + 0.5)
        self.notificationHolderFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, xOffset, 0, yOffset)
        return
    end

    self.notificationHolderFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, yOffset)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_TrackConnection(conn)
    if conn then
        table.insert(self._connections, conn)
        if #self._connections % 100 == 0 then
            local activeConnections = {}
            for _, item in ipairs(self._connections) do
                if item then
                    if typeof(item) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                        if item.Connected then
                            table.insert(activeConnections, item)
                        end
                    else
                        table.insert(activeConnections, item)
                    end
                end
            end
            self._connections = activeConnections
        end
    end
    return conn
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:RegisterControl(flag, getter, setter)
    if type(flag) ~= "string" or flag == "" then return end
    if type(getter) ~= "function" or type(setter) ~= "function" then return end
    self._trackedControls[flag] = {
        get = getter,
        set = setter
    }
    self._lastControlRegistration = os.clock()
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_RegisterRefreshJob(interval, isAliveFn, stepFn)
    if type(stepFn) ~= "function" then
        return nil
    end

    local job = {
        Interval = math.max(tonumber(interval) or 0.5, 0.05),
        IsAlive = isAliveFn,
        Step = stepFn,
        Accumulator = 0
    }

    table.insert(self._refreshJobs, job)
    return job
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_StepRefreshJobs(dt)
    if not self._refreshJobs then
        return
    end

    local resolvedDt = tonumber(dt) or 0
    for index = #self._refreshJobs, 1, -1 do
        local job = self._refreshJobs[index]
        local keepJob = true

        if type(job) ~= "table" or type(job.Step) ~= "function" then
            keepJob = false
        elseif type(job.IsAlive) == "function" then
            local okAlive, isAlive = pcall(job.IsAlive)
            keepJob = okAlive and isAlive ~= false
        end

        if not keepJob then
            table.remove(self._refreshJobs, index)
        else
            job.Accumulator = (job.Accumulator or 0) + resolvedDt
            if job.Accumulator >= (job.Interval or 0.5) then
                job.Accumulator = 0
                local okStep, keepResult = pcall(job.Step)
                if not okStep or keepResult == false then
                    table.remove(self._refreshJobs, index)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_BuildConfigPayload()
    local payload = {
        version = 3,
        ui = self.config.Name,
        controls = {},
        visuals = {
            accentColor = serializeConfigValue(self.config.AccentColor),
            blurEnabled = self._uiVisualSettings.Blur == true,
            overlayEnabled = self._uiVisualSettings.Snow == true,
            overlayMode = self._overlayMode,
            overlayPhysics = self._overlayPhysicsEnabled == true,
            backgroundEffects = self._uiVisualSettings.BackgroundEffects == true,
            backgroundOverlayOpacity = self._backgroundOverlayOpacity,
            backgroundImageSource = self.config.BackgroundImageSource,
            backgroundImageTransparency = self.config.BackgroundImageTransparency,
            textGradient = self._uiVisualSettings.TextGradient == true,
            espPreview = self._uiVisualSettings.ESPSelfPreview == true,
            hideName = self._uiVisualSettings.HideName == true,
            fontPresetIndex = tonumber(self._fontPresetIndex) or 1,
            autoSaveConfig = self._autoConfigEnabled == true
        }
    }

    local trackedFlags = {}
    for flag in pairs(self._trackedControls) do
        table.insert(trackedFlags, flag)
    end
    table.sort(trackedFlags)

    for _, flag in ipairs(trackedFlags) do
        local control = self._trackedControls[flag]
        if control and type(control.get) == "function" then
            local ok, value = pcall(control.get)
            if ok then
                payload.controls[flag] = serializeConfigValue(value)
            end
        end
    end

    return payload
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_BuildAutoConfigSnapshot()
    local payload = self:_BuildConfigPayload()
    local okEncode, encoded = pcall(function()
        return httpServiceNoCap:JSONEncode(payload)
    end)

    if okEncode then
        return encoded
    end

    return nil
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_TryAutoSaveConfig(forceSave)
    if self._destroyed or not self._autoConfigEnabled or self._isApplyingConfig then
        return false
    end

    if not writefile or next(self._trackedControls) == nil then
        return false
    end

    local snapshot = self:_BuildAutoConfigSnapshot()
    if not snapshot then
        return false
    end

    if not forceSave and snapshot == self._autoConfigSnapshot then
        return false
    end

    local okSave = self:SaveConfig(self._autoConfigName)
    if okSave then
        self._autoConfigSnapshot = snapshot
    end

    return okSave == true
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_TryAutoLoadConfig(forceAttempt)
    if self._destroyed then
        return false
    end

    if self._autoConfigLoadAttempted and not forceAttempt then
        return false
    end

    if not readfile or next(self._trackedControls) == nil then
        return false
    end

    self._isApplyingConfig = true
    local okLoad = self:LoadConfig(self._autoConfigName)
    self._isApplyingConfig = false
    self._autoConfigLoadAttempted = true
    self._autoConfigAccumulator = 0
    self._autoConfigSnapshot = self:_BuildAutoConfigSnapshot()
    return okLoad == true
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_RefreshViewportMetrics()
    local viewportTarget = nil
    if self.snowLayerFrame and self.snowLayerFrame.Parent then
        viewportTarget = self.snowLayerFrame
    elseif self.mainFrameYay and self.mainFrameYay.Parent then
        viewportTarget = self.mainFrameYay
    else
        viewportTarget = self.screenGuiYay
    end
    local viewportSize = (viewportTarget and viewportTarget.AbsoluteSize) or Vector2.new(0, 0)
    local width = math.max(200, math.floor(viewportSize.X > 0 and viewportSize.X or 1280))
    local height = math.max(320, math.floor(viewportSize.Y > 0 and viewportSize.Y or 720))
    self._cachedViewportSize = Vector2.new(width, height)
    self._cachedViewportWidth = width
    self._cachedViewportHeight = height
    self._cachedViewportAreaScale = math.clamp(math.sqrt((width * height) / (1280 * 720)), isMobileUserSadly and 0.52 or 0.68, 1.12)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_InvalidateESPPreviewProjection(clearCache)
    self._espPreviewProjectionDirty = true
    if clearCache then
        self._espPreviewProjectionCache = nil
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_EnsureBlurEffect()
    if self._blurEffectRef and self._blurEffectRef.Parent then
        return self._blurEffectRef
    end
    local existingBlur = lightingServiceLooksClean:FindFirstChild("NexusUIBlurEffect")
    if existingBlur and existingBlur:IsA("BlurEffect") then
        self._blurEffectRef = existingBlur
        return existingBlur
    end
    self._blurEffectRef = makeThingExist("BlurEffect", {
        Name = "NexusUIBlurEffect",
        Size = 0,
        Enabled = true,
        Parent = lightingServiceLooksClean
    })
    return self._blurEffectRef
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SetBlurActive(active, instant)
    local blur = self:_EnsureBlurEffect()
    local shouldEnable = self._uiVisualSettings.Blur and active
    local targetSize = shouldEnable and (isMobileUserSadly and 14 or 18) or 0
    if instant then
        blur.Size = targetSize
        return
    end
    smoothMoveGoWoosh(blur, {Size = targetSize}, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ClearSnowflakes()
    for i = #self._snowflakes, 1, -1 do
        local snowObj = self._snowflakes[i]
        if snowObj and snowObj.instance and snowObj.instance.Parent then
            snowObj.instance:Destroy()
        end
        self._snowflakes[i] = nil
    end
end

local function lerpNumber(fromValue, toValue, alpha)
    return fromValue + (toValue - fromValue) * alpha
end

resolveFirstDefined = function(...)
    for index = 1, select("#", ...) do
        local value = select(index, ...)
        if value ~= nil then
            return value
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function getSnowSegmentCount(targetWidth, compact)
    local resolvedWidth = math.max(tonumber(targetWidth) or 0, (compact and 72 or 120) * mobileScaleFactor)
    local divisor = math.max((compact and 28 or 24) * mobileScaleFactor, 1)
    return math.clamp(math.floor((resolvedWidth / divisor) + 0.5), compact and 4 or 5, compact and 7 or 9)
end

local function getSnowRenderBlobCount(targetWidth, compact)
    local resolvedWidth = math.max(tonumber(targetWidth) or 0, (compact and 72 or 120) * mobileScaleFactor)
    local divisor = math.max((compact and 58 or 46) * mobileScaleFactor, 1)
    return math.clamp(math.floor((resolvedWidth / divisor) + 0.5), compact and 2 or 3, compact and 4 or 6)
end

local function getSnowRenderDripCount(blobCount, compact)
    local resolvedBlobCount = math.max(math.floor(tonumber(blobCount) or 0), 0)
    if resolvedBlobCount <= 0 then
        return 0
    end
    return math.min(resolvedBlobCount, compact and 1 or 2)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_EnsureSnowTargetProfile(entry, targetWidth)
    if type(entry) ~= "table" or not entry.CapContainer or not entry.CapContainer.Parent then
        return 0
    end

    local desiredCount = getSnowSegmentCount(targetWidth, entry.Compact == true)
    local desiredBlobCount = getSnowRenderBlobCount(targetWidth, entry.Compact == true)
    local desiredDripCount = getSnowRenderDripCount(desiredBlobCount, entry.Compact == true)
    entry.SegmentCount = desiredCount
    entry.RenderBlobCount = desiredBlobCount
    entry.RenderDripCount = desiredDripCount
    entry.MassProfile = entry.MassProfile or {}
    entry.DisplayMassProfile = entry.DisplayMassProfile or {}
    entry.SegmentFrames = entry.SegmentFrames or {}
    entry.IcicleFrames = entry.IcicleFrames or {}

    local segmentZIndex = (entry.Overlay and entry.Overlay.ZIndex or 2) + 3
    local icicleZIndex = (entry.Overlay and entry.Overlay.ZIndex or 2) + 2

    for index = 1, desiredCount do
        if entry.MassProfile[index] == nil then
            entry.MassProfile[index] = 0
        end
        if entry.DisplayMassProfile[index] == nil then
            entry.DisplayMassProfile[index] = entry.MassProfile[index]
        end
    end

    for index = 1, desiredBlobCount do
        if not entry.SegmentFrames[index] or not entry.SegmentFrames[index].Parent then
            local segmentFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
                Name = "NexusSnowBlob" .. index,
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(236, 247, 255),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
                Visible = false,
                ZIndex = segmentZIndex,
                Parent = entry.CapContainer
            })
            makeThingExist("UICorner", {
                CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0),
                Parent = segmentFrame
            })
            makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
                Rotation = 90,
                Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(245, 250, 255)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(186, 224, 255))
                }),
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.06),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0.18),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.34)
                }),
                Parent = segmentFrame
            })
            entry.SegmentFrames[index] = segmentFrame
        end
    end

    for index = 1, desiredDripCount do
        if not entry.IcicleFrames[index] or not entry.IcicleFrames[index].Parent then
            local icicleFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
                Name = "NexusSnowDrip" .. index,
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(194, 228, 255),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
                Visible = false,
                ZIndex = icicleZIndex,
                Parent = entry.CapContainer
            })
            makeThingExist("UICorner", {
                CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0),
                Parent = icicleFrame
            })
            makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
                Rotation = 90,
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, 0.3),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
                }),
                Parent = icicleFrame
            })
            entry.IcicleFrames[index] = icicleFrame
        end
    end

    for index = #entry.SegmentFrames, desiredBlobCount + 1, -1 do
        if entry.SegmentFrames[index] and entry.SegmentFrames[index].Parent then
            entry.SegmentFrames[index]:Destroy()
        end
        entry.SegmentFrames[index] = nil
    end
    for index = #entry.IcicleFrames, desiredDripCount + 1, -1 do
        if entry.IcicleFrames[index] and entry.IcicleFrames[index].Parent then
            entry.IcicleFrames[index]:Destroy()
        end
        entry.IcicleFrames[index] = nil
    end
    for index = #entry.MassProfile, desiredCount + 1, -1 do
        entry.MassProfile[index] = nil
    end
    for index = #entry.DisplayMassProfile, desiredCount + 1, -1 do
        entry.DisplayMassProfile[index] = nil
    end

    return desiredCount
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SpreadSnowMassProfile(entry, dt, preserveDeposits)
    if type(entry) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    local profile = entry.MassProfile
    local maxPileMass = math.max(tonumber(entry.MaxPileMass) or (entry.Compact and 0.58 or 0.92), 0.12)
    local segmentCount = entry.SegmentCount or (type(profile) == "table" and #profile or 0)
    if type(profile) ~= "table" or segmentCount <= 0 then
        entry.Deposit = math.max((tonumber(entry.Deposit) or 0) - dt * (preserveDeposits and 0.05 or 0.28), 0)
        return
    end

    local nextProfile = table.create and table.create(segmentCount, 0) or {}
    local settleAlpha = math.clamp((tonumber(dt) or 0) * (preserveDeposits and 7.4 or 4.2), 0, 1)
    local meltRate = preserveDeposits and (entry.Compact and 0.012 or 0.009) or (entry.Compact and 0.16 or 0.13)
    local totalMass = 0

    for index = 1, segmentCount do
        local currentMass = profile[index] or 0
        local leftMass = profile[index - 1] or currentMass
        local rightMass = profile[index + 1] or currentMass
        local settledMass = currentMass * 0.58 + leftMass * 0.21 + rightMass * 0.21
        local cooledMass = math.clamp(lerpNumber(currentMass, settledMass, settleAlpha) - ((tonumber(dt) or 0) * meltRate), 0, maxPileMass)
        nextProfile[index] = cooledMass
        totalMass = totalMass + cooledMass
    end

    entry.MassProfile = nextProfile
    entry.DisplayMassProfile = entry.DisplayMassProfile or {}

    local displayAlpha = math.clamp((tonumber(dt) or 0) * (preserveDeposits and 11.5 or 7.2), 0, 1)
    for index = 1, segmentCount do
        local currentDisplayMass = entry.DisplayMassProfile[index]
        if currentDisplayMass == nil then
            currentDisplayMass = nextProfile[index]
        end
        entry.DisplayMassProfile[index] = lerpNumber(currentDisplayMass, nextProfile[index], displayAlpha)
    end

    local averageMass = segmentCount > 0 and (totalMass / segmentCount) or 0
    entry.Deposit = math.clamp(
        lerpNumber(tonumber(entry.Deposit) or 0, averageMass, math.clamp((tonumber(dt) or 0) * 8.5, 0, 1)),
        0,
        maxPileMass * 1.35
    )
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_InjectSnowMassIntoTarget(entry, normalizedX, amount)
    if type(entry) ~= "table" or not entry.Target or not entry.Target.Parent then
        return
    end

    local segmentCount = self:_EnsureSnowTargetProfile(entry, entry.Target.AbsoluteSize.X)
    if segmentCount <= 0 then
        return
    end

    local centerIndex = math.clamp(math.floor((math.clamp(tonumber(normalizedX) or 0.5, 0, 1) * (segmentCount - 1)) + 1.5), 1, segmentCount)
    local depositAmount = math.clamp(tonumber(amount) or (entry.Compact and 0.085 or 0.072), 0.01, 0.36)
    local pileCap = math.max(tonumber(entry.MaxPileMass) or (entry.Compact and 0.58 or 0.92), 0.12)
    local falloff = {
        [0] = 1,
        [1] = 0.58,
        [2] = 0.22,
    }

    for offset = -2, 2 do
        local segmentIndex = centerIndex + offset
        local segmentWeight = falloff[math.abs(offset)]
        if segmentWeight and entry.MassProfile[segmentIndex] ~= nil then
            entry.MassProfile[segmentIndex] = math.clamp(
                (entry.MassProfile[segmentIndex] or 0) + depositAmount * segmentWeight * (entry.Compact and 0.94 or 1.04),
                0,
                pileCap
            )
        end
    end

    entry.Deposit = math.clamp((tonumber(entry.Deposit) or 0) + depositAmount * 1.65, 0, pileCap * 1.35)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SpawnIceShard(startX, startY, options)
    if not self.snowLayerFrame or not self.snowLayerFrame.Parent then
        return
    end

    options = options or {}
    local shardWidth = math.max(2 * mobileScaleFactor, tonumber(options.Width) or (math.random(4, 8) * mobileScaleFactor))
    local shardHeight = math.max(shardWidth * 1.4, tonumber(options.Height) or (math.random(8, 18) * mobileScaleFactor))
    local shardX = tonumber(startX) or 0
    local shardY = tonumber(startY) or 0
    local shard = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(198 + math.random(0, 18), 228 + math.random(0, 18), 255),
        BackgroundTransparency = math.random(16, 30) / 100,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, shardX, 0, shardY),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, shardWidth, 0, shardHeight),
        Rotation = math.random(-18, 18),
        ZIndex = 12,
        Parent = self.snowLayerFrame
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {
        CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0),
        Parent = shard
    })
    makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 90,
        Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.12),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 0.26),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        }),
        Parent = shard
    })

    table.insert(self._snowflakes, {
        overlayType = "SnowShard",
        instance = shard,
        xOffset = shardX,
        yOffset = shardY,
        velocityX = tonumber(options.VelocityX) or (math.random(-42, 42) * mobileScaleFactor),
        velocityY = tonumber(options.VelocityY) or (math.random(34, 76) * mobileScaleFactor),
        gravity = tonumber(options.Gravity) or (math.random(180, 260) * mobileScaleFactor),
        drag = tonumber(options.Drag) or 1.8,
        rotationSpeed = tonumber(options.RotationSpeed) or math.random(-110, 110),
        width = shardWidth,
        height = shardHeight,
        baseTransparency = shard.BackgroundTransparency,
        depositAmount = tonumber(options.DepositAmount) or (math.random(16, 26) / 100),
    })
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_RegisterSnowFrostTarget(target, options)
    if not target or not target:IsA("GuiObject") then
        return nil
    end

    self._snowFrostTargets = self._snowFrostTargets or {}
    local existingEntry = self._snowFrostTargets[target]
    if existingEntry then
        return existingEntry
    end

    options = options or {}
    local overlayZIndex = math.max((target.ZIndex or 1) + 1, 2)
    local overlayFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        Name = "NexusSnowFrostTarget",
        Active = false,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = overlayZIndex,
        Parent = target
    })
    local targetCorner = target:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
    if targetCorner then
        makeThingExist("UICorner", {
            CornerRadius = targetCorner.CornerRadius,
            Parent = overlayFrame
        })
    end

    local topFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(236, 247, 255),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        ZIndex = overlayZIndex + 1,
        Parent = overlayFrame
    })
    if targetCorner then
        makeThingExist("UICorner", {
            CornerRadius = targetCorner.CornerRadius,
            Parent = topFrame
        })
    end
    makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 90,
        Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.02),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.28, 0.12),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.62, 0.38),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        }),
        Parent = topFrame
    })

    local glazeFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(162, 206, 255),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        ZIndex = overlayZIndex + 2,
        Parent = overlayFrame
    })
    if targetCorner then
        makeThingExist("UICorner", {
            CornerRadius = targetCorner.CornerRadius,
            Parent = glazeFrame
        })
    end
    makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 90,
        Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.08),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 0.3),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        }),
        Parent = glazeFrame
    })

    local capContainer = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22 * mobileScaleFactor),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        ZIndex = overlayZIndex + 3,
        Parent = overlayFrame
    })
    if targetCorner then
        makeThingExist("UICorner", {
            CornerRadius = targetCorner.CornerRadius,
            Parent = capContainer
        })
    end

    local entry = {
        Target = target,
        Overlay = overlayFrame,
        Top = topFrame,
        Glaze = glazeFrame,
        CapContainer = capContainer,
        Compact = options.Compact == true,
        Weight = math.clamp(tonumber(options.Weight) or 1, 0.2, 1.4),
        CatchSnow = options.CatchSnow ~= false,
        CatchInsetX = math.max(tonumber(options.CatchInsetX) or (8 * mobileScaleFactor), 0),
        CatchOffsetY = tonumber(options.CatchOffsetY) or 0,
        Deposit = 0,
        CapHeight = 0,
        MaxPileMass = math.max(tonumber(options.MaxPileMass or options.MaxPile) or (options.Compact and 0.58 or 0.92), 0.12),
        Specks = {},
        MaxSpecks = math.floor((options.MaxSpecks or (options.Compact and 4 or 8)) + 0.5),
        SegmentFrames = {},
        IcicleFrames = {},
        MassProfile = {},
        DisplayMassProfile = {},
        SegmentCount = 0,
        RenderBlobCount = 0,
        RenderDripCount = 0,
        LastAbsolutePosition = target.AbsolutePosition,
        LastAbsoluteSize = target.AbsoluteSize,
        MotionAccumulator = 0,
        ProfilePhase = math.random() * math.pi * 2,
        ShardCooldown = 0
    }

    self._snowFrostTargets[target] = entry
    self:_EnsureSnowTargetProfile(entry, target.AbsoluteSize.X)
    self:_UpdateSnowFrostTargets(true)
    return entry
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_StepSnowFrostTargets(dt)
    if type(self._snowFrostTargets) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    local resolvedDt = math.max(tonumber(dt) or 0, 0)
    local preserveDeposits = self._overlayPhysicsEnabled and self.isToggledOn and self._uiVisualSettings.Snow and self._overlayMode == "Snow"

    for target, entry in pairs(self._snowFrostTargets) do
        if not target or not target.Parent or type(entry) ~= "table" then
            self._snowFrostTargets[target] = nil
        else
            local isVisible = isNexusUIGuiObjectActuallyVisible(target)
            local currentAbsolutePosition = target.AbsolutePosition
            local currentAbsoluteSize = target.AbsoluteSize
            local previousAbsolutePosition = entry.LastAbsolutePosition or currentAbsolutePosition
            local previousAbsoluteSize = entry.LastAbsoluteSize or currentAbsoluteSize
            local deltaX = currentAbsolutePosition.X - previousAbsolutePosition.X
            local deltaY = currentAbsolutePosition.Y - previousAbsolutePosition.Y
            local deltaSizeX = currentAbsoluteSize.X - previousAbsoluteSize.X
            local deltaSizeY = currentAbsoluteSize.Y - previousAbsoluteSize.Y
            local motionDistance = math.sqrt(deltaX * deltaX + deltaY * deltaY)
            local resizeDistance = math.sqrt(deltaSizeX * deltaSizeX + deltaSizeY * deltaSizeY)
            local allowSurfacePhysics = preserveDeposits and isVisible

            entry.LastAbsolutePosition = currentAbsolutePosition
            entry.LastAbsoluteSize = currentAbsoluteSize
            entry.ShardCooldown = math.max((tonumber(entry.ShardCooldown) or 0) - resolvedDt, 0)

            if allowSurfacePhysics then
                local motionEnergy = motionDistance + resizeDistance * 0.75
                if motionEnergy > 0.18 then
                    entry.MotionAccumulator = (tonumber(entry.MotionAccumulator) or 0) + motionEnergy
                    local threshold = entry.Compact and 5.5 or 7.25
                    if entry.MotionAccumulator >= threshold then
                        local velocityScale = 1 / math.max(resolvedDt, 1 / 120)
                        self:_ShakeSnowOffTarget(entry, math.clamp(entry.MotionAccumulator / (entry.Compact and 26 or 34), 0.08, 0.62), {
                            MotionX = deltaX * velocityScale,
                            MotionY = deltaY * velocityScale,
                            ResizeX = deltaSizeX,
                            ResizeY = deltaSizeY
                        })
                        entry.MotionAccumulator = 0
                    end
                else
                    entry.MotionAccumulator = math.max((tonumber(entry.MotionAccumulator) or 0) - resolvedDt * 12, 0)
                end
            else
                entry.MotionAccumulator = 0
            end

            self:_EnsureSnowTargetProfile(entry, currentAbsoluteSize.X)
            self:_SpreadSnowMassProfile(entry, resolvedDt, allowSurfacePhysics)

            if not allowSurfacePhysics and type(entry.Specks) == "table" and #entry.Specks > 0 then
                local purgeCount = math.max(1, math.floor(#entry.Specks * math.clamp(resolvedDt * 3.5, 0.08, 1)))
                for _ = 1, purgeCount do
                    if #entry.Specks <= 0 then
                        break
                    end
                    local speck = table.remove(entry.Specks, 1)
                    if speck and speck.Parent then
                        speck:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_UpdateSnowFrostTargets(instant)
    if self.snowFrostOverlayFrame then
        self.snowFrostOverlayFrame.Visible = false
    end

    if type(self._snowFrostTargets) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    local globalIntensity = math.clamp(tonumber(self._snowFrostIntensity) or 0, 0, 1)
    local allowSnowFrostTargets = self._overlayPhysicsEnabled and self.isToggledOn and self._uiVisualSettings.Snow and self._overlayMode == "Snow"

    for target, entry in pairs(self._snowFrostTargets) do
        if not target or not target.Parent or type(entry) ~= "table" then
            self._snowFrostTargets[target] = nil
        else
            local targetSize = target.AbsoluteSize
            local targetVisible = isNexusUIGuiObjectActuallyVisible(target)
            local segmentCount = self:_EnsureSnowTargetProfile(entry, targetSize.X)
            local displayProfile = entry.DisplayMassProfile or entry.MassProfile or {}
            local profileAverage = 0
            local profilePeak = 0
            for index = 1, segmentCount do
                local segmentMass = displayProfile[index] or 0
                profileAverage = profileAverage + segmentMass
                if segmentMass > profilePeak then
                    profilePeak = segmentMass
                end
            end
            if segmentCount > 0 then
                profileAverage = profileAverage / segmentCount
            end

            local baselineMass = math.clamp(globalIntensity * (entry.Weight or 1) * (entry.Compact and 0.24 or 0.3), 0, 0.36)
            local localIntensity = math.clamp(baselineMass + profileAverage * 0.92 + (entry.Deposit or 0) * 0.3, 0, 1.28)
            local showTargetFrost = allowSnowFrostTargets and localIntensity > 0.015 and targetVisible and targetSize.X > 0 and targetSize.Y > 0

            if not showTargetFrost then
                if entry.Overlay then
                    entry.Overlay.Visible = false
                end
                if type(entry.SegmentFrames) == "table" then
                    for index = 1, #entry.SegmentFrames do
                        local segmentFrame = entry.SegmentFrames[index]
                        if segmentFrame then
                            segmentFrame.Visible = false
                        end
                    end
                end
                if type(entry.IcicleFrames) == "table" then
                    for index = 1, #entry.IcicleFrames do
                        local icicleFrame = entry.IcicleFrames[index]
                        if icicleFrame then
                            icicleFrame.Visible = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                entry.Overlay.Visible = true

                local compactScale = entry.Compact and 0.78 or 1
                local topHeight = math.max(2, math.floor((1.7 + ((baselineMass + profileAverage * 0.55) * 7.6 * compactScale)) * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5))
                local topTransparency = math.clamp(0.94 - (localIntensity * 0.38), 0.22, 0.95)
                local glazeHeight = math.max(topHeight + math.floor(4 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5), math.floor(6 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5))
                local maxIcicleHeight = math.min(
                    math.max(math.floor((baselineMass * 4 + profilePeak * (entry.Compact and 10 or 15)) * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5), math.floor(5 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5)),
                    math.max(6, math.floor(targetSize.Y * (entry.Compact and 0.24 or 0.36)))
                )
                local renderBlobCount = math.max(entry.RenderBlobCount or #entry.SegmentFrames, 1)
                local segmentWidth = targetSize.X / math.max(segmentCount, 1)
                local renderBlobWidth = targetSize.X / math.max(renderBlobCount, 1)
                local blobInfluenceRadius = math.max(0.16, (1 / math.max(renderBlobCount, 1)) * (entry.Compact and 1.18 or 1.34))
                local renderedPeakHeight = topHeight
                local blobRenderInfo = {}

                if entry.Top then
                    entry.Top.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, topHeight)
                    entry.Top.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(236, 247, 255)
                    entry.Top.BackgroundTransparency = topTransparency
                end
                if entry.Glaze then
                    entry.Glaze.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, glazeHeight)
                    entry.Glaze.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(0.985 - (localIntensity * 0.58), 0.32, 0.975)
                end

                for index = 1, renderBlobCount do
                    local normalizedX = renderBlobCount == 1 and 0.5 or ((index - 0.5) / renderBlobCount)
                    local ridgeBias = math.sin(normalizedX * math.pi)
                    ridgeBias = ridgeBias * ridgeBias
                    local wave = (math.sin((entry.ProfilePhase or 0) + normalizedX * math.pi * 2.4) + 1) * 0.5
                    local weightedMass = 0
                    local totalWeight = 0
                    local localPeak = 0

                    for profileIndex = 1, segmentCount do
                        local segmentCenter = segmentCount == 1 and 0.5 or ((profileIndex - 0.5) / segmentCount)
                        local distance = math.abs(segmentCenter - normalizedX)
                        local influence = math.max(0, 1 - ((distance / blobInfluenceRadius) ^ 2))
                        if influence > 0 then
                            local segmentMass = displayProfile[profileIndex] or 0
                            weightedMass = weightedMass + segmentMass * influence
                            totalWeight = totalWeight + influence
                            if segmentMass > localPeak then
                                localPeak = segmentMass
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    local blobMass = totalWeight > 0 and (weightedMass / totalWeight) or 0
                    local bandMass = math.clamp(blobMass + baselineMass * (0.94 + ridgeBias * 0.52) + wave * baselineMass * 0.16, 0, 1.3)
                    local segmentHeight = math.max(topHeight, math.floor((2.2 + bandMass * (entry.Compact and 9.5 or 14.5) + ridgeBias * 3.6) * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5))
                    local segmentFrame = entry.SegmentFrames[index]
                    local segmentPixelWidth = math.min(
                        targetSize.X + math.floor(10 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5),
                        math.max(
                            math.floor(renderBlobWidth * (entry.Compact and 1.55 or 1.85) + 0.5),
                            math.floor(renderBlobWidth + 8 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5)
                        ) + math.floor(bandMass * renderBlobWidth * (entry.Compact and 0.22 or 0.34) + 0.5)
                    )
                    local segmentCenterX = normalizedX * targetSize.X
                    local segmentX = math.floor(segmentCenterX - segmentPixelWidth * 0.5 + 0.5)
                    renderedPeakHeight = math.max(renderedPeakHeight, segmentHeight)

                    if segmentFrame then
                        segmentFrame.Visible = bandMass > 0.01
                        if segmentFrame.Visible then
                            segmentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, segmentX, 0, 0)
                            segmentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, segmentPixelWidth, 0, segmentHeight)
                            segmentFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(236, 247, 255):Lerp(
                                Color3.fromRGB(154, 207, 255),
                                math.clamp((bandMass * 0.22) + wave * 0.06, 0.04, 0.32)
                            )
                            segmentFrame.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(0.78 - (bandMass * 0.28), 0.14, 0.82)

                            local segmentGradient = segmentFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
                            if segmentGradient then
                                segmentGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(245, 250, 255)),
                                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.46, Color3.fromRGB(223, 240, 255)),
                                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(172, 215, 255))
                                })
                                segmentGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, math.clamp(0.04 + bandMass * 0.02, 0.04, 0.12)),
                                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.72, math.clamp(0.12 + bandMass * 0.06, 0.1, 0.3)),
                                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, math.clamp(0.28 + bandMass * 0.12, 0.22, 0.46))
                                })
                            end

                            blobRenderInfo[#blobRenderInfo + 1] = {
                                Strength = bandMass,
                                Height = segmentHeight,
                                Width = segmentPixelWidth,
                                CenterX = segmentCenterX,
                                Wave = wave,
                                Score = bandMass * (segmentHeight / math.max(1, topHeight))
                            }
                        end
                    end
                end

                table.sort(blobRenderInfo, function(left, right)
                    return (left.Score or 0) > (right.Score or 0)
                end)

                for index = 1, #(entry.IcicleFrames or {}) do
                    local icicleFrame = entry.IcicleFrames[index]
                    local sourceBlob = blobRenderInfo[index]
                    if icicleFrame and sourceBlob and sourceBlob.Strength > 0.16 then
                        local icicleHeight = math.min(
                            math.floor((sourceBlob.Strength * (entry.Compact and 9 or 13) * (0.6 + sourceBlob.Wave * 0.35)) * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5),
                            maxIcicleHeight
                        )
                        if icicleHeight > 0 then
                            local horizontalNudge = (index % 2 == 0 and -1 or 1) * sourceBlob.Width * 0.12
                            icicleFrame.Visible = true
                            icicleFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, math.floor(sourceBlob.CenterX + horizontalNudge + 0.5), 0, sourceBlob.Height - 1)
                            icicleFrame.Size = UDim2.new(
                                0,
                                math.max(2, math.floor(math.min(segmentWidth * (entry.Compact and 0.42 or 0.36), sourceBlob.Width * 0.16) + 0.5)),
                                0,
                                icicleHeight
                            )
                            icicleFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(194, 228, 255)
                            icicleFrame.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(0.68 - sourceBlob.Strength * 0.16, 0.18, 0.72)
                        else
                            icicleFrame.Visible = false
                        end
                    elseif icicleFrame then
                        icicleFrame.Visible = false
                    end
                end

                local capContainerHeight = math.max(
                    renderedPeakHeight + maxIcicleHeight + math.floor(4 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5),
                    math.floor((entry.Compact and 14 or 18) * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5)
                )
                if entry.CapContainer then
                    entry.CapContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, capContainerHeight)
                end

                entry.CapHeight = renderedPeakHeight

                if type(entry.Specks) == "table" then
                    local speckTransparency = math.clamp(0.86 - (localIntensity * 0.46), 0.12, 0.82)
                    for index = #entry.Specks, 1, -1 do
                        local speck = entry.Specks[index]
                        if speck and speck.Parent then
                            speck.BackgroundTransparency = speckTransparency
                        else
                            table.remove(entry.Specks, index)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_FindSnowLandingTarget(localX, previousBottomY, nextBottomY)
    if not self._overlayPhysicsEnabled or type(self._snowFrostTargets) ~= "table" or not self.mainFrameYay or not self.mainFrameYay.Parent then
        return nil, nil
    end

    local mainAbsolutePosition = self.mainFrameYay.AbsolutePosition
    local resolvedX = tonumber(localX) or 0
    local previousY = tonumber(previousBottomY) or 0
    local nextY = tonumber(nextBottomY) or 0
    local bestEntry = nil
    local bestSurfaceY = nil

    for target, entry in pairs(self._snowFrostTargets) do
        if entry
            and entry.CatchSnow ~= false
            and target
            and target.Parent
            and isNexusUIGuiObjectActuallyVisible(target) then
            local targetAbsolutePosition = target.AbsolutePosition
            local targetAbsoluteSize = target.AbsoluteSize
            local localLeft = targetAbsolutePosition.X - mainAbsolutePosition.X
            local localTop = targetAbsolutePosition.Y - mainAbsolutePosition.Y + (entry.CatchOffsetY or 0)
            local insetX = math.min(entry.CatchInsetX or (8 * mobileScaleFactor), math.max(0, (targetAbsoluteSize.X * 0.28)))
            local leftBound = localLeft + insetX
            local rightBound = localLeft + targetAbsoluteSize.X - insetX

            if rightBound > leftBound
                and resolvedX >= leftBound
                and resolvedX <= rightBound
                and previousY <= localTop
                and nextY >= localTop
                and (bestSurfaceY == nil or localTop < bestSurfaceY) then
                bestEntry = entry
                bestSurfaceY = localTop
            end
        end
    end

    return bestEntry, bestSurfaceY
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_DepositSnowOnTarget(entry, localX, amount)
    if type(entry) ~= "table" or not entry.Target or not entry.Target.Parent or not entry.CapContainer or not entry.CapContainer.Parent then
        return
    end

    local targetSize = entry.Target.AbsoluteSize
    if targetSize.X <= 0 then
        return
    end

    local mainAbsolutePosition = self.mainFrameYay and self.mainFrameYay.AbsolutePosition or Vector2.new(0, 0)
    local targetAbsolutePosition = entry.Target.AbsolutePosition
    local relativeX = math.clamp(
        (tonumber(localX) or 0) - (targetAbsolutePosition.X - mainAbsolutePosition.X),
        4 * mobileScaleFactor,
        math.max(4 * mobileScaleFactor, targetSize.X - 4 * mobileScaleFactor)
    )

    self:_InjectSnowMassIntoTarget(entry, relativeX / math.max(1, targetSize.X), amount)

    if (entry.MaxSpecks or 0) <= 0 then
        return
    end
    if math.random() > (entry.Compact and 0.22 or 0.34) then
        return
    end

    local capHeightPixels = math.max(1, math.floor(((entry.CapHeight or 0) + 2 * mobileScaleFactor) + 0.5))
    local speckWidth = math.max(3 * mobileScaleFactor, (entry.Compact and math.random(5, 9) or math.random(7, 13)) * mobileScaleFactor)
    local speckHeight = math.max(1, math.floor((entry.Compact and math.random(1, 2) or math.random(2, 4)) * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5))
    local speck = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 246, 255),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.22,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, relativeX + math.random(-3, 3) * mobileScaleFactor, 0, math.random(0, math.max(0, capHeightPixels - speckHeight))),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, speckWidth, 0, speckHeight),
        ZIndex = (entry.Overlay and entry.Overlay.ZIndex or 3) + 4,
        Parent = entry.CapContainer
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = speck})

    table.insert(entry.Specks, speck)
    while #entry.Specks > (entry.MaxSpecks or 12) do
        local oldestSpeck = table.remove(entry.Specks, 1)
        if oldestSpeck and oldestSpeck.Parent then
            oldestSpeck:Destroy()
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ShakeSnowOffTarget(targetOrEntry, strength, options)
    if not self._overlayPhysicsEnabled then
        return
    end

    local entry = targetOrEntry
    if targetOrEntry and typeof(targetOrEntry) == "Instance" then
        entry = self._snowFrostTargets and self._snowFrostTargets[targetOrEntry]
    end
    if type(entry) ~= "table" or not entry.Target or not entry.Target.Parent then
        return
    end

    local segmentCount = self:_EnsureSnowTargetProfile(entry, entry.Target.AbsoluteSize.X)
    local massProfile = entry.MassProfile or {}
    local displayProfile = entry.DisplayMassProfile or {}
    local specks = entry.Specks or {}
    local removalStrength = math.clamp(tonumber(strength) or 0.2, 0.05, 1)
    local totalMass = 0
    for index = 1, segmentCount do
        totalMass = totalMass + (massProfile[index] or 0)
    end
    if #specks <= 0 and totalMass <= 0.02 and (entry.Deposit or 0) <= 0.01 then
        return
    end

    local mainAbsolutePosition = self.mainFrameYay and self.mainFrameYay.AbsolutePosition or Vector2.new(0, 0)
    local targetAbsolutePosition = entry.Target.AbsolutePosition
    local targetLocalTop = targetAbsolutePosition.Y - mainAbsolutePosition.Y + math.max(1, (entry.CapHeight or 0) - math.floor(2 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5))
    local segmentWidth = segmentCount > 0 and (entry.Target.AbsoluteSize.X / segmentCount) or 0
    local motionX = type(options) == "table" and tonumber(options.MotionX) or 0
    local motionY = type(options) == "table" and tonumber(options.MotionY) or 0
    local removalPasses = math.max(1, math.floor((segmentCount * removalStrength) * (entry.Compact and 0.18 or 0.24) + 0.5))
    local canEmitShards = (tonumber(entry.ShardCooldown) or 0) <= 0

    for _ = 1, removalPasses do
        local pickIndex = nil
        local pickScore = 0
        for index = 1, segmentCount do
            local segmentMass = massProfile[index] or 0
            if segmentMass > 0.01 then
                local score = segmentMass * (0.55 + math.random() * 0.45)
                if score > pickScore then
                    pickScore = score
                    pickIndex = index
                end
            end
        end

        if not pickIndex then
            break
        end

        local segmentMass = massProfile[pickIndex] or 0
        local removedMass = math.min(segmentMass, math.max(0.035, segmentMass * (0.34 + removalStrength * 0.38)))
        massProfile[pickIndex] = math.max(segmentMass - removedMass, 0)
        if displayProfile[pickIndex] ~= nil then
            displayProfile[pickIndex] = math.max(displayProfile[pickIndex] - removedMass * 0.5, massProfile[pickIndex])
        end

        if canEmitShards and segmentWidth > 0 then
            local emitX = (targetAbsolutePosition.X - mainAbsolutePosition.X) + ((pickIndex - 0.5) * segmentWidth)
            local shardCount = removedMass >= 0.12 and 2 or 1
            for shardIndex = 1, shardCount do
                self:_SpawnIceShard(
                    emitX + math.random(-4, 4) * mobileScaleFactor,
                    targetLocalTop + math.random(-1, 2) * mobileScaleFactor,
                    {
                        Width = math.max(2 * mobileScaleFactor, (3 + removedMass * 16 + shardIndex) * mobileScaleFactor * 0.5),
                        Height = math.max(6 * mobileScaleFactor, (6 + removedMass * 28 + shardIndex * 2) * mobileScaleFactor * 0.55),
                        VelocityX = math.clamp(motionX * 0.14 + math.random(-36, 36) * mobileScaleFactor, -150, 150),
                        VelocityY = math.clamp(math.max(28 * mobileScaleFactor, math.abs(motionY) * 0.04) + math.random(18, 44) * mobileScaleFactor, 18, 180),
                        Gravity = math.random(180, 260) * mobileScaleFactor,
                        RotationSpeed = math.random(-140, 140),
                        DepositAmount = removedMass * (entry.Compact and 0.82 or 0.96)
                    }
                )
            end
        end
    end

    local removeCount = math.min(#specks, math.max(1, math.floor(#specks * removalStrength + 0.5)))
    for _ = 1, removeCount do
        if #specks <= 0 then
            break
        end
        local index = math.random(1, #specks)
        local speck = table.remove(specks, index)
        if speck and speck.Parent then
            speck:Destroy()
        end
    end

    if canEmitShards then
        entry.ShardCooldown = entry.Compact and 0.05 or 0.035
    end
    entry.Deposit = math.max((tonumber(entry.Deposit) or 0) - (removalStrength * 0.16), 0)
    self:_UpdateSnowFrostTargets(true)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ShakeVisibleSnowTargets(strength)
    if not self._overlayPhysicsEnabled or type(self._snowFrostTargets) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    local visibleEntries = {}
    for target, entry in pairs(self._snowFrostTargets) do
        if target
            and entry
            and target.Parent
            and isNexusUIGuiObjectActuallyVisible(target)
            and ((entry.Deposit or 0) > 0.02 or (type(entry.Specks) == "table" and #entry.Specks > 0)) then
            table.insert(visibleEntries, entry)
        end
    end

    if #visibleEntries <= 0 then
        return
    end

    local shakeCount = math.min(#visibleEntries, math.max(1, math.floor(#visibleEntries * math.clamp(tonumber(strength) or 0.18, 0.05, 1) * 0.5 + 0.5)))
    for _ = 1, shakeCount do
        local index = math.random(1, #visibleEntries)
        local entry = table.remove(visibleEntries, index)
        self:_ShakeSnowOffTarget(entry, strength)
        if #visibleEntries <= 0 then
            break
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:Totallyspringedoff(targetOrEntry, strength, options)
    self:_ShakeSnowOffTarget(targetOrEntry, strength, options)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SpawnSnowflake(startX, startY, options)
    if not self.snowLayerFrame or not self.snowLayerFrame.Parent then
        return
    end
    options = options or {}
    local width = self._cachedViewportWidth or 200
    local height = self._cachedViewportHeight or 320
    local size = math.max(4 * mobileScaleFactor, tonumber(options.Size) or (math.random(7, 12) * mobileScaleFactor))
    local resolvedStartX = tonumber(startX)
    if resolvedStartX == nil then
        resolvedStartX = math.random(0, math.max(0, math.floor(math.max(0, width - size) + 0.5)))
    else
        resolvedStartX = math.clamp(resolvedStartX, 0, math.max(0, width - size))
    end
    local resolvedStartY = tonumber(startY)
    if resolvedStartY == nil then
        resolvedStartY = math.random(0, math.max(0, math.floor(10 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5)))
    else
        resolvedStartY = math.clamp(resolvedStartY, 0, math.max(0, height - size))
    end
    local flakeColor = Color3.fromRGB(224 + math.random(0, 18), 240 + math.random(0, 12), 255)
    local flake = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, resolvedStartX, 0, resolvedStartY),
        Rotation = math.random(0, 359),
        ZIndex = 12,
        Parent = self.snowLayerFrame
    })
    local flakeParts = {}
    local armThickness = math.max(1, math.floor(size * 0.14 + 0.5))
    for _, rotation in ipairs({0, 60, -60}) do
        local arm = makeThingExist("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = flakeColor,
            BackgroundTransparency = math.random(10, 24) / 100,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, armThickness, 0, math.max(3, math.floor(size * 0.94 + 0.5))),
            Rotation = rotation,
            ZIndex = 13,
            Parent = flake
        })
        makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = arm})
        table.insert(flakeParts, arm)
    end
    local centerDot = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(248, 250, 255),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(2, math.floor(size * 0.28 + 0.5)), 0, math.max(2, math.floor(size * 0.28 + 0.5))),
        ZIndex = 14,
        Parent = flake
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = centerDot})
    table.insert(flakeParts, centerDot)
    local glow = makeThingExist("ImageLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Image = "rbxassetid://5028857084",
        ImageColor3 = flakeColor,
        ImageTransparency = 0.88,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1.4, 0, 1.4, 0),
        ZIndex = 11,
        Parent = flake
    })

    local baseTransparency = math.random(10, 24) / 100
    local fallSpeed = tonumber(options.FallSpeed) or (math.random(24, 38) * mobileScaleFactor)
    for _, part in ipairs(flakeParts) do
        part.BackgroundTransparency = baseTransparency
    end

    table.insert(self._snowflakes, {
        overlayType = "Snow",
        instance = flake,
        parts = flakeParts,
        glow = glow,
        xOffset = resolvedStartX,
        velocityX = tonumber(options.VelocityX) or (math.random(-8, 8) * mobileScaleFactor),
        velocityY = tonumber(options.VelocityY) or fallSpeed,
        airDrag = tonumber(options.AirDrag) or 1.45,
        maxFallSpeed = tonumber(options.MaxFallSpeed) or (fallSpeed * 2.2),
        driftAmount = tonumber(options.DriftAmount) or (math.random(10, 22) * mobileScaleFactor),
        swirlAmount = tonumber(options.SwirlAmount) or (math.random(2, 8) * mobileScaleFactor),
        driftSpeed = tonumber(options.DriftSpeed) or (math.random(4, 10) / 10),
        twinkleSpeed = tonumber(options.TwinkleSpeed) or (math.random(7, 14) / 10),
        baseTransparency = baseTransparency,
        phase = math.random() * math.pi * 2,
        spin = tonumber(options.Spin) or math.random(-8, 8),
        size = size,
        yOffset = flake.Position.Y.Offset,
        depositAmount = tonumber(options.DepositAmount) or (options.StartInside == true and 0.18 or 0.08),
        startInside = options.StartInside == true
    })
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SpawnRainDrop()
    if not self.snowLayerFrame or not self.snowLayerFrame.Parent then
        return
    end
    local width = self._cachedViewportWidth or 200
    local height = self._cachedViewportHeight or 320
    local depthScale = math.random(62, 150) / 100
    local length = (math.random(16, 30) * depthScale + 4) * mobileScaleFactor
    local thickness = math.max(1, math.floor((0.8 + depthScale * 0.9) * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5))
    local startY = -length - math.random(6, 28)
    local fallSpeed = (math.random(320, 520) * depthScale) * mobileScaleFactor
    local windSpeed = (math.random(24, 56) * depthScale) * mobileScaleFactor
    local travelHeight = math.max(1, (height - startY) + (8 * mobileScaleFactor))
    local projectedTravelX = (windSpeed / math.max(fallSpeed, 1)) * travelHeight
    local safeMargin = math.max(8 * mobileScaleFactor, length * 0.22)
    local startMinX = safeMargin
    local startMaxX = width - safeMargin - projectedTravelX
    local startX
    if startMaxX >= startMinX then
        startX = math.random(math.floor(startMinX + 0.5), math.floor(startMaxX + 0.5))
    else
        startX = math.clamp((width - projectedTravelX) * 0.5, safeMargin, width - safeMargin)
    end
    local angle = math.deg(math.atan2(fallSpeed, windSpeed)) - 90
    local drop = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(176 + math.random(0, 26), 208 + math.random(0, 26), 255),
        BackgroundTransparency = math.random(18, 38) / 100,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, startX, 0, startY),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, thickness, 0, length),
        Rotation = angle,
        ZIndex = 11,
        Parent = self.snowLayerFrame
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = drop})
    makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 90,
        Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.18, 0.28),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.82, 0.12),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        }),
        Parent = drop
    })
    table.insert(self._snowflakes, {
        overlayType = "RainDrop",
        instance = drop,
        xOffset = startX,
        yOffset = startY,
        velocityX = windSpeed,
        velocityY = fallSpeed,
        baseWindSpeed = windSpeed,
        angle = angle,
        width = thickness,
        length = length,
        baseTransparency = drop.BackgroundTransparency,
        driftSpeed = math.random(11, 24) / 10,
        windJitter = math.random(2, 7) * mobileScaleFactor,
        stretchPulse = math.random(10, 18) / 10,
        splashChance = 0.8 + math.random() * 0.18,
        phase = math.random() * math.pi * 2,
        impactY = height - safeMargin
    })
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SpawnRainSplash(xPosition, yPosition)
    if not self.snowLayerFrame or not self.snowLayerFrame.Parent then
        return
    end
    local splash = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, xPosition, 0, yPosition),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 28 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 14 * mobileScaleFactor),
        ZIndex = 11,
        Parent = self.snowLayerFrame
    })
    local splashColor = Color3.fromRGB(194 + math.random(0, 18), 224 + math.random(0, 18), 255)
    local ripple = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        BackgroundColor3 = splashColor,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.28,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -1 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 6 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, math.max(1, math.floor(2 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5))),
        ZIndex = 12,
        Parent = splash
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = ripple})
    local leftDroplet = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        BackgroundColor3 = splashColor,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.18,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -2 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, -1 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(1, math.floor(2 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5)), 0, 6 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Rotation = -32,
        ZIndex = 12,
        Parent = splash
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = leftDroplet})
    local rightDroplet = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        BackgroundColor3 = splashColor,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.18,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 2 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, -1 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(1, math.floor(2 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5)), 0, 6 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Rotation = 32,
        ZIndex = 12,
        Parent = splash
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = rightDroplet})

    local life = math.random(10, 18) / 100
    table.insert(self._snowflakes, {
        overlayType = "RainSplash",
        instance = splash,
        ripple = ripple,
        leftDroplet = leftDroplet,
        rightDroplet = rightDroplet,
        life = life,
        totalLife = life,
        spread = math.random(8, 16) * mobileScaleFactor,
        rise = math.random(4, 8) * mobileScaleFactor
    })
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SpawnStarParticle()
    if not self.snowLayerFrame or not self.snowLayerFrame.Parent then
        return
    end
    local width = self._cachedViewportWidth or 200
    local height = self._cachedViewportHeight or 320
    local size = (math.random(10, 28) / 10) * mobileScaleFactor
    local startX = math.random(0, width)
    local startY = math.random(0, height)
    local star = makeThingExist("ImageLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Image = "rbxassetid://3570695787",
        ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(224 + math.random(0, 28), 233 + math.random(0, 20), 255),
        ImageTransparency = math.random(22, 56) / 100,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, startX, 0, startY),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size),
        ZIndex = 10,
        Parent = self.snowLayerFrame
    })
    table.insert(self._snowflakes, {
        overlayType = "Stars",
        instance = star,
        xOffset = startX,
        yOffset = startY,
        velocityX = (math.random(-16, 16) / 10) * mobileScaleFactor,
        velocityY = (math.random(-9, 9) / 10) * mobileScaleFactor,
        baseSize = size,
        driftAmount = math.random(5, 18) * mobileScaleFactor,
        driftLift = math.random(4, 14) * mobileScaleFactor,
        driftSpeed = math.random(3, 10) / 10,
        twinkleSpeed = math.random(7, 18) / 10,
        pulseAmount = (math.random(8, 24) / 100),
        baseTransparency = star.ImageTransparency,
        phase = math.random() * math.pi * 2
    })
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SetSnowEnabled(enabled)
    self._uiVisualSettings.Snow = enabled == true
    if self._uiVisualSettings.Snow and self._overlayMode == "None" then
        self:_SetOverlayMode(self._lastActiveOverlayMode or self.config.DefaultOverlayMode or "Snow")
    end
    if self.snowLayerFrame then
        self.snowLayerFrame.Visible = self._uiVisualSettings.Snow and self.isToggledOn and self._overlayMode ~= "None"
    end
    if not self._uiVisualSettings.Snow or not self.isToggledOn or self._overlayMode == "None" then
        self._overlayUpdateAccumulator = 0
        self._snowSpawnAccumulator = 0
        self._snowFrostExposure = 0
        self._snowPhysicsMoveAccumulator = 0
        self:_SetSnowFrostIntensity(0, true)
        self:_ClearSnowflakes()
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SetOverlayMode(mode)
    local normalized = string.lower(tostring(mode or "snow"))
    local resolved = "Snow"
    if normalized == "rain" then
        resolved = "Snow"
        if not self._rainOverlayDisabledNotified and not self._isApplyingConfig then
            self._rainOverlayDisabledNotified = true
            self:Notify({
                Title = "Overlay",
                Description = "Rain overlay is disabled for now.",
                Duration = 3
            })
        end
    elseif normalized == "stars" or normalized == "star" then
        resolved = "Stars"
    elseif normalized == "none" or normalized == "off" then
        resolved = "None"
    end
    local changed = self._overlayMode ~= resolved
    self._overlayMode = resolved
    if resolved ~= "None" then
        self._lastActiveOverlayMode = resolved
    end
    if self.uiOverlayModeValueLabel then
        self.uiOverlayModeValueLabel.Text = resolved
    end
    if self.snowLayerFrame then
