        self.snowLayerFrame.Visible = self.isToggledOn and self._uiVisualSettings.Snow and resolved ~= "None"
    end
    if changed or resolved == "None" then
        self._overlayUpdateAccumulator = 0
        self._snowSpawnAccumulator = 0
        if resolved ~= "Snow" then
            self._snowFrostExposure = 0
            self._snowPhysicsMoveAccumulator = 0
            self:_SetSnowFrostIntensity(0, true)
        end
        self:_ClearSnowflakes()
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SetBackgroundEffectsEnabled(enabled)
    if self.config and self.config.ForceBackgroundEffects == true then
        enabled = true
    end
    self._uiVisualSettings.BackgroundEffects = enabled == true
    if not self._uiVisualSettings.BackgroundEffects then
        self._backgroundFxAccumulator = 0
    end
    if self.mainBackgroundEffectsFrame then
        self.mainBackgroundEffectsFrame.Visible = self._uiVisualSettings.BackgroundEffects and self.isToggledOn
    end
    self:_ApplyBackgroundEffectsOpacity(true)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ApplyBackgroundEffectsOpacity(instant)
    if not self.mainBackgroundEffectsFrame then
        return
    end

    local targetTransparency = 1 - math.clamp(tonumber(self._backgroundOverlayOpacity) or 0.55, 0, 1)
    self.config.BackgroundOverlayOpacity = math.clamp(tonumber(self._backgroundOverlayOpacity) or 0.1, 0, 1)

    if instant then
        self.mainBackgroundEffectsFrame.BackgroundTransparency = targetTransparency
        return
    end

    smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.mainBackgroundEffectsFrame, {
        BackgroundTransparency = targetTransparency
    }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:SetBackgroundOverlayOpacity(opacity, skipSliderSync)
    self._backgroundOverlayOpacity = math.clamp(tonumber(opacity) or self._backgroundOverlayOpacity or 0.55, 0, 1)
    self.config.BackgroundOverlayOpacity = self._backgroundOverlayOpacity
    if not skipSliderSync and self.uiBackgroundOpacitySliderRef and type(self.uiBackgroundOpacitySliderRef.Set) == "function" then
        self.uiBackgroundOpacitySliderRef:Set(self._backgroundOverlayOpacity, true)
    end
    self:_ApplyBackgroundEffectsOpacity(false)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:SetOverlayPhysicsEnabled(enabled, skipNotification)
    self._overlayPhysicsEnabled = enabled == true
    if not self._overlayPhysicsEnabled then
        self._snowPhysicsMoveAccumulator = 0
        self._snowFrostExposure = 0
        if type(self._snowFrostTargets) == "table" then
            for _, entry in pairs(self._snowFrostTargets) do
                if type(entry) == "table" then
                    entry.Deposit = 0
                    if type(entry.MassProfile) == "table" then
                        for index = 1, #entry.MassProfile do
                            entry.MassProfile[index] = 0
                        end
                    end
                    if type(entry.DisplayMassProfile) == "table" then
                        for index = 1, #entry.DisplayMassProfile do
                            entry.DisplayMassProfile[index] = 0
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        self:_SetSnowFrostIntensity(0, true)
        if self.uiOverlayPhysicsToggleRef and type(self.uiOverlayPhysicsToggleRef.Get) == "function" and self.uiOverlayPhysicsToggleRef:Get() ~= false then
            self.uiOverlayPhysicsToggleRef:Set(false, true)
        end
    else
        if self.uiOverlayPhysicsToggleRef and type(self.uiOverlayPhysicsToggleRef.Get) == "function" and self.uiOverlayPhysicsToggleRef:Get() ~= true then
            self.uiOverlayPhysicsToggleRef:Set(true, true)
        end
        if not skipNotification and not self._isApplyingConfig then
            self:Notify({
                Title = "Overlay Physics",
                Description = "PERFORMANCE INTENSIVE BBG!",
                Duration = 3.2
            })
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SetSnowFrostIntensity(intensity, instant)
    local resolvedIntensity = math.clamp(tonumber(intensity) or 0, 0, 1)
    self._snowFrostIntensity = resolvedIntensity

    if self.snowFrostOverlayFrame then
        self.snowFrostOverlayFrame.Visible = false
    end

    self:_UpdateSnowFrostTargets(instant)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SetNameHidden(enabled)
    self._uiVisualSettings.HideName = enabled == true
    local hideName = self._uiVisualSettings.HideName == true

    if self.userNameLabel then
        self.userNameLabel.Visible = not hideName
        if not hideName then
            self.userNameLabel.Text = "@" .. tostring(theOneAndOnlyPlayer.Name)
        end
    end

    if self._espPreviewHeaderTag then
        self._espPreviewHeaderTag.Visible = not hideName
        if not hideName then
            self._espPreviewHeaderTag.Text = "@" .. tostring(theOneAndOnlyPlayer.Name)
        end
    end

    if self._espPreviewPanel then
        self:_UpdateESPPreview(0)
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_SetTextGradientEnabled(enabled)
    self._uiVisualSettings.TextGradient = enabled == true
    if not self._uiVisualSettings.TextGradient then
        self._gradientAnimationAccumulator = 0
    end
    local accent = self.config.AccentColor
    local gradientSweepSpeed = 0.9
    local animationTime = tonumber(self._textGradientAnimationTime) or 0
    local sweepX = ((animationTime * gradientSweepSpeed) % 2) - 1
    for i = #self._gradientObjects, 1, -1 do
        self._gradientObjects[i] = nil
    end
    for _, label in ipairs(self._gradientLabels) do
        if label and label.Parent then
            local gradientObj = label:FindFirstChild("NexusUITextGradient")
            if self._uiVisualSettings.TextGradient then
                if not gradientObj then
                    gradientObj = makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
                        Name = "NexusUITextGradient",
                        Rotation = 0,
                        Parent = label
                    })
                end
                gradientObj.Rotation = 0
                gradientObj.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, accent:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.2)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, accent:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.35))
                })
                gradientObj.Offset = Vector2.new(sweepX, 0)
                table.insert(self._gradientObjects, gradientObj)
            elseif gradientObj then
                gradientObj:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_AnimateTextGradients(dt)
    if not self._uiVisualSettings.TextGradient then
        return
    end

    self._gradientAnimationAccumulator = (tonumber(self._gradientAnimationAccumulator) or 0) + (dt or 0)
    if self._gradientAnimationAccumulator < (1 / 30) then
        return
    end
    local resolvedDt = self._gradientAnimationAccumulator
    self._gradientAnimationAccumulator = 0

    local gradientSweepSpeed = 0.9
    self._textGradientAnimationTime = (tonumber(self._textGradientAnimationTime) or 0) + resolvedDt
    local sweepX = ((self._textGradientAnimationTime * gradientSweepSpeed) % 2) - 1
    local animatedOffset = Vector2.new(sweepX, 0)

    for i = #self._gradientObjects, 1, -1 do
        local gradientObj = self._gradientObjects[i]
        if gradientObj and gradientObj.Parent then
            gradientObj.Rotation = 0
            gradientObj.Offset = animatedOffset
        else
            table.remove(self._gradientObjects, i)
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:ApplyFontPreset(index)
    if #self._fontPresets == 0 then
        return
    end
    local normalizedIndex = tonumber(index) or (self._fontPresetIndex + 1)
    if normalizedIndex > #self._fontPresets then
        normalizedIndex = 1
    elseif normalizedIndex < 1 then
        normalizedIndex = #self._fontPresets
    end
    self._fontPresetIndex = normalizedIndex
    local preset = self._fontPresets[self._fontPresetIndex]
    local targetFontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
    if preset.Family then
        local okFont, generatedFont = pcall(function()
            return Font.new(preset.Family, preset.Weight or Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
        end)
        if okFont then
            targetFontFace = generatedFont
        end
    elseif preset.EnumFont then
        pcall(function()
            targetFontFace = Font.fromEnum(preset.EnumFont)
        end)
    end
    if self.screenGuiYay and self.screenGuiYay.Parent then
        for _, obj in ipairs(self.screenGuiYay:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox") then
                local isFontLocked = false
                local okLocked, lockedValue = pcall(function()
                    return obj:GetAttribute("NexusUIFontLock") == true
                end)
                isFontLocked = okLocked and lockedValue == true

                if not isFontLocked then
                    if preset.EnumFont then
                        pcall(function()
                            obj.Font = preset.EnumFont
                        end)
                    end
                    applyNexusUIFont(obj, targetFontFace, preset.EnumFont or defaultNexusUIFontEnum)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if self.uiSettingsFontValueLabel then
        self.uiSettingsFontValueLabel.Text = preset.Name
    end
    if type(self._refreshSettingsFontSelector) == "function" then
        pcall(self._refreshSettingsFontSelector)
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_RefreshAccentCore()
    local accent = self.config.AccentColor
    if self.mobileToggleIcon then
        self.mobileToggleIcon.ImageColor3 = accent
    end
    if self.watermarkGlow then
        self.watermarkGlow.ImageColor3 = accent
    end
    if self.watermarkStroke then
        self.watermarkStroke.Color = accent:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(46, 50, 60), 0.64)
    end
    if self.watermarkNotificationBadge then
        self.watermarkNotificationBadge.BackgroundColor3 = accent
    end
    if self.mainBackgroundAccentGlow then
        self.mainBackgroundAccentGlow.ImageColor3 = accent
    end
    if self.uiAccentPreviewFrame then
        self.uiAccentPreviewFrame.BackgroundColor3 = accent
    end
    if self.uiBackgroundOpacitySliderFill then
        self.uiBackgroundOpacitySliderFill.BackgroundColor3 = accent
    end
    if self.uiSettingsButtonStroke then
        self.uiSettingsButtonStroke.Color = self.uiSettingsOpen and accent:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20), 0.45) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
    end
    if self.currentTabYay and self.currentTabYay.buttonFrame then
        self.currentTabYay.buttonFrame.BackgroundColor3 = accent
    end
    self:_SetWatermarkStatus(self._watermarkStatusText or "READY", self._watermarkStatusColor or accent)
    self:_SetDynamicIslandNotificationCount(self._dynamicIslandNotificationCount or 0)
    if self._scrollbarRefreshers then
        for index = #self._scrollbarRefreshers, 1, -1 do
            local refresher = self._scrollbarRefreshers[index]
            local okRefresh, keepRefresher = pcall(refresher)
            if not okRefresh or keepRefresher == false then
                table.remove(self._scrollbarRefreshers, index)
            end
        end
    end
    self:_SetTextGradientEnabled(self._uiVisualSettings.TextGradient)
    self:_RefreshSearchHighlight()
    if type(self._refreshSearchBoxVisuals) == "function" then
        pcall(self._refreshSearchBoxVisuals)
    end
    if type(self._refreshSettingsFontSelector) == "function" then
        pcall(self._refreshSettingsFontSelector)
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ApplyBackgroundImage()
    if not self.mainBackgroundBrandImage then
        return
    end

    local backgroundState = self._backgroundImageState or {}
    local resolvedImage = backgroundState.ResolvedImage or ""
    local hasImage = resolvedImage ~= ""

    self.mainBackgroundBrandImage.Image = resolvedImage
    self.mainBackgroundBrandImage.Visible = hasImage
    self.mainBackgroundBrandImage.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    self.mainBackgroundBrandImage.ImageTransparency = hasImage and self.config.BackgroundImageTransparency or 1
    self.mainBackgroundBrandImage.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.54, 0)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ResolveVideoLayerParent(videoConfig)
    videoConfig = type(videoConfig) == "table" and videoConfig or {}
    if typeof(videoConfig.Parent) == "Instance" then
        return videoConfig.Parent
    end

    local target = string.lower(tostring(videoConfig.Target or videoConfig.Role or "screen"))
    if target == "background" or target == "backgroundfx" or target == "mainbackground" then
        return self.mainBackgroundEffectsFrame or self.mainFrameYay or self.screenGuiYay
    end
    if target == "main" or target == "window" or target == "mainframe" then
        return self.mainFrameYay or self.screenGuiYay
    end
    return self.screenGuiYay
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ResolveVideoLayerDefaults(videoConfig)
    videoConfig = type(videoConfig) == "table" and videoConfig or {}
    local target = string.lower(tostring(videoConfig.Target or videoConfig.Role or "screen"))
    local isFullScreenRole = target == "screen" or target == "overlay" or target == "intro" or target == "loading"
    local isBackgroundRole = target == "background" or target == "backgroundfx" or target == "mainbackground"

    local defaults = {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        ImageTransparency = 0,
        ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
        ZIndex = 0
    }

    if isFullScreenRole then
        defaults.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        defaults.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
        defaults.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        defaults.ZIndex = target == "intro" and 12000 or (target == "loading" and 11500 or 9000)
    elseif isBackgroundRole then
        defaults.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        defaults.ZIndex = 0
    end

    return defaults
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_BuildVideoLayerAuth(videoConfig)
    videoConfig = type(videoConfig) == "table" and videoConfig or {}
    local headers = {}

    local function copyHeaders(sourceHeaders)
        if type(sourceHeaders) ~= "table" then
            return
        end
        for key, value in pairs(sourceHeaders) do
            if key ~= nil and value ~= nil then
                headers[tostring(key)] = tostring(value)
            end
        end
    end

    copyHeaders(videoConfig.Headers)
    copyHeaders(videoConfig.AuthHeaders)
    copyHeaders(videoConfig.ApiHeaders)

    local apiKey = videoConfig.ApiKey or videoConfig.AgentKey or videoConfig.AccessKey or videoConfig.Key or self._configuredVideoApiKey
    if apiKey ~= nil and apiKey ~= "" then
        local headerName = tostring(videoConfig.ApiKeyHeader or videoConfig.AuthHeader or "X-API-Key")
        if headers[headerName] == nil then
            headers[headerName] = tostring(apiKey)
        end
    end

    if videoConfig.BearerToken ~= nil and videoConfig.BearerToken ~= "" and headers.Authorization == nil then
        headers.Authorization = "Bearer " .. tostring(videoConfig.BearerToken)
    end

    return headers, apiKey
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ShouldUseVideoQueryKey(videoConfig)
    videoConfig = type(videoConfig) == "table" and videoConfig or {}
    return videoConfig.UseQueryKey == true or videoConfig.QueryKey == true or videoConfig.EmbedKeyInUrl == true
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ResolveVideoManifestRequest(videoConfig, manifestSource)
    local headers, apiKey = self:_BuildVideoLayerAuth(videoConfig)
    local requestSource = manifestSource
    if type(requestSource) == "string" and self:_ShouldUseVideoQueryKey(videoConfig) then
        requestSource = appendNexusUIQueryValue(requestSource, tostring(videoConfig.QueryKeyName or "api_key"), apiKey)
    end
    return requestSource, headers, apiKey
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_LoadVideoManifest(manifestSource, headers)
    local okLoad, manifestOrError = loadNexusUIJsonDocument(manifestSource, headers)
    if not okLoad or type(manifestOrError) ~= "table" then
        return false, tostring(manifestOrError or "Failed to load video manifest.")
    end

    local manifest = manifestOrError
    if (manifest.kind ~= "sheet_sequence" and manifest.kind ~= "frame_sequence") or type(manifest.frames) ~= "table" then
        return false, "Video manifest must be kind='sheet_sequence' or kind='frame_sequence' with frames[]."
    end

    return true, manifest
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ResolveVideoManifestUrl(manifestSource, assetUrl)
    if type(assetUrl) ~= "string" or assetUrl == "" then
        return ""
    end
    if assetUrl:match("^https?://") or assetUrl:match("^rbxasset") then
        return assetUrl
    end
    if type(manifestSource) == "string" then
        return resolveNexusUIRelativeUrl(manifestSource, assetUrl)
    end
    return assetUrl
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ResolveVideoAsset(assetUrl, headers)
    local assetState = resolveNexusUIBackgroundImageSource(assetUrl, headers)
    if type(assetState) ~= "table" or assetState.ResolvedImage == nil or assetState.ResolvedImage == "" then
        return false, nil, tostring((assetState and assetState.Error) or "Failed to resolve video asset.")
    end
    return true, assetState.ResolvedImage, assetState
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:CreateVideoLayer(videoConfig)
    videoConfig = type(videoConfig) == "table" and videoConfig or {}
    local manifestSource = videoConfig.Manifest or videoConfig.ManifestUrl or videoConfig.Source
    if manifestSource == nil then
        return nil, "Video layer requires ManifestUrl, Manifest, or Source."
    end
    local preparedAssets = type(videoConfig._NexusPreparedVideoAssets) == "table" and videoConfig._NexusPreparedVideoAssets or nil
    local manifestRequestSource, authHeaders, apiKey = self:_ResolveVideoManifestRequest(videoConfig, manifestSource)
    if preparedAssets and preparedAssets.ManifestSource ~= nil then
        manifestRequestSource = preparedAssets.ManifestSource
    end
    if preparedAssets and type(preparedAssets.Headers) == "table" then
        authHeaders = preparedAssets.Headers
    end

    local parent = self:_ResolveVideoLayerParent(videoConfig)
    if not parent then
        return nil, "Video layer parent is unavailable."
    end

    local manifest = preparedAssets and preparedAssets.Manifest or nil
    if not manifest then
        local okManifest, manifestOrError = self:_LoadVideoManifest(manifestRequestSource, authHeaders)
        if not okManifest then
            return nil, manifestOrError
        end
        manifest = manifestOrError
    end

    self._videoLayerCounter = (tonumber(self._videoLayerCounter) or 0) + 1
    local defaults = self:_ResolveVideoLayerDefaults(videoConfig)
    local layerName = tostring(videoConfig.Name or videoConfig.Id or ("VideoLayer" .. tostring(self._videoLayerCounter)))
    local zIndex = tonumber(videoConfig.ZIndex) or defaults.ZIndex
    local container = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        Name = layerName,
        AnchorPoint = videoConfig.AnchorPoint or defaults.AnchorPoint,
        Position = videoConfig.Position or defaults.Position,
        Size = videoConfig.Size or defaults.Size,
        BackgroundColor3 = videoConfig.BackgroundColor3 or videoConfig.BackgroundColor or defaults.BackgroundColor3,
        BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(tonumber(videoConfig.BackgroundTransparency) or defaults.BackgroundTransparency, 0, 1),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ClipsDescendants = videoConfig.ClipsDescendants ~= false,
        Visible = videoConfig.Visible ~= false,
        ZIndex = zIndex,
        Parent = parent
    })

    if videoConfig.CornerRadius then
        makeThingExist("UICorner", {
            CornerRadius = videoConfig.CornerRadius,
            Parent = container
        })
    end

    local imageLabel = makeThingExist("ImageLabel", {
        Name = "VideoImage",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ImageTransparency = math.clamp(tonumber(videoConfig.ImageTransparency) or defaults.ImageTransparency, 0, 1),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        ScaleType = videoConfig.ScaleType or defaults.ScaleType,
        ZIndex = zIndex + 1,
        Parent = container
    })

    local warmHolder = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        Name = "VideoWarmAssets",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-10000, -10000),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1),
        ZIndex = zIndex,
        Parent = container
    })

    local controller = {
        Library = self,
        Name = layerName,
        ManifestSource = manifestRequestSource,
        ApiKey = apiKey,
        Headers = authHeaders,
        Manifest = manifest,
        Container = container,
        ImageLabel = imageLabel,
        WarmHolder = warmHolder,
        Frames = {},
        Sheets = {},
        Ready = false,
        IsPlaying = false,
        Loop = videoConfig.Loop ~= false,
        PlaybackRate = math.max(0.01, tonumber(videoConfig.PlaybackRate) or 1),
        TimePosition = 0,
        TotalDuration = 0,
        _frameIndex = 1,
        _destroyed = false
    }

    local function reportStatus(status, detail)
        controller.Status = status
        controller.StatusDetail = detail
        if type(videoConfig.OnStatus) == "function" then
            pcall(videoConfig.OnStatus, controller, status, detail)
        end
    end

    function controller:_HandleCompletion()
        if self._destroyed or self._completionFired then
            return
        end

        self._completionFired = true
        reportStatus("Completed", self.TotalDuration)

        task.defer(function()
            if controller._destroyed then
                return
            end

            if type(videoConfig.OnComplete) == "function" then
                pcall(videoConfig.OnComplete, controller)
            end

            if controller._destroyed then
                return
            end

            if videoConfig.HideOnComplete == true then
                controller:SetVisible(false)
            end

            if videoConfig.DestroyOnComplete == true or videoConfig.AutoDestroy == true then
                controller:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end

    local function resolveVideoAssetUrl(assetUrl)
        local resolvedUrl = self:_ResolveVideoManifestUrl(manifestRequestSource, assetUrl)
        if self:_ShouldUseVideoQueryKey(videoConfig) then
            resolvedUrl = appendNexusUIQueryValue(resolvedUrl, tostring(videoConfig.QueryKeyName or "api_key"), apiKey)
        end
        return resolvedUrl
    end

    local function resolveVideoAsset(assetUrl)
        local cachedAssets = preparedAssets and preparedAssets.AssetsByUrl
        if type(cachedAssets) == "table" and cachedAssets[assetUrl] then
            local cached = cachedAssets[assetUrl]
            return true, cached.Asset, cached.State
        end
        return self:_ResolveVideoAsset(assetUrl, authHeaders)
    end

    local sheetByIndex = {}
    if manifest.kind == "sheet_sequence" then
        if type(manifest.sheets) ~= "table" or #manifest.sheets == 0 then
            container:Destroy()
            return nil, "Sheet video manifest must include sheets[]."
        end

        for sheetIndex, rawSheet in ipairs(manifest.sheets) do
            reportStatus("CachingSheet", sheetIndex)
            local sheetUrl = resolveVideoAssetUrl(rawSheet.url or rawSheet.file)
            local okAsset, asset, assetStateOrError = resolveVideoAsset(sheetUrl)
            if not okAsset then
                container:Destroy()
                return nil, "Failed to cache sheet #" .. tostring(sheetIndex) .. ": " .. tostring(assetStateOrError)
            end
            local sheet = {
                Index = tonumber(rawSheet.index) or sheetIndex,
                Asset = asset,
                State = assetStateOrError
            }
            table.insert(controller.Sheets, sheet)
            sheetByIndex[sheet.Index] = sheet
        end

        for frameIndex, rawFrame in ipairs(manifest.frames) do
            local sheet = sheetByIndex[tonumber(rawFrame.sheetIndex)]
            if not sheet then
                container:Destroy()
                return nil, "Frame #" .. tostring(frameIndex) .. " references missing sheet " .. tostring(rawFrame.sheetIndex)
            end
            local duration = math.max(1 / 240, tonumber(rawFrame.duration or manifest.frameDuration) or (1 / math.max(1, tonumber(manifest.fps) or 30)))
            controller.TotalDuration = controller.TotalDuration + duration
            controller.Frames[frameIndex] = {
                Asset = sheet.Asset,
                X = tonumber(rawFrame.x) or 0,
                Y = tonumber(rawFrame.y) or 0,
                Width = tonumber(rawFrame.width or manifest.frameWidth) or 0,
                Height = tonumber(rawFrame.height or manifest.frameHeight) or 0,
                Duration = duration,
                Start = controller.TotalDuration - duration
            }
        end
    else
        for frameIndex, rawFrame in ipairs(manifest.frames) do
            reportStatus("CachingFrame", frameIndex)
            local frameUrl = resolveVideoAssetUrl(rawFrame.url or rawFrame.file)
            local okAsset, asset, assetStateOrError = resolveVideoAsset(frameUrl)
            if not okAsset then
                container:Destroy()
                return nil, "Failed to cache frame #" .. tostring(frameIndex) .. ": " .. tostring(assetStateOrError)
            end
            local duration = math.max(1 / 240, tonumber(rawFrame.duration or manifest.frameDuration) or (1 / math.max(1, tonumber(manifest.fps) or 30)))
            controller.TotalDuration = controller.TotalDuration + duration
            controller.Frames[frameIndex] = {
                Asset = asset,
                Duration = duration,
                Start = controller.TotalDuration - duration
            }
        end
    end

    if #controller.Frames == 0 then
        container:Destroy()
        return nil, "Video manifest produced zero frames."
    end

    function controller:_ApplyFrame(frameIndex)
        if self._destroyed then
            return
        end
        local frame = self.Frames[frameIndex]
        if not frame then
            return
        end
        if imageLabel.Image ~= frame.Asset then
            imageLabel.Image = frame.Asset
        end
        if frame.Width and frame.Height then
            imageLabel.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(frame.X or 0, frame.Y or 0)
            imageLabel.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(frame.Width, frame.Height)
        else
            imageLabel.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            imageLabel.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        end
        self._frameIndex = frameIndex
    end

    function controller:_Preload()
        if videoConfig.Preload == false then
            return
        end
        if preparedAssets then
            reportStatus("Preloaded", #self.Sheets > 0 and #self.Sheets or #self.Frames)
            return
        end

        reportStatus("Preloading", #self.Sheets > 0 and #self.Sheets or #self.Frames)
        local preloadTargets = {}
        local warmTargets = #self.Sheets > 0 and self.Sheets or self.Frames
        for index, target in ipairs(warmTargets) do
            local warmImage = makeThingExist("ImageLabel", {
                Name = "WarmVideoAsset" .. tostring(index),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                ImageTransparency = 1,
                Image = target.Asset,
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1),
                Parent = warmHolder
            })
            target.WarmImage = warmImage
            table.insert(preloadTargets, warmImage)
        end

        local batchSize = math.max(1, tonumber(videoConfig.PreloadBatchSize) or 24)
        local index = 1
        while index <= #preloadTargets do
            local batch = {}
            for batchIndex = index, math.min(#preloadTargets, index + batchSize - 1) do
                table.insert(batch, preloadTargets[batchIndex])
            end
            pcall(function()
                contentProviderIsCool:PreloadAsync(batch)
            end)
            index = index + batchSize
            task.wait()
        end

        if videoConfig.KeepWarmImages == false and warmHolder and warmHolder.Parent then
            warmHolder:Destroy()
            self.WarmHolder = nil
        end
    end

    function controller:Play()
        if not self.Ready or self._destroyed then
            return false
        end
        if not self.Loop and (tonumber(self.TimePosition) or 0) >= self.TotalDuration then
            self.TimePosition = 0
            self:_ApplyFrame(1)
        end
        self._completionFired = false
        self.IsPlaying = true
        return true
    end

    function controller:Pause()
        self.IsPlaying = false
    end

    function controller:Restart()
        self._completionFired = false
        self.TimePosition = 0
        self:_ApplyFrame(1)
        self:Play()
    end

    function controller:Seek(seconds)
        local targetSeconds = math.clamp(tonumber(seconds) or 0, 0, self.TotalDuration)
        self.TimePosition = targetSeconds
        local nextFrameIndex = 1
        for index, frame in ipairs(self.Frames) do
            if targetSeconds >= frame.Start then
                nextFrameIndex = index
            else
                break
            end
        end
        self:_ApplyFrame(nextFrameIndex)
    end

    function controller:SetVisible(visible)
        if self.Container then
            self.Container.Visible = visible == true
        end
    end

    function controller:SetPosition(position)
        if self.Container and typeof(position) == "UDim2" then
            self.Container.Position = position
        end
    end

    function controller:SetSize(size)
        if self.Container and typeof(size) == "UDim2" then
            self.Container.Size = size
        end
    end

    function controller:SetTransparency(transparency)
        if self.Container then
            self.Container.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(tonumber(transparency) or self.Container.BackgroundTransparency, 0, 1)
        end
    end

    function controller:SetImageTransparency(transparency)
        if self.ImageLabel then
            self.ImageLabel.ImageTransparency = math.clamp(tonumber(transparency) or self.ImageLabel.ImageTransparency, 0, 1)
        end
    end

    function controller:SetZIndex(newZIndex)
        local resolvedZ = tonumber(newZIndex)
        if not resolvedZ then
            return
        end
        if self.Container then
            self.Container.ZIndex = resolvedZ
        end
        if self.ImageLabel then
            self.ImageLabel.ZIndex = resolvedZ + 1
        end
    end

    function controller:SetPlaybackRate(rate)
        self.PlaybackRate = math.max(0.01, tonumber(rate) or self.PlaybackRate or 1)
    end

    function controller:Destroy()
        self._destroyed = true
        self.IsPlaying = false
        if self.Container and self.Container.Parent then
            self.Container:Destroy()
        end
        self.Container = nil
        self.ImageLabel = nil
        self.WarmHolder = nil
    end

    controller:_Preload()
    controller:_ApplyFrame(1)
    controller.Ready = true
    reportStatus("Ready", #controller.Frames)
    if videoConfig.AutoPlay ~= false then
        controller:Play()
    end

    self._videoLayers[layerName] = controller
    return controller
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_StepVideoLayers(dt)
    if type(self._videoLayers) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    for name, layer in pairs(self._videoLayers) do
        if type(layer) ~= "table" or layer._destroyed then
            self._videoLayers[name] = nil
        elseif layer.Ready and layer.IsPlaying then
            local frames = layer.Frames
            local currentFrame = frames[layer._frameIndex or 1]
            layer.TimePosition = (tonumber(layer.TimePosition) or 0) + ((tonumber(dt) or 0) * (tonumber(layer.PlaybackRate) or 1))

            while currentFrame and layer.TimePosition >= (currentFrame.Start + currentFrame.Duration) do
                local nextIndex = (layer._frameIndex or 1) + 1
                if nextIndex > #frames then
                    if layer.Loop then
                        layer.TimePosition = 0
                        nextIndex = 1
                    else
                        layer.TimePosition = layer.TotalDuration
                        layer.IsPlaying = false
                        if type(layer._HandleCompletion) == "function" then
                            layer:_HandleCompletion()
                        end
                        break
                    end
                end
                layer:_ApplyFrame(nextIndex)
                currentFrame = frames[layer._frameIndex or 1]
            end
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:GetVideoLayer(name)
    if type(self._videoLayers) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    return self._videoLayers[tostring(name or "")]
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:DestroyVideoLayer(nameOrLayer)
    local targetName = nil
    local layer = nil
    if type(nameOrLayer) == "table" then
        layer = nameOrLayer
        targetName = layer.Name
    else
        targetName = tostring(nameOrLayer or "")
        layer = self:GetVideoLayer(targetName)
    end
    if layer and type(layer.Destroy) == "function" then
        layer:Destroy()
    end
    if targetName and self._videoLayers then
        self._videoLayers[targetName] = nil
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:SetBackgroundVideo(videoConfig)
    videoConfig = type(videoConfig) == "table" and videoConfig or {}
    videoConfig.Target = videoConfig.Target or "Background"
    videoConfig.Name = videoConfig.Name or "BackgroundVideo"
    videoConfig.ScaleType = videoConfig.ScaleType or Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    videoConfig.ZIndex = videoConfig.ZIndex or 0
    if videoConfig.EnableBackgroundEffects ~= false then
        self:_SetBackgroundEffectsEnabled(true)
    end
    if videoConfig.HideBackgroundImage ~= false and self.mainBackgroundBrandImage then
        self.mainBackgroundBrandImage.Visible = false
    end
    return self:CreateVideoLayer(videoConfig)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:SetIntroVideo(videoConfig)
    videoConfig = type(videoConfig) == "table" and videoConfig or {}
    videoConfig.Target = videoConfig.Target or "Intro"
    videoConfig.Name = videoConfig.Name or "IntroVideo"
    videoConfig.BackgroundTransparency = videoConfig.BackgroundTransparency
    if videoConfig.BackgroundTransparency == nil then
        videoConfig.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    end
    return self:CreateVideoLayer(videoConfig)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:SetLoadingVideo(videoConfig)
    videoConfig = type(videoConfig) == "table" and videoConfig or {}
    videoConfig.Target = videoConfig.Target or "Loading"
    videoConfig.Name = videoConfig.Name or "LoadingVideo"
    videoConfig.BackgroundTransparency = videoConfig.BackgroundTransparency
    if videoConfig.BackgroundTransparency == nil then
        videoConfig.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    end
    return self:CreateVideoLayer(videoConfig)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_CollectConfiguredVideoLayerConfigs()
    local layers = {}

    local function addLayer(label, config)
        if type(config) == "table" then
            table.insert(layers, {
                Label = label,
                Config = config
            })
        end
    end

    addLayer("BackgroundVideo", self._configuredBackgroundVideo)
    addLayer("IntroVideo", self._configuredIntroVideo)
    addLayer("LoadingVideo", self._configuredLoadingVideo)

    if type(self._configuredVideoLayers) == "table" then
        for key, layerConfig in pairs(self._configuredVideoLayers) do
            if type(layerConfig) == "table" then
                addLayer(tostring(layerConfig.Name or key or "VideoLayer"), layerConfig)
            end
        end
    end

    return layers
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_PrepareVideoLayerAssets(videoConfig, label)
    videoConfig = type(videoConfig) == "table" and videoConfig or {}
    if videoConfig.PreloadBeforeUI == false or videoConfig.PrepareBeforeUI == false then
        return true
    end
    if type(videoConfig._NexusPreparedVideoAssets) == "table" then
        return true
    end

    local manifestSource = videoConfig.Manifest or videoConfig.ManifestUrl or videoConfig.Source
    if manifestSource == nil then
        return false, "missing manifest source"
    end

    local manifestRequestSource, authHeaders, apiKey = self:_ResolveVideoManifestRequest(videoConfig, manifestSource)
        print("[Nexus UI Video] Preparing " .. tostring(label or videoConfig.Name or "VideoLayer") .. " manifest")

    local okManifest, manifestOrError = self:_LoadVideoManifest(manifestRequestSource, authHeaders)
    if not okManifest then
        return false, manifestOrError
    end

    local manifest = manifestOrError
    local assetsByUrl = {}
    local preloadTargets = {}
    local warmImages = {}

    local function resolveAssetUrl(assetUrl)
        local resolvedUrl = self:_ResolveVideoManifestUrl(manifestRequestSource, assetUrl)
        if self:_ShouldUseVideoQueryKey(videoConfig) then
            resolvedUrl = appendNexusUIQueryValue(resolvedUrl, tostring(videoConfig.QueryKeyName or "api_key"), apiKey)
        end
        return resolvedUrl
    end

    local function prepareAsset(assetUrl, kind, index, total)
        local resolvedUrl = resolveAssetUrl(assetUrl)
        print(string.format("[Nexus UI Video] Caching %s %d/%d for %s", kind, index, total, tostring(label or videoConfig.Name or "VideoLayer")))
        local okAsset, asset, assetStateOrError = self:_ResolveVideoAsset(resolvedUrl, authHeaders)
        if not okAsset then
            return false, assetStateOrError
        end
        assetsByUrl[resolvedUrl] = {
            Asset = asset,
            State = assetStateOrError
        }
        local warmImage = makeThingExist("ImageLabel", {
            Name = "WarmVideoPreload" .. tostring(index),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            ImageTransparency = 1,
            Image = asset,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
        })
        table.insert(preloadTargets, warmImage)
        table.insert(warmImages, warmImage)
        return true
    end

    if manifest.kind == "sheet_sequence" then
        if type(manifest.sheets) ~= "table" or #manifest.sheets == 0 then
            return false, "sheet manifest has no sheets"
        end
        for index, sheet in ipairs(manifest.sheets) do
            local okAsset, errAsset = prepareAsset(sheet.url or sheet.file, "sheet", index, #manifest.sheets)
            if not okAsset then
                return false, errAsset
            end
        end
    else
        for index, frame in ipairs(manifest.frames) do
            local okAsset, errAsset = prepareAsset(frame.url or frame.file, "frame", index, #manifest.frames)
            if not okAsset then
                return false, errAsset
            end
        end
    end

    local batchSize = math.max(1, tonumber(videoConfig.PreloadBatchSize) or 24)
    local index = 1
    while index <= #preloadTargets do
        local batch = {}
        for batchIndex = index, math.min(#preloadTargets, index + batchSize - 1) do
            table.insert(batch, preloadTargets[batchIndex])
        end
        print(string.format("[Nexus UI Video] Preloading asset batch %d/%d for %s", math.ceil(index / batchSize), math.ceil(#preloadTargets / batchSize), tostring(label or videoConfig.Name or "VideoLayer")))
        local okPreload, preloadError = pcall(function()
            contentProviderIsCool:PreloadAsync(batch)
        end)
        if not okPreload then
            warn("[Nexus UI Video] PreloadAsync warning: " .. tostring(preloadError))
        end
        index = index + batchSize
        task.wait()
    end

    for _, warmImage in ipairs(warmImages) do
        pcall(function()
            warmImage:Destroy()
        end)
    end

    videoConfig._NexusPreparedVideoAssets = {
        Manifest = manifest,
        ManifestSource = manifestRequestSource,
        Headers = authHeaders,
        ApiKey = apiKey,
        AssetsByUrl = assetsByUrl
    }

    print("[Nexus UI Video] Prepared " .. tostring(label or videoConfig.Name or "VideoLayer"))
    return true
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_PrepareConfiguredVideoAssets()
    if self._configuredVideoAssetsPrepared then
        return
    end
    self._configuredVideoAssetsPrepared = true

    local layers = self:_CollectConfiguredVideoLayerConfigs()
    if #layers == 0 then
        return
    end

    print("[Nexus UI Video] Preparing " .. tostring(#layers) .. " configured video layer(s) before UI creation")
    for _, layerInfo in ipairs(layers) do
        local okPrepare, errPrepare = pcall(function()
            local okLayer, layerError = self:_PrepareVideoLayerAssets(layerInfo.Config, layerInfo.Label)
            if not okLayer then
                error(layerError, 0)
            end
        end)
        if not okPrepare then
            warn("[Nexus UI Video] Failed to prepare " .. tostring(layerInfo.Label) .. ": " .. tostring(errPrepare))
        end
    end
    print("[Nexus UI Video] Video asset preparation finished; creating UI")
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_CreateConfiguredVideoLayers()
    if self._configuredVideoLayersCreated then
        return
    end
    self._configuredVideoLayersCreated = true

    if type(self._configuredBackgroundVideo) == "table" then
        pcall(function()
            self:SetBackgroundVideo(self._configuredBackgroundVideo)
        end)
    end
    if type(self._configuredIntroVideo) == "table" then
        pcall(function()
            self:SetIntroVideo(self._configuredIntroVideo)
        end)
    end
    if type(self._configuredLoadingVideo) == "table" then
        pcall(function()
            self:SetLoadingVideo(self._configuredLoadingVideo)
        end)
    end
    if type(self._configuredVideoLayers) == "table" then
        for key, layerConfig in pairs(self._configuredVideoLayers) do
            if type(layerConfig) == "table" then
                if layerConfig.Name == nil and type(key) == "string" then
                    layerConfig.Name = key
                end
                pcall(function()
                    self:CreateVideoLayer(layerConfig)
                end)
            end
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ApplyOpenCloseVisuals(instant)
    self:_SetBlurActive(self.isToggledOn, instant)
    if self.backdropDimFrame then
        local targetTransparency = (self.isToggledOn and self._uiVisualSettings.Blur) and 0.55 or 1
        if instant then
            self.backdropDimFrame.BackgroundTransparency = targetTransparency
        else
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.backdropDimFrame, {
                BackgroundTransparency = targetTransparency
            }, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        end
    end
    if self.mainBackgroundEffectsFrame then
        self.mainBackgroundEffectsFrame.Visible = self.isToggledOn and self._uiVisualSettings.BackgroundEffects
    end
    self:_ApplyBackgroundEffectsOpacity(instant)
    if not self.isToggledOn or not self._uiVisualSettings.BackgroundEffects then
        self._backgroundFxAccumulator = 0
    end
    if self.snowLayerFrame then
        self.snowLayerFrame.Visible = self.isToggledOn and self._uiVisualSettings.Snow and self._overlayMode ~= "None"
    end
    if not self.isToggledOn or not self._uiVisualSettings.Snow or self._overlayMode == "None" then
        self._overlayUpdateAccumulator = 0
        self._snowSpawnAccumulator = 0
        self._snowFrostExposure = 0
        self._snowPhysicsMoveAccumulator = 0
        self:_SetSnowFrostIntensity(0, true)
        self:_ClearSnowflakes()
    end
    local activeTabName = "READY"
    if self.currentTabYay and self.currentTabYay.displayName then
        activeTabName = self.currentTabYay.displayName
    elseif self.currentTabYay and self.currentTabYay.tabNameForSearch then
        activeTabName = self.currentTabYay.tabNameForSearch
    end
    self:_SetWatermarkStatus(
        activeTabName,
        self.isToggledOn and (self.config.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(2, 133, 255)) or Color3.fromRGB(142, 147, 158)
    )
    self:_UpdateESPPreview(0)
end

local function NexusPreviewPullValue(sourceTable, keys)
    if type(sourceTable) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    for _, key in ipairs(keys) do
        local value = sourceTable[key]
        if value ~= nil then
            return value
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function NexusPreviewToBool(value)
    if type(value) == "boolean" then
        return value
    end
    if type(value) == "number" then
        return value ~= 0
    end
    if type(value) == "string" then
        local normalized = string.lower(value)
        if normalized == "true" or normalized == "on" or normalized == "enabled" or normalized == "yes" or normalized == "1" then
            return true
        end
        if normalized == "false" or normalized == "off" or normalized == "disabled" or normalized == "no" or normalized == "0" then
            return false
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function NexusPreviewToColor3(value)
    if typeof(value) == "Color3" then
        return value
    end
    if type(value) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    local r = value.r or value.R or value[1]
    local g = value.g or value.G or value[2]
    local b = value.b or value.B or value[3]
    if type(r) ~= "number" or type(g) ~= "number" or type(b) ~= "number" then
        return nil
    end
    if r > 1 or g > 1 or b > 1 then
        r, g, b = r / 255, g / 255, b / 255
    end
    return Color3.new(math.clamp(r, 0, 1), math.clamp(g, 0, 1), math.clamp(b, 0, 1))
end

local NexusPreviewCoreBodyParts = {
    Head = true,
    UpperTorso = true,
    LowerTorso = true,
    Torso = true,
    LeftUpperArm = true,
    LeftLowerArm = true,
    LeftHand = true,
    RightUpperArm = true,
    RightLowerArm = true,
    RightHand = true,
    LeftUpperLeg = true,
    LeftLowerLeg = true,
    LeftFoot = true,
    RightUpperLeg = true,
    RightLowerLeg = true,
    RightFoot = true,
    ["Left Arm"] = true,
    ["Right Arm"] = true,
    ["Left Leg"] = true,
    ["Right Leg"] = true
}

local NexusPreviewBoundingCornerSigns = {
    Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1),
    Vector3.new(-1, -1, 1),
    Vector3.new(-1, 1, -1),
    Vector3.new(-1, 1, 1),
    Vector3.new(1, -1, -1),
    Vector3.new(1, -1, 1),
    Vector3.new(1, 1, -1),
    Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
}

local function getModelBoundingBoxSafe(model)
    if not model or not model.Parent then
        return nil, nil
    end
    local okBounds, boundsCF, boundsSize = pcall(function()
        return model:GetBoundingBox()
    end)
    if okBounds and typeof(boundsCF) == "CFrame" and typeof(boundsSize) == "Vector3" and boundsSize.Magnitude > 0 then
        return boundsCF, boundsSize
    end
    return nil, nil
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:SetESPPreviewProvider(providerFn)
    if providerFn ~= nil and type(providerFn) ~= "function" then
        return false, "ESP preview provider must be a function or nil."
    end
    self._espPreviewProvider = providerFn
    self._espPreviewResolveAccumulator = 0.2
    return true
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:SetESPPreviewData(previewData)
    if previewData ~= nil and type(previewData) ~= "table" then
        return false, "ESP preview data must be a table or nil."
    end
    self._espPreviewData = previewData
    self._espPreviewResolveAccumulator = 0.2
    return true
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:SetESPPreviewEnabled(enabled)
    self._uiVisualSettings.ESPSelfPreview = enabled == true
    if not self._uiVisualSettings.ESPSelfPreview then
        self._espPreviewWasShowing = false
        self._espPreviewUpdateAccumulator = 0
        self:_DestroyESPPreviewCharacter()
    else
        self:_InvalidateESPPreviewProjection(true)
    end
    self:_UpdateESPPreview(0)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ResolveESPPreviewWalkAnimationId(sourceCharacter)
    if not sourceCharacter then
        return nil
    end
    local animateScript = sourceCharacter:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if animateScript then
        local preferredFolders = {"walk", "run"}
        for _, folderName in ipairs(preferredFolders) do
            local folder = animateScript:FindFirstChild(folderName)
            if folder then
                local directAnimation = folder:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Animation")
                if directAnimation and type(directAnimation.AnimationId) == "string" and directAnimation.AnimationId ~= "" then
                    return directAnimation.AnimationId
                end
                for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
                    if child:IsA("Animation") and type(child.AnimationId) == "string" and child.AnimationId ~= "" then
                        return child.AnimationId
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(animateScript:GetDescendants()) do
            if child:IsA("Animation") then
                local childName = string.lower(child.Name or "")
                if (string.find(childName, "walk", 1, true) or string.find(childName, "run", 1, true))
                    and type(child.AnimationId) == "string"
                    and child.AnimationId ~= "" then
                    return child.AnimationId
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_StartESPPreviewWalkAnimation(sourceCharacter)
    if self._espPreviewStaticMode then
        if self._espPreviewWalkTrack then
            pcall(function()
                self._espPreviewWalkTrack:Stop(0.1)
            end)
            pcall(function()
                self._espPreviewWalkTrack:Destroy()
            end)
            self._espPreviewWalkTrack = nil
        end
        self._espPreviewAnimationId = nil
        return
    end
    if not self._espPreviewCharacter or not self._espPreviewCharacter.Parent then
        return
    end
    if self._espPreviewWalkTrack and self._espPreviewWalkTrack.IsPlaying and self._espPreviewAnimationId then
        pcall(function()
            self._espPreviewWalkTrack:AdjustSpeed(0.95)
        end)
        return
    end
    local previewHumanoid = self._espPreviewCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not previewHumanoid then
        return
    end
    local resolvedId = self:_ResolveESPPreviewWalkAnimationId(sourceCharacter)
    if not resolvedId then
        return
    end
    if self._espPreviewWalkTrack and self._espPreviewAnimationId ~= resolvedId then
        pcall(function()
            self._espPreviewWalkTrack:Stop(0.1)
        end)
        pcall(function()
            self._espPreviewWalkTrack:Destroy()
        end)
        self._espPreviewWalkTrack = nil
    end

    local animator = previewHumanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then
        animator = Instance.new("Animator")
        animator.Parent = previewHumanoid
    end
    if not self._espPreviewWalkTrack then
        local walkAnimation = Instance.new("Animation")
        walkAnimation.AnimationId = resolvedId
        local okTrack, track = pcall(function()
            return animator:LoadAnimation(walkAnimation)
        end)
        walkAnimation:Destroy()
        if not okTrack or not track then
            return
        end
        track.Looped = true
        self._espPreviewWalkTrack = track
        self._espPreviewAnimationId = resolvedId
    end
    if self._espPreviewWalkTrack and not self._espPreviewWalkTrack.IsPlaying then
        pcall(function()
            self._espPreviewWalkTrack:Play(0.15, 1, 1)
        end)
    end
    if self._espPreviewWalkTrack then
        pcall(function()
            self._espPreviewWalkTrack:AdjustSpeed(0.95)
        end)
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_CreateESPPreviewFallbackCharacter()
    local fallbackModel = nil
    local created = false
    local previewUserId, previewImageUrl = resolveAvatar3DUserIdFromRobloxEndpoint(theOneAndOnlyPlayer.UserId)
    self._espPreviewAvatar3DUserId = previewUserId
    self._espPreviewAvatar3DImageUrl = previewImageUrl

    local okCreate = pcall(function()
        fallbackModel = playersAreNoobs:CreateHumanoidModelFromUserId(previewUserId)
    end)
    if not okCreate or not fallbackModel then
        local okDescription, humanoidDescription = pcall(function()
            return playersAreNoobs:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(previewUserId)
        end)
        if okDescription and humanoidDescription then
            pcall(function()
                fallbackModel = playersAreNoobs:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(humanoidDescription, Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
                created = true
            end)
        end
    end
    if fallbackModel then
        fallbackModel.Name = "NexusESPSelfPreviewAvatar"
        if created and not fallbackModel:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
            fallbackModel:Destroy()
            return nil
        end
    end
    return fallbackModel
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_DestroyESPPreviewCharacter()
    if self._espPreviewWalkTrack then
        pcall(function()
            self._espPreviewWalkTrack:Stop(0.1)
        end)
        pcall(function()
            self._espPreviewWalkTrack:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    self._espPreviewWalkTrack = nil
    self._espPreviewAnimationId = nil
    if self._espPreviewHighlight then
        self._espPreviewHighlight.Adornee = nil
        self._espPreviewHighlight.Enabled = false
    end
    self._espPreviewPartDefaults = {}
    self._espPreviewHeadPart = nil
    self._espPreviewRootPart = nil
    self._espPreviewVisualState = nil
    self._espPreviewVisualDirty = false
    self._espPreviewProjectionCache = nil
    self._espPreviewProjectionDirty = true
    if self._espPreviewCharacter and self._espPreviewCharacter.Parent then
        self._espPreviewCharacter:Destroy()
    end
    self._espPreviewCharacter = nil
    self._espPreviewLastCharacter = nil
    self._espPreviewPivotYOffset = -2
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_EnsureESPPreviewCharacter()
    if not self._espPreviewWorldModel then
        return
    end
    local liveCharacter = theOneAndOnlyPlayer.Character
    if self._espPreviewCharacter and self._espPreviewCharacter.Parent then
        if liveCharacter and self._espPreviewLastCharacter == liveCharacter then
            return
        end
        if not liveCharacter and self._espPreviewLastCharacter == false then
            return
        end
    end
    self:_DestroyESPPreviewCharacter()

    local function cloneFromLiveCharacter()
        if not liveCharacter then
            return nil
        end
        local previousArchivable = liveCharacter.Archivable
        local cloneCharacter = nil
        local clonedOk = pcall(function()
            liveCharacter.Archivable = true
            cloneCharacter = liveCharacter:Clone()
        end)
        pcall(function()
            liveCharacter.Archivable = previousArchivable
        end)
        if not clonedOk then
            return nil
        end
        return cloneCharacter
    end

    local function preparePreviewCharacter(model)
        if not model then
            return false
        end
        self._espPreviewPartDefaults = {}
        local rootPart = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            or model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            or model:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        local corePartCount = 0
        local visibleCorePartCount = 0
        local visiblePartCount = 0
        for _, obj in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Script") or obj:IsA("LocalScript") then
                obj:Destroy()
            elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                local isRootPart = obj.Name == "HumanoidRootPart"
                local isCoreBodyPart = NexusPreviewCoreBodyParts[obj.Name] == true
                local sanitizedTransparency = math.clamp(tonumber(obj.Transparency) or 0, 0, 1)
                if isRootPart then
                    sanitizedTransparency = 1
                elseif isCoreBodyPart and sanitizedTransparency >= 0.98 then
                    sanitizedTransparency = 0
                end
                self._espPreviewPartDefaults[obj] = {
                    Color = obj.Color,
                    Material = obj.Material,
                    Transparency = sanitizedTransparency
                }
                obj.Transparency = sanitizedTransparency
                pcall(function()
                    obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                end)
                if isRootPart then
                    pcall(function()
                        obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                    end)
                end
                obj.CanCollide = false
                pcall(function()
                    obj.CanQuery = false
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    obj.CanTouch = false
                end)
                obj.Massless = true
                obj.CastShadow = false
                if rootPart == nil then
                    rootPart = obj
                end
                obj.Anchored = obj == rootPart

                if sanitizedTransparency < 0.985 and obj.Size.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                    visiblePartCount = visiblePartCount + 1
                end
                if isCoreBodyPart then
                    corePartCount = corePartCount + 1
                    if sanitizedTransparency < 0.985 then
                        visibleCorePartCount = visibleCorePartCount + 1
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local headPart = model:FindFirstChild("Head")
            or model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            or model:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            or rootPart

        if corePartCount > 0 and visibleCorePartCount < math.min(4, corePartCount) then
            for part, originalData in pairs(self._espPreviewPartDefaults) do
                if part and part.Parent and originalData and NexusPreviewCoreBodyParts[part.Name] then
                    originalData.Transparency = 0
                    part.Transparency = 0
                end
            end
            visibleCorePartCount = math.max(visibleCorePartCount, math.min(4, corePartCount))
        end

        if visiblePartCount < 6 then
            for _, obj in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Transparency >= 0.98 and obj.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                    local originalData = self._espPreviewPartDefaults[obj]
                    if originalData then
                        originalData.Transparency = 0
                    end
                    obj.Transparency = 0
                end
            end
            visiblePartCount = 6
        end

        self._espPreviewHeadPart = headPart
        self._espPreviewRootPart = rootPart or headPart
        self._espPreviewVisualDirty = true
        self:_InvalidateESPPreviewProjection(true)
        if rootPart then
            pcall(function()
                model.PrimaryPart = rootPart
            end)
        end

        if corePartCount > 0 and corePartCount < 3 then
            return false
        end
        if corePartCount >= 3 and visibleCorePartCount < 3 then
            return false
        end
        return visiblePartCount >= 6
    end

    local previewCharacter = self:_CreateESPPreviewFallbackCharacter()
    local hasReliableAvatar = preparePreviewCharacter(previewCharacter)
    if not hasReliableAvatar and previewCharacter and previewCharacter.Parent == nil then
        previewCharacter:Destroy()
        previewCharacter = nil
    end

    if not previewCharacter then
        previewCharacter = cloneFromLiveCharacter()
        local hasReliableClone = preparePreviewCharacter(previewCharacter)
        if not hasReliableClone and previewCharacter and previewCharacter.Parent == nil then
            previewCharacter:Destroy()
            previewCharacter = nil
        end
    end
    if not previewCharacter then
        return
    end

    previewCharacter.Parent = self._espPreviewWorldModel
    local previewHumanoid = previewCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if previewHumanoid then
        previewHumanoid.AutoRotate = false
        previewHumanoid.PlatformStand = false
        pcall(function()
            previewHumanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
        end)
    end
    local previewExtents = Vector3.new(4, 6, 3)
    local okPreviewExtents, calculatedPreviewExtents = pcall(function()
        return previewCharacter:GetExtentsSize()
    end)
    if okPreviewExtents and calculatedPreviewExtents then
        previewExtents = calculatedPreviewExtents
    end
    local pivotYOffset = self._espPreviewStaticMode and 0 or -math.clamp(previewExtents.Y * 0.43, 1.2, 3.2)
    self._espPreviewPivotYOffset = pivotYOffset
    local allowPreviewRotate = self._espPreviewAllowManualRotation == true
    local initialYaw = (self._espPreviewStaticMode and not allowPreviewRotate) and math.rad(180) or (tonumber(self._espPreviewRotationYaw) or tonumber(self._espPreviewRotationTargetYaw) or math.rad(180))
    self._espPreviewRotationYaw = initialYaw
    self._espPreviewRotationTargetYaw = initialYaw
    pcall(function()
        previewCharacter:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, pivotYOffset, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, initialYaw, 0))
    end)
    self._espPreviewCharacter = previewCharacter
    self._espPreviewLastCharacter = liveCharacter or false
    if self._espPreviewHighlight then
        self._espPreviewHighlight.Adornee = previewCharacter
    end

    if self._espPreviewCamera then
        if self._espPreviewStaticMode then
            self._espPreviewCamera.FieldOfView = 30
            local boundsCF, boundsSize = getModelBoundingBoxSafe(previewCharacter)
            if boundsCF and boundsSize then
                local boundsCenter = boundsCF.Position
                local viewportSize = self._espPreviewViewport and self._espPreviewViewport.AbsoluteSize or Vector2.new(1, 1)
                local aspectRatio = math.max(0.5, viewportSize.X / math.max(1, viewportSize.Y))
                local verticalHalfFov = math.rad(self._espPreviewCamera.FieldOfView) * 0.5
                local horizontalHalfFov = math.atan(math.tan(verticalHalfFov) * aspectRatio)
                local fitDepthY = (boundsSize.Y * 0.5) / math.max(0.001, math.tan(verticalHalfFov))
                local fitDepthX = (boundsSize.X * 0.5) / math.max(0.001, math.tan(horizontalHalfFov))
                local depth = math.max(fitDepthX, fitDepthY) + boundsSize.Z * 0.92 + 1.25
                depth = math.clamp(depth, 7.2, 16.2)
                local focus = boundsCenter + Vector3.new(0, math.clamp(boundsSize.Y * 0.03, -0.08, 0.25), 0)
                self._espPreviewCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(focus + Vector3.new(0, math.clamp(boundsSize.Y * 0.03, -0.08, 0.35), depth), focus)
            else
                local fallbackFocus = Vector3.new(0, 0.45, 0)
                self._espPreviewCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(0, fallbackFocus.Y, 9.2), fallbackFocus)
            end
        else
            local focus = Vector3.new(0, math.clamp(previewExtents.Y * 0.36, 1.2, 2.45), 0)
            local depth = math.clamp(math.max(6.8, previewExtents.Y * 1.05 + previewExtents.X * 0.65), 6.8, 18)
            self._espPreviewCamera.FieldOfView = 32
            self._espPreviewCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(0, focus.Y, depth), focus)
        end
    end
    if not self._espPreviewStaticMode then
        self:_StartESPPreviewWalkAnimation(liveCharacter)
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_CreateESPPreviewPanel()
    if self._espPreviewPanel and self._espPreviewPanel.Parent then
        return
    end
    if not self.screenGuiYay or not self.screenGuiYay.Parent then
        return
    end

    local panelWidth = 226 * mobileScaleFactor
    local panelHeight = 296 * mobileScaleFactor
    local defaultPanelPosition = UDim2.new(0, 14 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 66 * mobileScaleFactor)
    if self.mainFrameYay and self.mainFrameYay.Parent then
        local mainPos = self.mainFrameYay.Position
        defaultPanelPosition = UDim2.new(
            mainPos.X.Scale,
            mainPos.X.Offset - panelWidth - 12 * mobileScaleFactor,
            mainPos.Y.Scale,
            mainPos.Y.Offset + 42 * mobileScaleFactor
        )
    end
    local panel = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        Name = "NexusESPSelfPreviewPanel",
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 16),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.06,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = defaultPanelPosition,
        Size = UDim2.new(0, panelWidth, 0, panelHeight),
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 30,
        Parent = self.screenGuiYay
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10), Parent = panel})
    makeThingExist("UIStroke", {Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 44), Thickness = 1.1, Parent = panel})
    makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
        Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 24)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 14))
        }),
        Rotation = 90,
        Parent = panel
    })
    local panelDragHandle = makeThingExist("TextButton", {
        Name = "ESPPreviewDragHandle",
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Active = true,
        ZIndex = 36,
        Parent = panel
    })

    makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 10 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 8 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 110 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 16 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Text = "ESP Preview",
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240),
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal),
        TextSize = 13 * mobileScaleFactor,
        ZIndex = 31,
        Parent = panel
    })

    self._espPreviewHeaderTag = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0.42, 0, 0, 8 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(0.58, -8 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 16 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Text = "@" .. tostring(theOneAndOnlyPlayer.Name),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(156, 156, 164),
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Medium, Enum.FontStyle.Normal),
        TextSize = 12 * mobileScaleFactor,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        ZIndex = 31,
        Parent = panel
    })

    local card = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(11, 11, 13),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 8 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 28 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -16 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, -34 * mobileScaleFactor),
        ZIndex = 30,
        Parent = panel
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8), Parent = card})
    makeThingExist("UIStroke", {Color = Color3.fromRGB(33, 33, 37), Parent = card})

    local viewport = makeThingExist("ViewportFrame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 18),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 6 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 6 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -12 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, -44 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Active = true,
        LightColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        LightDirection = Vector3.new(-0.34, -1, -0.22),
        Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(122, 122, 130),
        ZIndex = 31,
        Parent = card
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7), Parent = viewport})
    local worldModel = makeThingExist("WorldModel", {Parent = viewport})
    local camera = makeThingExist("Camera", {
        CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(0, 1.4, 7.2), Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)),
        Parent = viewport
    })
    viewport.CurrentCamera = camera

    local rotateCapture = makeThingExist("TextButton", {
        Name = "ESPPreviewRotateCapture",
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        Active = self._espPreviewAllowManualRotation == true,
        Selectable = false,
        ZIndex = 35,
        Parent = viewport
    })

    self._espPreviewBox = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0.18, 0, 0.07, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0.64, 0, 0.84, 0),
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 33,
        Parent = viewport
    })
    self._espPreviewBoxStroke = makeThingExist("UIStroke", {
        Color = self.config.AccentColor,
        Thickness = 1.5,
        Parent = self._espPreviewBox
    })

    self._espPreviewHealthTrack = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 32),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0.15, 0, 0.07, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 3 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.84, 0),
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 33,
        Parent = viewport
    })
    self._espPreviewHealthFill = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 100),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        ZIndex = 34,
        Parent = self._espPreviewHealthTrack
    })

    self._espPreviewDot = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = self.config.AccentColor,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -3 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.27, -3 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 6 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 6 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 34,
        Parent = viewport
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = self._espPreviewDot})

    self._espPreviewTracer = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = self.config.AccentColor,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -1 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.58, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 2 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.34, 0),
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 33,
        Parent = viewport
    })

    self._espPreviewName = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 1 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 15 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Text = tostring(theOneAndOnlyPlayer.Name),
        TextColor3 = self.config.AccentColor,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal),
        TextSize = 11 * mobileScaleFactor,
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 34,
        Parent = viewport
    })

    self._espPreviewDistance = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -18 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Text = "0m",
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(186, 186, 192),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Medium, Enum.FontStyle.Normal),
        TextSize = 11 * mobileScaleFactor,
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 34,
        Parent = card
    })

    self._espPreviewItem = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -32 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Text = "None",
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(206, 206, 212),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Medium, Enum.FontStyle.Normal),
        TextSize = 11 * mobileScaleFactor,
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 34,
        Parent = card
    })

    self._espPreviewHighlight = makeThingExist("Highlight", {
        Enabled = false,
        DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop,
        Parent = worldModel
    })

    self._espPreviewPanel = panel
    self._espPreviewViewport = viewport
    self._espPreviewWorldModel = worldModel
    self._espPreviewCamera = camera
    self._espPreviewRotateCapture = rotateCapture
    self:_TrackConnection(viewport:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
        self:_InvalidateESPPreviewProjection(false)
    end))

    if self._espPreviewAllowManualRotation == true then
        local function stopESPPreviewRotate()
            self._espPreviewIsRotating = false
            self._espPreviewRotateInput = nil
        end

        self:_TrackConnection(rotateCapture.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            local isMouse = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            local isTouch = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            if not isMouse and not isTouch then
                return
            end
            self._espPreviewIsRotating = true
            self._espPreviewRotateInput = isTouch and input or nil
            self._espPreviewRotateLastX = input.Position.X
        end))

        self:_TrackConnection(rotateCapture.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                stopESPPreviewRotate()
                return
            end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and (self._espPreviewRotateInput == nil or input == self._espPreviewRotateInput) then
                stopESPPreviewRotate()
            end
        end))

        self:_TrackConnection(thisInputServiceGoesHard.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if not self._espPreviewIsRotating then
                return
            end
            local isMouseMove = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and self._espPreviewRotateInput == nil
            local isTouchMove = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and (self._espPreviewRotateInput == nil or input == self._espPreviewRotateInput)
            if not isMouseMove and not isTouchMove then
                return
            end

            local previousX = tonumber(self._espPreviewRotateLastX) or input.Position.X
            local deltaX = input.Position.X - previousX
            self._espPreviewRotateLastX = input.Position.X

            local rotationSensitivity = isMobileUserSadly and 0.02 or 0.015
            self._espPreviewRotationTargetYaw = (tonumber(self._espPreviewRotationTargetYaw) or math.rad(180)) - deltaX * rotationSensitivity
        end))

        self:_TrackConnection(thisInputServiceGoesHard.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if not self._espPreviewIsRotating then
                return
            end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                stopESPPreviewRotate()
                return
            end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and (self._espPreviewRotateInput == nil or input == self._espPreviewRotateInput) then
                stopESPPreviewRotate()
            end
        end))
    else
        self._espPreviewIsRotating = false
        self._espPreviewRotateInput = nil
        self._espPreviewRotateLastX = 0
    end

    makeDraggableCuzWeCan(panel, panelDragHandle, self)
    self:_EnsureESPPreviewCharacter()
    self:_UpdateESPPreview(0)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_ResolveESPPreviewState()
    local state = {
        Enabled = true,
        ShowBox = false,
        ShowHealth = false,
        ShowName = false,
        UseDisplayName = false,
        ShowItem = false,
        ShowDistance = false,
        ShowTracers = false,
        ShowDot = false,
        ShowChams = false,
        ShowHighlight = false,
        ChamsTransparency = 0.55,
        HighlightFillTransparency = 0.5,
        HighlightOutlineTransparency = 0,
        Color = self.config.AccentColor,
        Name = tostring(theOneAndOnlyPlayer.Name),
