                                )
                            end
                            if self._overlayPhysicsEnabled and landingEntry then
                                self:_DepositSnowOnTarget(landingEntry, flakeCenterX, flakeData.depositAmount)
                                flakeObj:Destroy()
                                table.remove(self._snowflakes, i)
                            elseif flakeData.yOffset >= bottomLimitY then
                                flakeObj:Destroy()
                                table.remove(self._snowflakes, i)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            self._overlayUpdateAccumulator = 0
            self._snowFrostExposure = 0
            self:_SetSnowFrostIntensity(0, false)
        end
    end))
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:ToggleUI()
    self.isToggledOn = not self.isToggledOn
    local openPosition = self._mainFrameOpenPosition or UDim2.new(0.5, -392 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.5, -262 * mobileScaleFactor)
    local closedPosition = self._mainFrameClosedPosition or UDim2.new(0.5, openPosition.X.Offset, 1.5, 0)
    smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.mainFrameYay, {
        Position = self.isToggledOn and openPosition or closedPosition
    }, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, self.isToggledOn and Enum.EasingDirection.Out or Enum.EasingDirection.In)
    self:_ApplyOpenCloseVisuals(false)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:SetToggleButtonVisible(visible)
    self.toggleButtonVisible = visible
    if self.mobileToggleFrame then
        self.mobileToggleFrame.Visible = visible
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:SetToggleKey(keyCode)
    self.toggleKeyCode = keyCode
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:SetUserCustomizationEnabled(enabled)
    self._allowUserCustomization = enabled == true
    if self.uiSettingsButtonFrame then
        self.uiSettingsButtonFrame.Visible = self._allowUserCustomization
        self.uiSettingsButtonFrame.Active = self._allowUserCustomization
    end
    if self.uiSettingsPanelFrame then
        if not self._allowUserCustomization then
            self.uiSettingsOpen = false
            if type(self._closeSettingsFontSelector) == "function" then
                pcall(self._closeSettingsFontSelector, true)
            end
            self.uiSettingsPanelFrame.Visible = false
            self.uiSettingsPanelFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 185 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 0)
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:CreateMobileToggleButton()
    if self.config.DynamicIslandClickable == true then
        self.mobileToggleFrame = nil
        self.mobileToggleIcon = nil
        return
    end

    local buttonSize = 48 * mobileScaleFactor
    local resolvedLogo = self._dynamicIslandLogoState and self._dynamicIslandLogoState.ResolvedImage or ""
    
    self.mobileToggleFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        Name = "MobileToggleButton",
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 16),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -buttonSize/2, 0, 10),
        BorderColor3 = Color3.new(),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(0, buttonSize, 0, buttonSize),
        Visible = self.toggleButtonVisible == true,
        Parent = self.screenGuiYay
    })
    
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {
        CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10),
        Parent = self.mobileToggleFrame
    })
    
    local mobileStroke = makeThingExist("UIStroke", {
        Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40),
        Thickness = 1.5,
        Parent = self.mobileToggleFrame
    })
    
    makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
        Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
        }),
        Rotation = 45,
        Parent = mobileStroke
    })
    
    self.mobileToggleIcon = makeThingExist("ImageLabel", {
        Name = "ToggleIcon",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Image = resolvedLogo,
        ImageColor3 = self.config.AccentColor,
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 28 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 28 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Visible = resolvedLogo ~= "",
        Parent = self.mobileToggleFrame
    })
    
    local glowEffect = makeThingExist("ImageLabel", {
        Name = "GlowEffect",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Image = "rbxassetid://5028857084",
        ImageColor3 = self.config.AccentColor,
        ImageTransparency = 0.7,
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.new(1.8, 0, 1.8, 0),
        ZIndex = 0,
        Parent = self.mobileToggleFrame
    })
    
    local mobileToggleBtn = makeThingExist("TextButton", {
        Name = "ClickButton",
        Text = "",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        Parent = self.mobileToggleFrame
    })
    
    mobileToggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        self:ToggleUI()
        
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.mobileToggleIcon, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 24 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 24 * mobileScaleFactor)}, 0.1)
        task.delay(0.1, function()
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.mobileToggleIcon, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 28 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 28 * mobileScaleFactor)}, 0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        end)
    end)
    
    mobileToggleBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.mobileToggleFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 24)}, 0.2)
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(glowEffect, {ImageTransparency = 0.5}, 0.2)
    end)
    
    mobileToggleBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.mobileToggleFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 16)}, 0.2)
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(glowEffect, {ImageTransparency = 0.7}, 0.2)
    end)
    
    makeDraggableCuzWeCan(self.mobileToggleFrame, nil, self)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:CreateWatermarkBecauseWeCool()
    local titleText = tostring(self.config.DynamicIslandTitle or self.config.Name or "NEXUS")
    local metaText = "00:00:00 | 60 FPS"
    local statusText = tostring(self._watermarkStatusText or "READY")
    local islandHeight = 44 * mobileScaleFactor
    local islandRadius = math.floor(islandHeight * 0.5 + 0.5)
    local function computeIslandWidth(metaValue, statusValue)
        local titleWidth = howWideIsThisText(titleText, 12 * mobileScaleFactor, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
        local metaWidth = howWideIsThisText(metaValue, 11 * mobileScaleFactor, Enum.Font.Code)
        local statusWidth = howWideIsThisText(statusValue, 11 * mobileScaleFactor, Enum.Font.Code)
        return math.clamp(
            math.max(
                286 * mobileScaleFactor,
                104 * mobileScaleFactor + math.max(titleWidth, metaWidth) + statusWidth + (90 * mobileScaleFactor)
            ),
            286 * mobileScaleFactor,
            432 * mobileScaleFactor
        )
    end
    local initialWidth = computeIslandWidth(metaText, statusText)
    local resolvedLogo = self._dynamicIslandLogoState and self._dynamicIslandLogoState.ResolvedImage or ""

    self.watermarkFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        Name = "NexusDynamicIsland",
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 11, 14),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 7),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(0, initialWidth, 0, islandHeight),
        Parent = self.screenGuiYay
    })

    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, islandRadius), Parent = self.watermarkFrame})
    self.watermarkScale = makeThingExist("UIScale", {Scale = 1, Parent = self.watermarkFrame})
    self.watermarkStroke = makeThingExist("UIStroke", {
        Color = self.config.AccentColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(46, 50, 60), 0.64),
        Transparency = 0.16,
        Thickness = 1.15,
        Parent = self.watermarkFrame
    })
    makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 12,

        Parent = self.watermarkNotificationBadge
    })

        Name = "DynamicIslandButton",
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Active = self.config.DynamicIslandClickable == true,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        ZIndex = 4,
        Parent = self.watermarkFrame
    })
    if self.config.DynamicIslandClickable == true then
        self.watermarkClickButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if self._destroyed then
                return
            end
            self:ToggleUI()
            if self.watermarkScale and self.watermarkScale.Parent then
                self.watermarkScale.Scale = 0.97
                smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.watermarkScale, {Scale = 1}, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            end
        end)
        self.watermarkClickButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if self.watermarkStroke and self.watermarkStroke.Parent then
                smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.watermarkStroke, {
                    Color = self.config.AccentColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(68, 76, 92), 0.48)
                }, 0.16)
            end
            if self.watermarkGlow and self.watermarkGlow.Parent then
                smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.watermarkGlow, {ImageTransparency = 0.84}, 0.16)
            end
        end)
        self.watermarkClickButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            if self.watermarkStroke and self.watermarkStroke.Parent then
                smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.watermarkStroke, {
                    Color = self.config.AccentColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(46, 50, 60), 0.64)
                }, 0.18)
            end
            if self.watermarkGlow and self.watermarkGlow.Parent then
                smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.watermarkGlow, {ImageTransparency = 0.9}, 0.18)
            end
        end)
    end

    self._watermarkLastWidth = initialWidth
    self:_SetWatermarkStatus(statusText, self._watermarkStatusColor or self.config.AccentColor)
    self:_SetDynamicIslandNotificationCount(self._dynamicIslandNotificationCount or 0)
    self:_RefreshNotificationHolderPosition()
    makeDraggableCuzWeCan(self.watermarkFrame, self.watermarkClickButton or self.watermarkFrame, self)
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:_UpdateWatermark(dt)
    if not (self.watermarkFrame and self.watermarkFrame.Parent and self.watermarkMetaLabel and self.watermarkMetaLabel.Parent) then
        return
    end

    local islandHeight = 44 * mobileScaleFactor
    self._watermarkUpdateAccumulator = (self._watermarkUpdateAccumulator or 0) + (tonumber(dt) or 0)
    if self._watermarkUpdateAccumulator < 0.45 then
        return
    end
    self._watermarkUpdateAccumulator = 0

    local timeNow = os.date("%H:%M:%S")
    local fpsCount = self._latestFPSValue > 0 and self._latestFPSValue or 0
    local metaText = timeNow .. " | " .. fpsCount .. " FPS"
    if self.watermarkMetaLabel.Text ~= metaText then
        self.watermarkMetaLabel.Text = metaText
    end

    local titleText = tostring(self.config.DynamicIslandTitle or self.config.Name or "NEXUS")
    if self.watermarkTitleLabel and self.watermarkTitleLabel.Parent and self.watermarkTitleLabel.Text ~= titleText then
        self.watermarkTitleLabel.Text = titleText
    end

    if self.watermarkLogoImage and self.watermarkLogoImage.Parent then
        local resolvedLogo = self._dynamicIslandLogoState and self._dynamicIslandLogoState.ResolvedImage or ""
        self.watermarkLogoImage.Image = resolvedLogo
        self.watermarkLogoImage.Visible = resolvedLogo ~= ""
    end

    local statusText = tostring(self._watermarkStatusText or "READY")
    local titleWidth = howWideIsThisText(titleText, 12 * mobileScaleFactor, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
    local metaWidth = howWideIsThisText(metaText, 11 * mobileScaleFactor, Enum.Font.Code)
    local statusWidth = howWideIsThisText(statusText, 11 * mobileScaleFactor, Enum.Font.Code)
    local newWidth = math.clamp(
        math.max(
            286 * mobileScaleFactor,
            104 * mobileScaleFactor + math.max(titleWidth, metaWidth) + statusWidth + (90 * mobileScaleFactor)
        ),
        286 * mobileScaleFactor,
        432 * mobileScaleFactor
    )

    if math.abs(newWidth - (self._watermarkLastWidth or 0)) > 1 then
        self._watermarkLastWidth = newWidth
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.watermarkFrame, {Size = UDim2.new(0, newWidth, 0, islandHeight)}, 0.18)
    end

    self:_RefreshNotificationHolderPosition()
end

        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        ZIndex = 4,
        Parent = self.searchFrame
    })
    local searchBoxFocused = false
    self._refreshSearchBoxVisuals = function()
        local hasQuery = normalizeSearchText(self._searchQuery) ~= ""
        local accent = self.config.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(2, 133, 255)
        local targetStrokeColor = searchBoxFocused and accent:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.14)
            or (hasQuery and accent:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(32, 32, 32), 0.34) or Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 38))
        local targetFrameColor = searchBoxFocused and Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 30)
            or (hasQuery and Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 28) or Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 22))
        local targetIconColor = searchBoxFocused and accent:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.1)
            or (hasQuery and accent:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(104, 104, 112), 0.25) or Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120))

        if self.searchFrame then
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.searchFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = targetFrameColor}, 0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        end
        if self.searchFrameStroke then
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.searchFrameStroke, {Color = targetStrokeColor}, 0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        end
        if self.searchIconImage then
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.searchIconImage, {ImageColor3 = targetIconColor}, 0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        end
    end
    self:_TrackConnection(self.searchBox.Focused:Connect(function()
        searchBoxFocused = true
        if type(self._refreshSearchBoxVisuals) == "function" then
            self._refreshSearchBoxVisuals()
        end
    end))
    self:_TrackConnection(self.searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        searchBoxFocused = false
        if type(self._refreshSearchBoxVisuals) == "function" then
            self._refreshSearchBoxVisuals()
        end
    end))
    self:_TrackConnection(self.searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        self:ApplySearchFilter(self.searchBox.Text)
    end))
    if type(self._refreshSearchBoxVisuals) == "function" then
        self._refreshSearchBoxVisuals()
    end

    local settingsHoverInfoFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {

    local function showSettingsHoverInfo(message)
        if type(message) ~= "string" or message == "" then
            settingsHoverInfoFrame.Visible = false
            settingsHoverInfoLabel.Text = ""
            return
        end
        settingsHoverInfoLabel.Text = message
        settingsHoverInfoFrame.Visible = true
    end

    local function bindSettingsHoverInfo(trigger, message)
        if not trigger or type(message) ~= "string" or message == "" then
            return
        end
        self:_TrackConnection(trigger.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            showSettingsHoverInfo(message)
        end))
        self:_TrackConnection(trigger.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            showSettingsHoverInfo(nil)
        end))
    end

    makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),

    self.uiAccentPreviewFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {

    local rowY = 30 * mobileScaleFactor
    local rowStep = 24 * mobileScaleFactor
    local function createSettingsToggle(labelText, initial, callback, options)
        options = options or {}
        local rowFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, rowY),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20 * mobileScaleFactor),
            Parent = self.uiSettingsPanelFrame
        })
        local badgeOffset = 0
        if type(options.BadgeText) == "string" and options.BadgeText ~= "" then
            local badgeWidth = math.max(34 * mobileScaleFactor, math.floor((howWideIsThisText(options.BadgeText, 9.5 * mobileScaleFactor, Enum.Font.GothamSemibold) + 10 * mobileScaleFactor) + 0.5))
            local badgeFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
                BackgroundColor3 = options.BadgeColor or Color3.fromRGB(88, 67, 22),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -6 * mobileScaleFactor),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, badgeWidth, 0, 12 * mobileScaleFactor),
                Parent = rowFrame
            })
            makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = badgeFrame})
            makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
                FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold),
                Text = options.BadgeText,
                TextColor3 = options.BadgeTextColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 221, 130),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                TextSize = 9.5 * mobileScaleFactor,
                Parent = badgeFrame
            })
            badgeOffset = badgeWidth + 6 * mobileScaleFactor
            bindSettingsHoverInfo(badgeFrame, options.InfoText)
        end

        local label = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
            FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
            Text = labelText,
            TextColor3 = options.LabelColor or Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, badgeOffset, 0, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -(46 * mobileScaleFactor + badgeOffset + (options.InfoText and (14 * mobileScaleFactor) or 0)), 1, 0),
            TextSize = options.TextSize or (12 * mobileScaleFactor),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            Parent = rowFrame
        })
        bindSettingsHoverInfo(label, options.InfoText)
        bindSettingsHoverInfo(rowFrame, options.InfoText)

        if options.InfoText then
            local infoBadge = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
                FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold),
                Text = "i",
                TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 205, 214),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 42),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -48 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 12 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 12 * mobileScaleFactor),
                TextSize = 9 * mobileScaleFactor,
                Parent = rowFrame
            })
            makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = infoBadge})
            bindSettingsHoverInfo(infoBadge, options.InfoText)
        end
        local switchFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = initial and self.config.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(33, 33, 33),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -34 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.5, -8 * mobileScaleFactor),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 34 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 16 * mobileScaleFactor),
            Parent = rowFrame
        })
        makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = switchFrame})
        self:_RegisterSnowFrostTarget(switchFrame, {
            Compact = true,
            Weight = 0.34,
            CatchInsetX = 4 * mobileScaleFactor,
            MaxSpecks = 4
        })
        local knob = makeThingExist("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = initial and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Color3.fromRGB(95, 95, 95),
            Position = initial and UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -6 * mobileScaleFactor) or UDim2.new(0, 2 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.5, -6 * mobileScaleFactor),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 12 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 12 * mobileScaleFactor),
            Parent = switchFrame
        })
        makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = knob})

        local state = initial == true
        local function setState(nextState, skipCallback)
            state = nextState == true
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(switchFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = state and self.config.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(33, 33, 33)}, 0.16)
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(knob, {
                Position = state and UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -6 * mobileScaleFactor) or UDim2.new(0, 2 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.5, -6 * mobileScaleFactor),
                BackgroundColor3 = state and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Color3.fromRGB(95, 95, 95)
            }, 0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            self:_ShakeSnowOffTarget(switchFrame, 0.14)
            if not skipCallback then
                callback(state)
            end
        end

        local toggleButton = makeThingExist("TextButton", {
            Text = "",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
            Parent = rowFrame
        })
        toggleButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            setState(not state, false)
        end)

        rowY = rowY + rowStep
        return {
            Set = setState,
            Get = function()
                return state
            end
        }
    end

    local function createSettingsSlider(labelText, minValue, maxValue, initialValue, callback, formatter)
        minValue = tonumber(minValue) or 0
        maxValue = tonumber(maxValue) or 1
        if maxValue < minValue then
            minValue, maxValue = maxValue, minValue
        end

        local sliderValue = math.clamp(tonumber(initialValue) or minValue, minValue, maxValue)
        local rowFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, rowY),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 34 * mobileScaleFactor),
            Parent = self.uiSettingsPanelFrame
        })
        makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
            FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
            Text = labelText,
            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(0.62, 0, 0, 14 * mobileScaleFactor),
            TextSize = 12 * mobileScaleFactor,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            Parent = rowFrame
        })

        local valueLabel = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
            FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
            Text = "",
            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(194, 194, 194),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0.62, 0, 0, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0.38, 0, 0, 14 * mobileScaleFactor),
            TextSize = 11.5 * mobileScaleFactor,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            Parent = rowFrame
        })

        local trackFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 39),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 21 * mobileScaleFactor),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6 * mobileScaleFactor),
            Parent = rowFrame
        })
        makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = trackFrame})
        self:_RegisterSnowFrostTarget(trackFrame, {
            Compact = true,
            Weight = 0.38,
            CatchInsetX = 6 * mobileScaleFactor,
            MaxSpecks = 5
        })

        local fillFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = self.config.AccentColor,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
            Parent = trackFrame
        })
        makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = fillFrame})

        local knobFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(244, 244, 248),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 8 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 14 * mobileScaleFactor),
            Parent = trackFrame
        })
        makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 2 * mobileScaleFactor), Parent = knobFrame})

        local sliderButton = makeThingExist("TextButton", {
            Text = "",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
            Parent = rowFrame
        })

        local function getFormattedValue(value)
            if type(formatter) == "function" then
                return tostring(formatter(value))
            end
            return tostring(math.floor(value * 100 + 0.5)) .. "%"
        end

        local function updateSliderVisuals(animate)
            local ratio = maxValue > minValue and math.clamp((sliderValue - minValue) / (maxValue - minValue), 0, 1) or 0
            local absoluteWidth = math.max(1, trackFrame.AbsoluteSize.X)
            local knobMargin = math.min(4 * mobileScaleFactor, absoluteWidth * 0.5)
            local knobOffset = math.clamp(ratio * absoluteWidth, knobMargin, math.max(knobMargin, absoluteWidth - knobMargin))
            local fillSize = UDim2.new(ratio, 0, 1, 0)
            local knobPosition = UDim2.new(0, knobOffset, 0.5, 0)

            valueLabel.Text = getFormattedValue(sliderValue)
            if animate then
                smoothMoveGoWoosh(fillFrame, {Size = fillSize}, 0.12)
                smoothMoveGoWoosh(knobFrame, {Position = knobPosition}, 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            else
                fillFrame.Size = fillSize
                knobFrame.Position = knobPosition
            end
        end

        local function setSliderValue(newValue, skipCallback, instant)
            sliderValue = math.clamp(tonumber(newValue) or minValue, minValue, maxValue)
            updateSliderVisuals(instant ~= true)
            self:_ShakeSnowOffTarget(trackFrame, instant == true and 0.08 or 0.12)
            if not skipCallback then
                callback(sliderValue)
            end
        end

        local isDragging = false
        local activeTouchInput = nil
        local function setFromInput(inputObject, instant)
            if not inputObject then
                return
            end
            local absoluteX = trackFrame.AbsolutePosition.X
            local absoluteWidth = math.max(1, trackFrame.AbsoluteSize.X)
            local ratio = math.clamp((inputObject.Position.X - absoluteX) / absoluteWidth, 0, 1)
            setSliderValue(minValue + ((maxValue - minValue) * ratio), false, instant)
        end

        self:_TrackConnection(sliderButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject)
            local isMouse = inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            local isTouch = inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            if not isMouse and not isTouch then
                return
            end
            isDragging = true
            activeTouchInput = isTouch and inputObject or nil
            setFromInput(inputObject, true)
        end))
        self:_TrackConnection(thisInputServiceGoesHard.InputChanged:Connect(function(inputObject)
            if not isDragging then
                return
            end
            local isMouseMove = inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and activeTouchInput == nil
            local isTouchMove = inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and (activeTouchInput == nil or inputObject == activeTouchInput)
            if isMouseMove or isTouchMove then
                setFromInput(inputObject, true)
            end
        end))
        self:_TrackConnection(thisInputServiceGoesHard.InputEnded:Connect(function(inputObject)
            if inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                isDragging = false
                activeTouchInput = nil
            elseif inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and (activeTouchInput == nil or inputObject == activeTouchInput) then
                isDragging = false
                activeTouchInput = nil
            end
        end))

        updateSliderVisuals(false)
        rowY = rowY + 38 * mobileScaleFactor
        return {
            Set = function(value, skipCallback)
                setSliderValue(value, skipCallback == true, true)
            end,
            Get = function()
                return sliderValue
            end,
            Fill = fillFrame
        }
    end

    local blurToggleRef = createSettingsToggle("Blur Background", self._uiVisualSettings.Blur, function(enabled)
        self._uiVisualSettings.Blur = enabled
        self:_ApplyOpenCloseVisuals(false)
    end)
    local overlayToggleRef = createSettingsToggle("Overlay FX", self._uiVisualSettings.Snow, function(enabled)

        LabelColor = Color3.fromRGB(182, 208, 232)
    })
    self.uiOverlayPhysicsToggleRef = overlayPhysicsToggleRef

        self:_SetBackgroundEffectsEnabled(enabled)
    end)
    local backgroundOpacitySliderRef = createSettingsSlider("Background Opacity", 0, 1, self._backgroundOverlayOpacity, function(value)

    end)
    self.uiBackgroundOpacitySliderRef = backgroundOpacitySliderRef
    self.uiBackgroundOpacitySliderFill = backgroundOpacitySliderRef.Fill
    local gradientToggleRef = createSettingsToggle("Text Gradient", self._uiVisualSettings.TextGradient, function(enabled)
        self:_SetTextGradientEnabled(enabled)
    end)
    local espPreviewToggleRef = createSettingsToggle("ESP Preview", self._uiVisualSettings.ESPSelfPreview, function(enabled)
        self:SetESPPreviewEnabled(enabled)
    end)
    local hideNameToggleRef = createSettingsToggle("Hide Name", self._uiVisualSettings.HideName, function(enabled)
        self:_SetNameHidden(enabled)
    end)
    local autoSaveToggleRef = nil
    if self.config.ShowAutoSaveToggle ~= false then
        autoSaveToggleRef = createSettingsToggle("Auto Save Config", self._autoConfigEnabled, function(enabled)
            self._autoConfigEnabled = enabled == true
            if self._autoConfigEnabled then
                self._autoConfigLoadAttempted = false
                self._autoConfigAccumulator = self._autoConfigInterval or 1.2
                self:_TryAutoSaveConfig(true)
            else
                self._autoConfigAccumulator = 0
            end
        end)
    end

    self.uiSettingsToggleRefs = {blurToggleRef, overlayToggleRef, overlayPhysicsToggleRef, bgFxToggleRef, gradientToggleRef, espPreviewToggleRef, hideNameToggleRef}
    if autoSaveToggleRef then
        table.insert(self.uiSettingsToggleRefs, autoSaveToggleRef)
    end

    local overlayRow = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, rowY + 2 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Parent = self.uiSettingsPanelFrame
    })
    makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
        Text = "Overlay",
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 52 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, 0),
        TextSize = 12 * mobileScaleFactor,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = overlayRow
    })

    local overlayPickerFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(29, 29, 29),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -84 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 84 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, 0),
        Parent = overlayRow
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = overlayPickerFrame})
    makeThingExist("UIStroke", {Color = Color3.fromRGB(44, 44, 44), Parent = overlayPickerFrame})

    self.uiOverlayModeValueLabel = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
        Text = self._overlayMode,
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 210),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 8 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -24 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, 0),
        TextSize = 11.5 * mobileScaleFactor,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = overlayPickerFrame
    })

    local overlayArrowImage = makeThingExist("ImageLabel", {
        Image = "rbxassetid://111626678408582",
        ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(132, 132, 132),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -18 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.5, -6 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 12 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 12 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Parent = overlayPickerFrame
    })

    local overlayDropdownWidth = 102 * mobileScaleFactor
    local overlayDropdownRowHeight = 21 * mobileScaleFactor
    local overlayDropdownFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 16),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, overlayDropdownWidth, 0, 0),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 9999,
        Parent = self.dropdownHolderRef
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7), Parent = overlayDropdownFrame})
    makeThingExist("UIStroke", {Color = Color3.fromRGB(42, 42, 42), Parent = overlayDropdownFrame})

    local overlayDropdownContainer = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 4 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 4 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -8 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 0),
        ZIndex = 10000,
        Parent = overlayDropdownFrame
    })

    local overlayDropdownOpen = false
    local overlayDropdownPositionConn = nil
    local overlayDropdownOutsideConn = nil

    local function updateOverlayDropdownPosition()
        local pickerAbsPos = overlayPickerFrame.AbsolutePosition
        local pickerAbsSize = overlayPickerFrame.AbsoluteSize
        overlayDropdownFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, pickerAbsPos.X + pickerAbsSize.X - overlayDropdownWidth, 0, pickerAbsPos.Y + pickerAbsSize.Y + 5)
    end

    local function closeOverlayDropdown(isInstant)
        overlayDropdownOpen = false
        if overlayDropdownPositionConn then
            overlayDropdownPositionConn()
            overlayDropdownPositionConn = nil
        end
        if overlayDropdownOutsideConn then
            overlayDropdownOutsideConn:Disconnect()
            overlayDropdownOutsideConn = nil
        end
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(overlayArrowImage, {Rotation = 0}, 0.16)
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(overlayPickerFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(29, 29, 29)}, 0.16)
        if isInstant then
            overlayDropdownFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, overlayDropdownWidth, 0, 0)
            overlayDropdownFrame.Visible = false
            return
        end
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(overlayDropdownFrame, {Size = UDim2.new(0, overlayDropdownWidth, 0, 0)}, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        task.delay(0.18, function()
            if overlayDropdownFrame and overlayDropdownFrame.Parent and not overlayDropdownOpen then
                overlayDropdownFrame.Visible = false
            end
        end)
    end

    local rebuildOverlayDropdownOptions
    rebuildOverlayDropdownOptions = function()
        for _, child in ipairs(overlayDropdownContainer:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Frame") or child:IsA("TextButton") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
        local optionY = 0
        for _, modeName in ipairs(self._overlayModes) do
            local isSelected = modeName == self._overlayMode
            local optionFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
                BackgroundColor3 = isSelected and Color3.fromRGB(33, 33, 33) or Color3.fromRGB(23, 23, 23),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, optionY),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, overlayDropdownRowHeight - 2 * mobileScaleFactor),
                ZIndex = 10001,
                Parent = overlayDropdownContainer
            })
            makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5), Parent = optionFrame})
            local optionLabel = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
                FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
                Text = modeName,
                TextColor3 = isSelected and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Color3.fromRGB(138, 138, 138),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -8 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, 0),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 8 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 0),
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                TextSize = 11.5 * mobileScaleFactor,
                ZIndex = 10002,
                Parent = optionFrame
            })
            local optionButton = makeThingExist("TextButton", {
                Text = "",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                ZIndex = 10003,
                Parent = optionFrame
            })
            optionButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                self:_SetOverlayMode(modeName)
                self.uiOverlayModeValueLabel.Text = self._overlayMode
                rebuildOverlayDropdownOptions()
                closeOverlayDropdown(false)
            end)
            optionButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                if self._overlayMode ~= modeName then
                    smoothMoveGoWoosh(optionFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)}, 0.12)
                    smoothMoveGoWoosh(optionLabel, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(188, 188, 188)}, 0.12)
                end
            end)
            optionButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                if self._overlayMode ~= modeName then
                    smoothMoveGoWoosh(optionFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(23, 23, 23)}, 0.12)
                    smoothMoveGoWoosh(optionLabel, {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(138, 138, 138)}, 0.12)
                end
            end)
            optionY = optionY + overlayDropdownRowHeight
        end
        overlayDropdownContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, optionY)
    end

    local function openOverlayDropdown()
        overlayDropdownOpen = true
        rebuildOverlayDropdownOptions()
        updateOverlayDropdownPosition()
        overlayDropdownFrame.Visible = true
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(overlayDropdownFrame, {Size = UDim2.new(0, overlayDropdownWidth, 0, #self._overlayModes * overlayDropdownRowHeight + 8 * mobileScaleFactor)}, 0.2)
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(overlayArrowImage, {Rotation = 180}, 0.16)
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(overlayPickerFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)}, 0.16)
        if overlayDropdownPositionConn then
            overlayDropdownPositionConn()
            overlayDropdownPositionConn = nil
        end
        overlayDropdownPositionConn = startFloatingPositionTracker(self, overlayPickerFrame, function()
            if overlayDropdownOpen then
                updateOverlayDropdownPosition()
            end
        end)
        if overlayDropdownOutsideConn then
            overlayDropdownOutsideConn:Disconnect()
            overlayDropdownOutsideConn = nil
        end
        overlayDropdownOutsideConn = self:_TrackConnection(thisInputServiceGoesHard.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if not overlayDropdownOpen then
                return
            end
            local isClick = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            if not isClick then
                return
            end
            local clickPos = input.Position
            local menuPos = overlayDropdownFrame.AbsolutePosition
            local menuSize = overlayDropdownFrame.AbsoluteSize
            local pickerPos = overlayPickerFrame.AbsolutePosition
            local pickerSize = overlayPickerFrame.AbsoluteSize
            local insideMenu = clickPos.X >= menuPos.X and clickPos.X <= menuPos.X + menuSize.X and clickPos.Y >= menuPos.Y and clickPos.Y <= menuPos.Y + menuSize.Y
            local insidePicker = clickPos.X >= pickerPos.X and clickPos.X <= pickerPos.X + pickerSize.X and clickPos.Y >= pickerPos.Y and clickPos.Y <= pickerPos.Y + pickerSize.Y
            if not insideMenu and not insidePicker then
                closeOverlayDropdown(false)
            end
        end))
    end

    local overlayPickerButton = makeThingExist("TextButton", {
        Text = "",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        Parent = overlayPickerFrame
    })
    overlayPickerButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if overlayDropdownOpen then
            closeOverlayDropdown(false)
        else
            openOverlayDropdown()
        end
    end)
    overlayPickerButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if not overlayDropdownOpen then
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(overlayPickerFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)}, 0.12)
        end
    end)
    overlayPickerButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not overlayDropdownOpen then
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(overlayPickerFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(29, 29, 29)}, 0.12)
        end
    end)

    rowY = rowY + rowStep

    local fontRowHeight = 22 * mobileScaleFactor
    local fontRow = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, rowY + 2 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, fontRowHeight),
        Parent = self.uiSettingsPanelFrame
    })
    makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
        Text = "Font",
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 34 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, 0),
        TextSize = 12 * mobileScaleFactor,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = fontRow
    })

    local function getSettingsFontDescriptor(preset)
        if type(preset) ~= "table" then
            return Font.fromEnum(defaultNexusUIFontEnum)
        end
        if preset.Family then
            return Font.new(
                preset.Family,
                preset.Weight or Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold,
                preset.Style or Enum.FontStyle.Normal
            )
        end
        return Font.fromEnum(preset.EnumFont or defaultNexusUIFontEnum)
    end

    local function markFontPreviewLabel(label)
        if label then
            pcall(function()
                label:SetAttribute("NexusUIFontLock", true)
            end)
        end
    end

    local function applyFontPreviewLabel(label, preset)
        if not label then
            return
        end
        markFontPreviewLabel(label)
        applyNexusUIFont(label, getSettingsFontDescriptor(preset), preset and preset.EnumFont or defaultNexusUIFontEnum)
    end

    local fontPickerFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(29, 29, 31),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 38 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -38 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, 0),
        Parent = fontRow
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = fontPickerFrame})
    local fontPickerStroke = makeThingExist("UIStroke", {
        Color = Color3.fromRGB(44, 44, 48),
        Parent = fontPickerFrame
    })
    self.uiSettingsFontValueLabel = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
        Text = self._fontPresets[self._fontPresetIndex].Name,
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(214, 214, 220),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 10 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -42 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, 0),
        TextSize = 11.6 * mobileScaleFactor,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = fontPickerFrame
    })
    local fontArrowImage = makeThingExist("ImageLabel", {
        Image = "rbxassetid://111626678408582",
        ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(138, 138, 142),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -20 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.5, -6 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 12 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 12 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Parent = fontPickerFrame
    })
    local fontPickerButton = makeThingExist("TextButton", {
        Text = "",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        Parent = fontPickerFrame
    })
    bindSettingsHoverInfo(fontPickerFrame, "Search and switch between all bundled font presets.")

    local fontDropdownWidth = math.max(188 * mobileScaleFactor, 170)
    local fontDropdownSearchHeight = 26 * mobileScaleFactor
    local fontDropdownRowHeight = 30 * mobileScaleFactor
    local fontDropdownVisibleRows = isMobileUserSadly and 5 or 7
    local fontDropdownFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 16),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(0, fontDropdownWidth, 0, 0),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 9999,
        Parent = self.dropdownHolderRef
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8), Parent = fontDropdownFrame})
    makeThingExist("UIStroke", {Color = Color3.fromRGB(44, 44, 48), Parent = fontDropdownFrame})

    local fontSearchFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 22),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 8 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 8 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -16 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, fontDropdownSearchHeight),
        ZIndex = 10000,
        Parent = fontDropdownFrame
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = fontSearchFrame})
    makeThingExist("UIStroke", {Color = Color3.fromRGB(36, 36, 40), Parent = fontSearchFrame})
    local fontSearchBox = makeThingExist("TextBox", {
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
        Text = "",
        PlaceholderText = "Search fonts...",
        PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(96, 96, 102),
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(228, 228, 232),
        TextSize = 11.4 * mobileScaleFactor,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        ClearTextOnFocus = false,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 10 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -20 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, 0),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        ZIndex = 10001,
        Parent = fontSearchFrame
    })

    local fontOptionScroll = makeThingExist("ScrollingFrame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 8 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 8 * mobileScaleFactor + fontDropdownSearchHeight + 6 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -16 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, -(fontDropdownSearchHeight + 22 * mobileScaleFactor)),
        ScrollBarThickness = 0,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        ZIndex = 10000,
        Parent = fontDropdownFrame
    })
    attachNexusUIScrollbar(self, fontOptionScroll, fontDropdownFrame, {
        TrackWidth = 6 * mobileScaleFactor,
        ThumbWidth = 3 * mobileScaleFactor,
        EdgeInset = 2 * mobileScaleFactor,
        VerticalInset = 10 * mobileScaleFactor,
        IdleThumbHeight = 42 * mobileScaleFactor,
        ZIndex = 10003
    })

    local fontDropdownOpen = false
    local fontDropdownTargetHeight = fontDropdownSearchHeight + 16 * mobileScaleFactor + fontDropdownRowHeight
    local fontDropdownPositionConn = nil
    local fontDropdownOutsideConn = nil

    local function updateFontPickerVisual(isOpen)
        local openState = isOpen == true
        springMoveGoBoing(fontPickerFrame, {
            BackgroundColor3 = openState and Color3.fromRGB(36, 36, 40) or Color3.fromRGB(29, 29, 31)
        }, 0.86, 5.8)
        springMoveGoBoing(fontPickerStroke, {
            Color = openState and self.config.AccentColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 28), 0.28) or Color3.fromRGB(44, 44, 48)
        }, 0.9, 6.2)
        springMoveGoBoing(fontArrowImage, {
            Rotation = openState and 180 or 0,
            ImageColor3 = openState and Color3.fromRGB(224, 224, 228) or Color3.fromRGB(138, 138, 142)
        }, 0.9, 6.2)
    end

    local function updateFontDropdownPosition()
        local pickerAbsolutePosition = fontPickerFrame.AbsolutePosition
        local pickerAbsoluteSize = fontPickerFrame.AbsoluteSize
        fontDropdownFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
            0,
            pickerAbsolutePosition.X + pickerAbsoluteSize.X - fontDropdownWidth,
            0,
            pickerAbsolutePosition.Y + pickerAbsoluteSize.Y + 6 * mobileScaleFactor
        )
    end

    local rebuildFontDropdownOptions
    local closeFontDropdown
    rebuildFontDropdownOptions = function()
        for _, child in ipairs(fontOptionScroll:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end

        local query = normalizeSearchText(fontSearchBox.Text)
        local optionY = 0
        local visibleCount = 0

        for presetIndex, preset in ipairs(self._fontPresets) do
            local presetName = tostring(preset.Name or ("Font " .. tostring(presetIndex)))
            if query == "" or string.find(normalizeSearchText(presetName), query, 1, true) ~= nil then
                visibleCount = visibleCount + 1

                local isSelected = presetIndex == self._fontPresetIndex
                local baseBackground = isSelected
                    and self.config.AccentColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 18), 0.4)
                    or Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 26)
                local baseTextColor = isSelected and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or Color3.fromRGB(196, 196, 202)
                local sampleTextColor = isSelected and Color3.fromRGB(244, 244, 248) or Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 148)

                local optionFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
                    BackgroundColor3 = baseBackground,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, optionY),
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, -2 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, fontDropdownRowHeight - 3 * mobileScaleFactor),
                    ZIndex = 10001,
                    Parent = fontOptionScroll
                })
                makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = optionFrame})

                local optionNameLabel = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
                    FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
                    Text = presetName,
                    TextColor3 = baseTextColor,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Position = UDim2.new(0, 10 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 3 * mobileScaleFactor),
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, -58 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 12 * mobileScaleFactor),
                    TextSize = 11 * mobileScaleFactor,
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                    TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                    ZIndex = 10002,
                    Parent = optionFrame
                })
                local optionSampleLabel = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
                    FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Medium),
                    Text = "Aa Bb 123",
                    TextColor3 = sampleTextColor,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Position = UDim2.new(0, 10 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 14 * mobileScaleFactor),
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, -58 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 10 * mobileScaleFactor),
                    TextSize = 9.8 * mobileScaleFactor,
                    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                    TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                    ZIndex = 10002,
                    Parent = optionFrame
                })
                applyFontPreviewLabel(optionNameLabel, preset)
                applyFontPreviewLabel(optionSampleLabel, preset)

                local selectedBadge = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
                    FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold),
                    Text = isSelected and "ON" or "",
                    TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(248, 248, 250),
                    BackgroundColor3 = isSelected
                        and self.config.AccentColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18), 0.15)
                        or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                    BackgroundTransparency = isSelected and 0 or 1,
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
                    Position = UDim2.new(1, -8 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.5, 0),
                    Size = UDim2.new(0, 28 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 14 * mobileScaleFactor),
                    TextSize = 8.6 * mobileScaleFactor,
                    ZIndex = 10003,
                    Parent = optionFrame
                })
                makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = selectedBadge})
                markFontPreviewLabel(selectedBadge)

                local optionButton = makeThingExist("TextButton", {
                    Text = "",
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                    ZIndex = 10004,
                    Parent = optionFrame
                })

                optionButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    self:ApplyFontPreset(presetIndex)
                    closeFontDropdown(false)
                end)

                optionButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                    if presetIndex ~= self._fontPresetIndex then
                        springMoveGoBoing(optionFrame, {
                            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(32, 32, 35)
                        }, 0.82, 6.4)
                        springMoveGoBoing(optionNameLabel, {
                            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(228, 228, 232)
                        }, 0.85, 6.4)
                        springMoveGoBoing(optionSampleLabel, {
                            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 196)
                        }, 0.85, 6.4)
                    end
                end)

                optionButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                    if presetIndex ~= self._fontPresetIndex then
                        springMoveGoBoing(optionFrame, {
                            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 26)
                        }, 0.82, 6.4)
                        springMoveGoBoing(optionNameLabel, {
                            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(196, 196, 202)
                        }, 0.85, 6.4)
                        springMoveGoBoing(optionSampleLabel, {
                            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 148)
                        }, 0.85, 6.4)
                    end
                end)

                optionY = optionY + fontDropdownRowHeight
            end
        end

        if visibleCount == 0 then
            local emptyLabel = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
                FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
                Text = "No fonts found",
                TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 148),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 8 * mobileScaleFactor),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18 * mobileScaleFactor),
                TextSize = 11 * mobileScaleFactor,
                ZIndex = 10002,
                Parent = fontOptionScroll
            })
            optionY = (18 * mobileScaleFactor) + 16 * mobileScaleFactor
        end

        fontOptionScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, optionY)
        local resolvedVisibleRows = math.clamp(visibleCount, 1, fontDropdownVisibleRows)
        local optionHeight = resolvedVisibleRows * fontDropdownRowHeight
        fontDropdownTargetHeight = fontDropdownSearchHeight + optionHeight + 24 * mobileScaleFactor

        if fontDropdownOpen then
            springMoveGoBoing(fontDropdownFrame, {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, fontDropdownWidth, 0, fontDropdownTargetHeight)
            }, 0.84, 6)
        end
    end

    self._refreshSettingsFontSelector = function()
        local activePreset = self._fontPresets[self._fontPresetIndex]
        if self.uiSettingsFontValueLabel then
            self.uiSettingsFontValueLabel.Text = activePreset and activePreset.Name or "Unknown"
        end
        updateFontPickerVisual(fontDropdownOpen)
        if fontDropdownOpen then
            rebuildFontDropdownOptions()
        end
    end

    closeFontDropdown = function(isInstant)
        fontDropdownOpen = false
        updateFontPickerVisual(false)
        if fontDropdownPositionConn then
            fontDropdownPositionConn()
            fontDropdownPositionConn = nil
        end
        if fontDropdownOutsideConn then
            fontDropdownOutsideConn:Disconnect()
            fontDropdownOutsideConn = nil
        end
        if isInstant then
            fontDropdownFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, fontDropdownWidth, 0, 0)
            fontDropdownFrame.Visible = false
            return
        end
        springMoveGoBoing(fontDropdownFrame, {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, fontDropdownWidth, 0, 0)
        }, 0.84, 6)
        task.spawn(function()
            spr.springend(fontDropdownFrame, "Size")
            if fontDropdownFrame and fontDropdownFrame.Parent and not fontDropdownOpen then
                fontDropdownFrame.Visible = false
            end
        end)
    end
    self._closeSettingsFontSelector = closeFontDropdown

    local function openFontDropdown()
        fontDropdownOpen = true
        fontSearchBox.Text = ""
        rebuildFontDropdownOptions()
        updateFontDropdownPosition()
        fontDropdownFrame.Visible = true
        updateFontPickerVisual(true)
        springMoveGoBoing(fontDropdownFrame, {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, fontDropdownWidth, 0, fontDropdownTargetHeight)
        }, 0.84, 6)

        if fontDropdownPositionConn then
            fontDropdownPositionConn()
            fontDropdownPositionConn = nil
        end
        fontDropdownPositionConn = startFloatingPositionTracker(self, fontPickerFrame, function()
            if fontDropdownOpen then
                updateFontDropdownPosition()
            end
        end)

        if fontDropdownOutsideConn then
            fontDropdownOutsideConn:Disconnect()
            fontDropdownOutsideConn = nil
        end
        fontDropdownOutsideConn = self:_TrackConnection(thisInputServiceGoesHard.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if not fontDropdownOpen then
                return
            end
            local isClick = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            if not isClick then
                return
            end
            local clickPosition = input.Position
            local menuPosition = fontDropdownFrame.AbsolutePosition
            local menuSize = fontDropdownFrame.AbsoluteSize
            local pickerPosition = fontPickerFrame.AbsolutePosition
            local pickerSize = fontPickerFrame.AbsoluteSize
            local insideMenu = clickPosition.X >= menuPosition.X and clickPosition.X <= menuPosition.X + menuSize.X
                and clickPosition.Y >= menuPosition.Y and clickPosition.Y <= menuPosition.Y + menuSize.Y
            local insidePicker = clickPosition.X >= pickerPosition.X and clickPosition.X <= pickerPosition.X + pickerSize.X
                and clickPosition.Y >= pickerPosition.Y and clickPosition.Y <= pickerPosition.Y + pickerSize.Y
            if not insideMenu and not insidePicker then
                closeFontDropdown(false)
            end
        end))
    end

    fontSearchBox.Focused:Connect(function()
        springMoveGoBoing(fontSearchFrame, {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 30)
        }, 0.88, 6.2)
    end)
    fontSearchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        springMoveGoBoing(fontSearchFrame, {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 22)
        }, 0.88, 6.2)
    end)
    self:_TrackConnection(fontSearchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        if fontDropdownOpen then
            rebuildFontDropdownOptions()
        end
    end))

    fontPickerButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if fontDropdownOpen then
            closeFontDropdown(false)
        else
            openFontDropdown()
        end
    end)
    fontPickerButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if not fontDropdownOpen then
            springMoveGoBoing(fontPickerFrame, {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(34, 34, 37)
            }, 0.86, 5.8)
            springMoveGoBoing(fontPickerStroke, {
                Color = Color3.fromRGB(56, 56, 60)
            }, 0.9, 6.2)
        end
    end)
    fontPickerButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not fontDropdownOpen then
            updateFontPickerVisual(false)
        end
    end)

    local accentRowY = rowY + fontRowHeight + 12 * mobileScaleFactor
    makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
        Text = "Accent",
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, accentRowY),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 46 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 16 * mobileScaleFactor),
        TextSize = 12 * mobileScaleFactor,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        Parent = self.uiSettingsPanelFrame
    })
    local accentColors = {
        Color3.fromRGB(2, 133, 255),
        Color3.fromRGB(48, 196, 122),
        Color3.fromRGB(237, 88, 88),
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 169, 64),
        Color3.fromRGB(185, 108, 255)
    }
    for index, color in ipairs(accentColors) do
        local swatch = makeThingExist("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = color,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, (58 + (index - 1) * 22) * mobileScaleFactor, 0, accentRowY - 1 * mobileScaleFactor),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 16 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 16 * mobileScaleFactor),
            Parent = self.uiSettingsPanelFrame
        })
        makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = swatch})
        local swatchClick = makeThingExist("TextButton", {
            Text = "",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
            Parent = swatch
        })
        swatchClick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            self:SetAccentColor(color)
            blurToggleRef:Set(self._uiVisualSettings.Blur, true)
            overlayToggleRef:Set(self._uiVisualSettings.Snow, true)
            bgFxToggleRef:Set(self._uiVisualSettings.BackgroundEffects, true)
            gradientToggleRef:Set(self._uiVisualSettings.TextGradient, true)
        end)
    end

    local settingsPanelExpandedHeight = math.max(324 * mobileScaleFactor, accentRowY + 72 * mobileScaleFactor)

    task.defer(function()
        if not (self.uiSettingsPanelFrame and self.uiSettingsPanelFrame.Parent) then
            return
        end
        local baseZ = self.uiSettingsPanelFrame.ZIndex
        for _, d in ipairs(self.uiSettingsPanelFrame:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("GuiObject") then
                d.ZIndex = math.max(d.ZIndex, baseZ + 1)
            end
        end
    end)

    local function setSettingsPanelOpen(openState)
        self.uiSettingsOpen = openState == true
        if self.uiSettingsOpen then
            self.uiSettingsPanelFrame.Visible = true
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.uiSettingsPanelFrame, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 185 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, settingsPanelExpandedHeight)}, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.uiSettingsButtonFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)}, 0.2)
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.uiSettingsButtonStroke, {Color = self.config.AccentColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20), 0.45)}, 0.2)
        else
            closeFontDropdown(true)
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.uiSettingsPanelFrame, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 185 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 0)}, 0.16)
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.uiSettingsButtonFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)}, 0.2)
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.uiSettingsButtonStroke, {Color = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)}, 0.2)
            task.delay(0.16, function()
                if not self.uiSettingsOpen then
                    self.uiSettingsPanelFrame.Visible = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    settingsToggleButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if self.uiSettingsGearIcon then
            local nextRotation = (tonumber(self.uiSettingsGearIcon.Rotation) or 0) + 180
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.uiSettingsGearIcon, {Rotation = nextRotation}, 0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            task.delay(0.24, function()
                if self.uiSettingsGearIcon and self.uiSettingsGearIcon.Parent then
                    self.uiSettingsGearIcon.Rotation = nextRotation % 360
                end
            end)
        end
        setSettingsPanelOpen(not self.uiSettingsOpen)
    end)
    settingsToggleButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.uiSettingsButtonFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28)}, 0.15)
    end)
    settingsToggleButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not self.uiSettingsOpen then
            smoothMoveGoWoosh(self.uiSettingsButtonFrame, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)}, 0.15)
        end
    end)
    if not allowUserCustomization then
        self:SetUserCustomizationEnabled(false)
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:CreateNotificationHolderForCoolNotifs()
    if self._notificationAnchorTracker then
        pcall(self._notificationAnchorTracker)
        self._notificationAnchorTracker = nil
    end

    self.notificationHolderFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, math.floor(68 * mobileScaleFactor + 0.5)),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 392 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 360 * mobileScaleFactor),
        ZIndex = 40,
        Parent = self.screenGuiYay
    })
    makeThingExist("UIListLayout", {
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 10 * mobileScaleFactor),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
        VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
        Parent = self.notificationHolderFrame
    })
    self:_RefreshNotificationHolderPosition()
    if self.watermarkFrame and self.watermarkFrame.Parent then
        self._notificationAnchorTracker = startFloatingPositionTracker(self, self.watermarkFrame, function()
            self:_RefreshNotificationHolderPosition()
        end)
        if self._notificationAnchorTracker then
            self:_TrackConnection(self._notificationAnchorTracker)
        end
    end
end

function superMegaAwesomeLibrary:Notify(config)
    config = config or {}
    config.Title = tostring(config.Title or "Notification")
    config.Description = tostring(config.Description or "")
    config.Duration = tonumber(config.Duration) or 3
    config.Duration = math.max(0.8, config.Duration)
    local defaultNotificationIcon = self._notificationLogoState and self._notificationLogoState.ResolvedImage or self._dynamicIslandLogoState and self._dynamicIslandLogoState.ResolvedImage or ""
    if config.Icon == nil or config.Icon == "" or config.Icon == "rbxassetid://10747361219" then
        config.Icon = defaultNotificationIcon
    end

    if self._destroyed or self._isApplyingConfig then
        return nil
    end

    if not (self.notificationHolderFrame and self.notificationHolderFrame.Parent) then
        self:CreateNotificationHolderForCoolNotifs()
    end
    self:_RefreshNotificationHolderPosition()

    local notificationKey = config.Title .. "\31" .. config.Description
    local nowClock = os.clock()
    local lastNotificationAt = self._notificationTimestamps[notificationKey]
    if lastNotificationAt and (nowClock - lastNotificationAt) < 0.85 then
        return nil
    end
    self._notificationTimestamps[notificationKey] = nowClock
    
    local hasDescription = normalizeSearchText(config.Description) ~= ""
    local titleBounds = textServiceBeLikeText:GetTextSize(
        config.Title,
        15 * mobileScaleFactor,
        Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
        Vector2.new(232 * mobileScaleFactor, math.huge)
    )
    local descBounds = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    if hasDescription then
        descBounds = textServiceBeLikeText:GetTextSize(
            config.Description,
            12.5 * mobileScaleFactor,
            Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            Vector2.new(238 * mobileScaleFactor, math.huge)
        )
    end

    local notifWidth = math.max(
        246 * mobileScaleFactor,
        math.min(388 * mobileScaleFactor, math.max(titleBounds.X, descBounds.X) + 112 * mobileScaleFactor)
    )
    local notifHeight = hasDescription and (78 * mobileScaleFactor) or (64 * mobileScaleFactor)
    local cornerRadius = math.max(20 * mobileScaleFactor, math.floor(notifHeight * 0.5 + 0.5))

    local notificationMount = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(0, notifWidth, 0, notifHeight),
        ClipsDescendants = false,
        ZIndex = 41,
        Parent = self.notificationHolderFrame
    })

    local notificationFrame = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 9, 12),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -14 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.12,
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        Parent = notificationMount
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, cornerRadius), Parent = notificationFrame})
    local notificationStroke = makeThingExist("UIStroke", {
        Color = self.config.AccentColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(26, 28, 34), 0.74),
        Thickness = 1,
        Parent = notificationFrame
    })
    makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
        Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, self.config.AccentColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(18, 20, 26), 0.78)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.28, Color3.fromRGB(12, 13, 18)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(8, 9, 12))
        }),
        Rotation = 0,
        Parent = notificationFrame
    })
    local uiScaleRef = makeThingExist("UIScale", {Scale = 0.92, Parent = notificationFrame})

    local sheenOverlay = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.965,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 4 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 3 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -8 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, math.max(12 * mobileScaleFactor, notifHeight * 0.42)),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex = 1,
        Parent = notificationFrame
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, cornerRadius), Parent = sheenOverlay})
    makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 90,
        Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.04),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, 0.72),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        }),
        Parent = sheenOverlay
    })

    local glowEffect = makeThingExist("ImageLabel", {
        Name = "NotifGlow",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Image = "rbxassetid://5028857084",
        ImageColor3 = self.config.AccentColor,
        ImageTransparency = 0.9,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 32 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.5, 0),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, notifHeight * 1.8, 0, notifHeight * 1.8),
        ZIndex = 0,
        Parent = notificationFrame
    })

    local accentBlend = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = self.config.AccentColor,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.76,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 8 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.5, 0),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 52 * mobileScaleFactor, 1, -14 * mobileScaleFactor),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex = 1,
        Parent = notificationFrame
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = accentBlend})
    makeThingExist("UIGradient", {
        Rotation = 0,
        Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.12),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 0.55),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        }),
        Parent = accentBlend
    })

    local iconHolder = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = self.config.AccentColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(18, 20, 24), 0.72),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 14 * mobileScaleFactor, 0.5, 0),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 34 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 34 * mobileScaleFactor),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex = 2,
        Parent = notificationFrame
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = iconHolder})
    makeThingExist("UIStroke", {
        Color = self.config.AccentColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18), 0.34),
        Thickness = 1,
        Parent = iconHolder
    })

    local notifImageLabel = makeThingExist("ImageLabel", {
        Image = config.Icon,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 18 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 18 * mobileScaleFactor),
        ImageTransparency = 1,
        ZIndex = 3,
        Parent = iconHolder
    })

    local textStartX = 60 * mobileScaleFactor
    local notifTitle = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
        FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(248, 248, 252),
        Text = config.Title,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = hasDescription and UDim2.new(0, textStartX, 0, 10 * mobileScaleFactor) or UDim2.new(0, textStartX, 0.5, -10 * mobileScaleFactor),
        TextSize = 14.5 * mobileScaleFactor,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -textStartX - 18 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 18 * mobileScaleFactor),
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        TextTransparency = 1,
        ZIndex = 2,
        Parent = notificationFrame
    })

    local notifDescription = nil
    if hasDescription then
        notifDescription = makeThingExist("TextLabel", {
            FontFace = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/GothamSSm.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(176, 178, 184),
            Text = config.Description,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, textStartX, 0, 31 * mobileScaleFactor),
            TextSize = 12.5 * mobileScaleFactor,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -textStartX - 18 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 15 * mobileScaleFactor),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            TextTransparency = 1,
            ZIndex = 2,
            Parent = notificationFrame
        })
    end

    local progressTrack = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 31, 36),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.28,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, textStartX, 1, -11 * mobileScaleFactor),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -textStartX - 18 * mobileScaleFactor, 0, 4 * mobileScaleFactor),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex = 2,
        Parent = notificationFrame
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = progressTrack})

    local progressFill = makeThingExist("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = self.config.AccentColor,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex = 3,
        Parent = progressTrack
    })
    makeThingExist("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0), Parent = progressFill})

    local progressTween = oMgThisTweenServiceIsSoCool:Create(
        progressFill,
        TweenInfo.new(config.Duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}
    )
